## 1890155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気づいていたの…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>行き着くところまでよ。<line-break>たとえ、そこが地獄でも<line-break>どこまでもついていくわ。<end>"
EN: "So you knew... To the very end. Even if it leads to hell, I'll follow you all the way.<end>"
KO: "알고 있었어…. 갈 데까지 가는 거야. 그곳이 지옥이라도 끝까지 따라갈게.<end>"

## 1890156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア。<line-break>お前はここに残れ。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia. Stay here.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아. 너는 여기 남아라.<end>"

## 1890157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤよ！<line-break>ついていく！<line-break>　<line-break>足手まといにはならないわ！<line-break>私だって魔王を倒した勇者のひとり、<line-break>閃光のケリュネイアよ！<end>"
EN: "No! I'm coming with you! I won't be a burden! I'm one of the Heroes who defeated the Demon King―Ceryneia the Flash!<end>"
KO: "싫어! 따라갈 거야! 발목 잡진 않을게! 나도 마왕을 쓰러뜨린 용사 중 하나, 섬광의 케류네이아야!<end>"

## 1890158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからこそだ。<line-break>お前にはここで、次々にわいてくる<line-break>ザコどもをくいとめてもらう。<line-break>そして、<value:$28>の<line-break>仲間たちを呼び出してもらう。<line-break>これができるのはお前だけだ。<end>"
EN: "That is precisely why. I need you to stay here and hold back the small fry that keep pouring in. You must also summon <value:$28>'s companions. You alone can do this.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 네가 필요한 거다. 여기서 계속 몰려드는 졸개들을 막아 줘. 그리고 <value:$28>의 동료들을 불러내라. 이걸 할 수 있는 건 너뿐이야.<end>"

## 1890159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難しい仕事だ。<line-break>できるか、ケリュネイア？<end>"
EN: "It will not be easy. Can you do it, Ceryneia?<end>"
KO: "어려운 일이다. 할 수 있겠나, 케류네이아?<end>"

## 1890160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったわ、兄さん。<line-break>勇者ネメアの妹として恥ずかしくない<line-break>働きをしてみせる。<end>"
EN: "All right, brother. I'll do the Hero Nemea proud as his sister.<end>"
KO: "알았어, 오빠. 용사 네메아의 여동생으로 부끄럽지 않게 활약해 보일게.<end>"

## 1890161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、私はここにいるわ。<line-break>仲間を呼び出したかったら<line-break>私に言って。<line-break>あとは頼んだわ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'll remain here. If you want to summon your companions, just speak to me. The rest is up to you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 나는 여기 있을게. 동료를 부르고 싶으면 나한테 말해. 나머지는 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグを呼び出して、<line-break>世界を虚無に帰すんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm going to summon Ulugh, the God of Destruction, and return the world to the Void.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그를 불러내서 세계를 허무로 되돌리는 거야.<end>"

## 1890163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、これはＶ７の１ですね。<line-break>予備メッセージの１０ですよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, this is Ｖ７-１. It's reserve message ten.<end>"
KO: "아아, 이건 V7의 1번이군요. 예비 메시지 10번입니다.<end>"

## 1890164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いよいよ。君ともお別れだね、<line-break>エルファス…。<end>"
EN: "So this is it. Time for us to say goodbye, Elfas...<end>"
KO: "드디어 너와도 작별이구나, 엘파스….<end>"

## 1890165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ…。<line-break>僕はようやく気づいたよ。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>と旅をして、<line-break>とても大事な…。<line-break>たったひとつのことに。<end>"
EN: "Shalli... I've finally realized something. Traveling with <value:$28> taught me one thing... one very important thing.<end>"
KO: "샤리…. 나 이제야 깨달았어. <value:$28>과 여행하면서 아주 소중한… 단 하나의 사실을.<end>"

## 1890166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ。何に気づいたの？<line-break>聞かせてよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? And what did you realize? Tell me.<end>"
KO: "그래? 뭘 깨달았는데? 들려줘.<end>"

## 1890167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界に存在する、<line-break>生きとし生けるものすべてが、<line-break>　<line-break>それぞれの悩みや痛みを抱いて、<line-break>生命をまっとうしているのだと…。<line-break>　<line-break>僕は<value:$28>のお蔭で<line-break>他人の痛みを知ることができた。<end>"
EN: "That every living thing in this world lives out its life bearing troubles and pain of its own... Thanks to <value:$28>, I came to understand the pain of others.<end>"
KO: "이 세상에 존재하는 모든 생명은 저마다 고민과 아픔을 안고 삶을 다하고 있다는 걸…. 나는 <value:$28> 덕분에 다른 사람의 아픔을 알 수 있었어.<end>"

## 1890168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうさ。人はみんな<line-break>大なり小なり痛みを抱えて<line-break>生きているんだよ。<line-break>姉さんを失った君だけが<line-break>特別だったわけじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>ただ君は自分の痛みにだけ目を向け<line-break>他人の痛みを知ろうとはしなかった。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>に感謝するんだね。<line-break>君の閉ざされていた目を<line-break>真実へと開かせてくれたんだから。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Everyone goes through life bearing pain, great or small. You weren't special just because you lost your sister. But you saw only your own pain and never tried to understand anyone else's. You should thank <value:$28> for opening your closed eyes to the truth.<end>"
KO: "그래. 사람은 누구나 크든 작든 아픔을 안고 살아가. 누나를 잃은 너만 특별했던 게 아니야. 그런데 너는 네 고통만 바라봤고 다른 사람의 고통은 알려 하지 않았지. <value:$28>에게 감사해. 닫혀 있던 네 눈을 진실로 향하게 해 줬으니까.<end>"

## 1890169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は<value:$28>のお蔭で<line-break>他人の痛みを知ることができた。<line-break>　<line-break>シャリ…。君も本当は他人のではなく、<line-break>自分自身の痛みを感じたいと<line-break>望んでいるのではないか？<end>"
EN: "Thanks to <value:$28>, I came to understand the pain of others. Shalli... Isn't it your own pain you truly wish to feel, rather than anyone else's?<end>"
KO: "나는 <value:$28> 덕분에 남의 아픔을 알 수 있었다. 샤리…. 너도 사실은 남이 아니라 자기 자신의 아픔을 느끼고 싶다고 바라는 게 아닐까?<end>"

## 1890170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚問だね。<line-break>僕には心もないし、痛みも感じない。<line-break>この肉体も精神もすべては虚ろ。<line-break>それにしても残念だよ、エルファス。<line-break>ここで世界が滅べば、<line-break>君の心を苛むものはなくなるんだよ。<line-break>大切な人を失う苦しみからも<line-break>解放されるのに…。<end>"
EN: "A foolish question. I have no heart, and I feel no pain. This body and mind are utterly hollow. Still, it's a pity, Elfas. If the world ended here, there would be nothing left to torment you. You would even be freed from the pain of losing someone dear to you...<end>"
KO: "어리석은 질문이군. 나에겐 마음도 없고 고통도 느끼지 않아. 이 육체와 정신 모두 공허하지. 그래도 아쉽군, 엘파스. 여기서 세계가 멸망하면 네 마음을 괴롭히는 것도 사라질 텐데. 소중한 사람을 잃는 고통에서도 해방될 수 있는데….<end>"

## 1890171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。運命の女神とやらも<line-break>よほどのいたずら好きと見える。<line-break>まさか、あれが目覚めていたとはな…。<line-break>余の計画を担うにふさわしい大器か。<line-break>あるいは名も残らぬ<line-break>ガラクタか…。<line-break>じっくり見定めるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "Heh. This so-called Goddess of Fate seems exceedingly fond of mischief. To think that had already awakened... A great talent worthy of carrying out my plan, or worthless refuse that will leave no name behind? I shall take my time and find out.<end>"
KO: "후후. 운명의 여신이라는 것도 꽤나 장난을 좋아하나 보군. 설마 저것이 깨어나 있었을 줄이야…. 짐의 계획을 짊어질 큰 그릇인가, 아니면 이름조차 남지 않을 고물인가…. 천천히 지켜보도록 하지.<end>"

## 1890172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新を目指したい<end>"
EN: "I want to work toward Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신을 지향하고 싶다<end>"

## 1890173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決戦が終わった後にそれを探したい<end>"
EN: "I want to search for my answer after the final battle.<end>"
KO: "결전이 끝난 뒤에 그걸 찾고 싶다<end>"

## 1890174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや、そこにいるのは<line-break>エルファスじゃないか。<line-break>懐かしいご同輩とここで会えるとは。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. Is that Elfas over there? I never expected to run into an old comrade here.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 거기 있는 건 엘파스 아닌가. 그리운 동료를 여기서 만나다니.<end>"

## 1890175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりだな、ジュサプブロス。<line-break>だが、あいにくと旧交を温めている<line-break>時間はない。<end>"
EN: "It's been a long time, Jusabros. Unfortunately, we don't have time to reminisce.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이군, 주사프브로스. 하지만 유감스럽게도 옛정을 나눌 시간은 없어.<end>"

## 1890176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そうだろうな。<line-break>今さら友情ごっこをするような<line-break>歳でもねえし。<line-break>それにしても、かつてはともに<line-break>世界を滅ぼそうと画策した仲間が<line-break>今や<value:$28>の味方とは…。<line-break>なかなかどうして退屈しないもんだよ。<end>"
EN: "No, I suppose not. We're a little old to play at being friends now. Still, someone who once plotted with me to destroy the world is now on <value:$28>'s side. Life does have a way of staying interesting.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇겠지. 이제 와서 우정 놀이나 할 나이도 아니고. 그런데 예전에는 함께 세계를 멸망시키려 꾸몄던 동료가 이제는 <value:$28>의 편이라니…. 참 지루할 틈이 없군.<end>"

## 1890177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、エルファス。<line-break>偽りの救世主…。<line-break>あなたは卑怯者よ。<line-break>なぜ、あなたが<line-break><value:$28>の隣にいるの？<line-break>　<line-break>世界を滅ぼすため<line-break>神にまでなろうとしたあなたこそ、<line-break>私の隣にいるべきだったのに。<end>"
EN: "And you, Elfas. False Savior... You are a coward. Why are you standing beside <value:$28>? You tried to become a god in order to destroy the world. You should have been standing beside me.<end>"
KO: "그리고 엘파스. 거짓된 구세주…. 당신은 비겁해. 왜 당신이 <value:$28> 곁에 있는 거지? 세계를 멸망시키기 위해 신이 되려고까지 했던 당신이야말로 내 곁에 있어야 했는데.<end>"

## 1890178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、本当なら僕は<line-break>君の隣に立っていたのかもしれない。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど<value:$28>のおかげで<line-break>闇に落ちずにすんだ。<line-break>そして僕は気づいたんだ。<line-break>神となることを望んだ本当の理由は、<line-break>僕自身が自分の弱さから<line-break>逃げたかったからだったんだと。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps, under other circumstances, I would have stood beside you. But <value:$28> kept me from falling into darkness. And I finally understood why I had truly wanted to become a god: I was trying to escape my own weakness.<end>"
KO: "확실히, 원래라면 내가 네 옆에 서 있었을지도 몰라. 하지만 <value:$28> 덕분에 어둠에 빠지지 않을 수 있었어. 그리고 깨달았지. 내가 신이 되길 바랐던 진짜 이유는 내 약함에서 도망치고 싶었기 때문이었다는 걸.<end>"

## 1890179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は<value:$28>と進む道を選んだ。<line-break>もう、過去からも運命からも<line-break>逃げるつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "I chose to walk the same path as <value:$28>. I will no longer run from either the past or fate.<end>"
KO: "나는 <value:$28>과 함께 가는 길을 선택했어. 이제 과거에서도 운명에서도 도망치지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 1890180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<value:$28>…。<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主。<line-break>あなたがすべての元凶なのね。<line-break>あなたにだけ人が集まり、<line-break>他はみんな、私を拒絶しようとする。<line-break>でも、そうは…いかない！<end>"
EN: "I see. <value:$28>... Bearer of the Infinite Soul. You are the source of all this. You're the only one people gather around; everyone else tries to reject me. But I won't let that happen!<end>"
KO: "그래. <value:$28>…. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자. 네가 모든 원흉이구나. 너한테만 사람들이 모이고, 다른 사람들은 모두 나를 거부하려 해. 하지만 그렇게는… 안 돼!<end>"

## 1890181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスもよくここまで来られたね。<line-break>褒めてあげるよ。えらいえらい。<end>"
EN: "You did well to make it this far, Elfas. Very good. Well done.<end>"
KO: "엘파스도 여기까지 잘 왔네. 칭찬해 줄게. 장하다, 장해.<end>"

## 1890182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕ひとりの力じゃない。<line-break><value:$28>が<line-break>僕のそばにいてくれたからだ。<end>"
EN: "I didn't do it alone. I made it because <value:$28> stayed by my side.<end>"
KO: "나 혼자만의 힘이 아니야. <value:$28>가 곁에 있어 줬기 때문이야.<end>"

## 1890183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ、勝手言っちゃって。<line-break>君が告死天使の長だった時、<line-break>忠実に従っていたレオニック文書…。<line-break>そこに記されていた予言を<line-break>自分自身の手で変えるつもりかい？<end>"
EN: "Tch. Easy for you to say. When you were the Leader of the Angels of Death, you faithfully followed the Leonic Texts... Now you mean to change the prophecy written there with your own hands?<end>"
KO: "쳇, 제멋대로 말하긴. 네가 고사천사의 수장이었을 때, 충실히 따랐던 레오닉 문서…. 거기에 적힌 예언을네 손으로 바꿀 생각이야?<end>"

## 1890184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できるかどうかは、わからない。<line-break>でも、やってみるつもりだ。<line-break><value:$28>とともに。<end>"
EN: "I don't know if I can. But I'll try. Together with <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "할 수 있을지는 모르겠어. 그래도 해 볼 생각이야. <value:$28>과 함께.<end>"

## 1890185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、それは無理かな。<line-break>僕はまだ見極めちゃいないんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>世界を滅ぼそうとする願い、<line-break>そして世界を救おうとする願い。<line-break>そのどちらをかなえるべきなのか。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. I don't think I can. I still haven't decided which wish should be granted: the wish to destroy the world, or the wish to save it.<end>"
KO: "음, 그건 무리일까. 난 아직 판단을 내리지 않았거든. 세계를 멸망시키려는 소원, 그리고 세계를 구하려는 소원. 어느 쪽을 이루어야 하는지.<end>"

## 1890186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、そうだ！<line-break>今からでも遅くないよ。<line-break>こっちに戻ってきたら？<line-break>寝返り大歓迎！<end>"
EN: "Oh, I know! It's not too late. Why don't you come back to our side? Defectors are more than welcome!<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다! 지금이라도 늦지 않았어. 이쪽으로 돌아올래? 전향은 대환영이야!<end>"

## 1890187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、僕は僕の意志で君を討つ。<line-break>もう以前の僕でないことは、<line-break>君がよく知っているはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Then I will strike you down of my own free will. You know better than anyone that I'm not who I once was.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 나는 내 의지로 너를 쓰러뜨리겠다. 이제 예전의 내가 아니라는 건 네가 잘 알고 있을 텐데.<end>"

## 1890188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かにね。君は自分の意志で<line-break>救世主になることを捨てた。<line-break>もう思い通りには動かせない。<line-break>だったら代わりの手駒を使うだけさ。<line-break>ね、ゾフォル爺さん？<end>"
EN: "True. You chose of your own free will to give up becoming the Savior. I can't make you dance to my tune anymore. So I'll simply use another pawn. Isn't that right, old Zofor?<end>"
KO: "확실히 그렇군. 너는 자신의 의지로 구세주가 되기를 버렸어. 이제 뜻대로 움직일 수 없지. 그렇다면 대신 다른 말을 쓰면 될 뿐이야. 그렇지, 조포르 할아버지?<end>"

## 1890189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私をさげすむか、ゾフォル？<end>"
EN: "Do you scorn me, Zofor?<end>"
KO: "나를 업신여기는 건가, 조포르?<end>"

## 1890190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さげすむだと？　笑わせおる。<line-break>今のお前にはそんな価値すらもない。<line-break>わしは失望すらせぬよ。<line-break>お前が変わっていくことは<line-break>うすうす予見できていたからな。<end>"
EN: "Scorn you? Don't make me laugh. As you are now, you're not even worth my scorn. I cannot even say I'm disappointed. I had some inkling that you would change.<end>"
KO: "경멸한다고? 웃기는군. 지금의 너는 그럴 가치조차 없다. 나는 실망조차 하지 않아. 네가 변해 갈 건 어렴풋이 예상하고 있었으니까.<end>"

## 1890191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは<line-break>自分の見た未来に従って<line-break>ただ進むのみ…。<end>"
EN: "I do nothing but follow the future I have seen...<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 본 미래를 따라 그저 나아갈 뿐….<end>"

## 1890192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、エルファス。<line-break>偽りの救世主…。<line-break>　<line-break>なぜ、あなたが<line-break><value:$28>の隣にいるの？<line-break>　<line-break>世界を滅ぼすため<line-break>神にまでなろうとしたあなたこそ、<line-break>私の隣にいるべきだったのに。<end>"
EN: "And you, Elfas. False Savior... Why are you standing beside <value:$28>? You tried to become a god to destroy the world. You, of all people, should be beside me.<end>"
KO: "그리고 엘파스. 거짓된 구세주…. 왜 당신이 <value:$28> 곁에 있는 거지? 세계를 멸망시키려고 신까지 되려 했던 당신이야말로 내 곁에 있어야 했는데.<end>"

## 1890193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、本当なら僕は<line-break>君の隣に立っていたのかもしれない。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど<value:$28>のおかげで<line-break>闇に落ちずにすんだ。<line-break>そして僕は気づいたんだ。<line-break>神となることを望んだ本当の理由は、<line-break>僕自身が自分の弱さから<line-break>逃げたかったからだったんだと。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps, under other circumstances, I would have stood beside you. But <value:$28> kept me from falling into darkness. And I finally understood why I truly wanted to become a god: I was trying to run away from my own weakness.<end>"
KO: "확실히, 원래라면 내가 네 옆에 서 있었을지도 몰라. 하지만 <value:$28> 덕분에 어둠에 빠지지 않을 수 있었어. 그리고 깨달았지. 내가 신이 되길 바랐던 진짜 이유는 내 약함에서 도망치고 싶었기 때문이었다는 걸.<end>"

## 1890194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしようもないおバカさんたち…。<line-break>英雄も、救世主も、そして…。<line-break>　<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主ですらも<line-break>暗闇に生きてきた私の苦しみを<line-break>理解してはくれなかった。<line-break>でも、もういいの。<line-break>もうすぐあなたたちも、すべて<line-break>私のものになるんだから。<end>"
EN: "You hopeless fools... The hero, the Savior, and even... the bearer of the Infinite Soul. Not one of you could understand the suffering I endured, living in Blindness. But it no longer matters. Soon, every last one of you will be mine.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없는 바보들…. 영웅도, 구세주도, 그리고…. 무한의 소울을 가진 자조차 어둠 속에서 살아온 내 고통을 이해해 주지 않았어. 하지만 이제 됐어. 곧 너희도 전부 내 것이 될 테니까.<end>"

## 1890195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ…。本音を言うと、<line-break>僕はやはり君とは戦いたくない。<line-break>できれば去ってくれないか？<end>"
EN: "Shalli... To tell you the truth, I still don't want to fight you. Couldn't you leave, if at all possible?<end>"
KO: "샤리…. 솔직히 말하면 난 역시 너와 싸우고 싶지 않아. 가능하면 떠나 주지 않겠어?<end>"

## 1890196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>賞賛するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. I commend your indomitable will.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는군, 네메아. 어둠의 신기 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 보통 사람이라면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 터. 그 강인한 정신은 칭찬하마.<end>"

## 1890197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>賞賛するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. I commend your indomitable will.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는군, 네메아. 어둠의 신기 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 보통 사람이라면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 터. 그 강인한 정신은 칭찬하마.<end>"

## 1890198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た。<line-break>だが、徒労だったな。<line-break>ここには魔王バロルはおらぬ。<line-break>傷を癒すべく、地上で最も闇の深い場所<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かった。<end>"
EN: "You have come far, but it was all in vain. Balor, Demon King, is not here. To heal his wounds, he has gone to the darkest place on earth: the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다. 하지만 헛걸음했구나. 이곳에 마왕 발로르는 없다. 상처를 치유하려 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳, 폐성의 왕좌로 향했다.<end>"

## 1890199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "Ｖ７終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "V7 끝<end>"

## 1900000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "Ｖ７その２開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "V7 파트 2 시작<end>"

## 1900001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またも円卓騎士か！<line-break>開け放つものヴァシュタール！<line-break>　<line-break>このエンシャントのどこかで、<line-break>闇の力が、極度に<line-break>強まっているということか。<end>"
EN: "Another Knight of the Round Table! Vashtar the Opener! Does this mean the power of darkness has grown immensely somewhere in Ancient?<end>"
KO: "또 원탁기사인가! 열어젖히는 자 바슈타르! 이 엔샨트 어딘가에서 어둠의 힘이 극도로 강해지고 있다는 뜻인가.<end>"

## 1900002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "罠か…。<line-break>ゾフォルの仕業だな。　　　　　　<line-break>先に行け、<value:$28>。<line-break>ゾフォルを倒さぬ限り、<line-break>こいつらは何度倒しても復活する。<end>"
EN: "A trap... Zofor's doing, no doubt. Go on ahead, <value:$28>. Until Zofor is defeated, these creatures will rise again no matter how many times we strike them down.<end>"
KO: "함정인가…. 조포르의 짓이군. 먼저 가라, <value:$28>. 조포르를 쓰러뜨리지 않는 한 이 녀석들은 몇 번을 쓰러뜨려도 부활한다.<end>"

## 1900003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が抑えておく。<line-break>その間にゾフォルを倒せ。<line-break>奴の気をこの先に感じる。<end>"
EN: "I'll hold them here. Defeat Zofor in the meantime. I sense his presence up ahead.<end>"
KO: "내가 억누르고 있겠다. 그사이에 조포르를 쓰러뜨려. 녀석의 기운이 이 앞에서 느껴진다.<end>"

## 1900004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行け！<end>"
EN: "Go!<end>"
KO: "가!<end>"

## 1900005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、来たよ。<line-break><value:$28>が。<end>"
EN: "Look who's here. It's <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "봐, 왔어. <value:$28>가.<end>"

## 1900006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紹介しよう。<line-break>エンシャントの新皇帝<line-break>ザギヴ・ディンガル陛下だ。　　　　<line-break>ディンガル家の正統な後継者であり、<line-break>強大な闇の力を身中に宿しておる。<line-break>闇の世を治めるにふさわしき皇帝よ。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to present Her Majesty Zagiv Dyneskal, the new Empress of Ancient. The rightful heir to House Dyneskal, she bears a mighty power of darkness within her. An empress worthy to rule the world of darkness.<end>"
KO: "소개하지. 엔샨트의 새 황제, 자기브 딩갈 폐하시다. 딩갈가의 정통 후계자이자 몸속에 강대한 어둠의 힘을 품고 계신다. 어둠의 시대를 다스리기에 걸맞은 황제지.<end>"

## 1900007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2あなた、ザギヴ様に何をしたの！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3お主、ザギヴに何をしおった！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4ザギヴ様になんかしたわね。<line-break>いけないじじいですね、まったく。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5てめえ、ザギヴに何かしやがったな！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6ザギヴに何をしたの！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>7ザギヴほどの魔道士が<line-break>操られている！？<line-break>シャリ、何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>8ザギヴに潜んでいる闇に<line-break>力を与えましたね。<line-break>ひどいことを…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>9ザギヴの様子が変ね。<line-break>シャリ、また何かやったね？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>12何かに操られているのか？<line-break>爺さん、あんた何をしたんだ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13あの女、様子が変だ…。<line-break>貴様、何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、ザギヴが変だよ。<line-break>こら、じじい！　ザギヴに何した！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14お主、ザギヴに何をしたのじゃ！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ザギヴさん…、様子が変だね。<line-break>シャリに操られている…？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17やーね、まったく、あの女、<line-break>自分を忘れてるじゃない。<line-break>いったいどうなってるのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>、<line-break>ザギヴさんが変だよぉ。<line-break>またシャリが何かしたんだよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20闇に<line-break>操られているな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21様子がおかしい…<line-break>貴様、何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23貴様、ザギヴに何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27様子が変だ…。<line-break>操られているのか？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26おぬし、ザギヴ殿に<line-break>何をしたんじゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>24様子がおかしい…？<line-break>操られているというの…？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>25様子が変… <end>あなた、何をしたの！？ <end>ディンガル新皇帝の誕生…。<line-break>歴史的な瞬間だけど、<line-break>こんな場面を詩にしたくはないよ。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2What have you done to Lady Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3What have you done to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4You did something to Lady Zagiv, didn't you? What a bad old man you are. Honestly.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5You bastard! You did something to Zagiv!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6What did you do to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7A sorceress as powerful as Zagiv is being controlled?! Shalli, what have you done?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8You gave power to the darkness lurking within Zagiv. What a horrible thing to do...<end><if><value:$01><equal>9There's something strange about Zagiv. Shalli, what have you done this time?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Is something controlling her? Old man, what did you do?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13There's something wrong with that woman... What have you done?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, something's wrong with Zagiv. Hey, old man! What did you do to her?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What have you done to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Something's wrong with Zagiv... Is Shalli controlling her?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh, honestly! That woman has completely forgotten herself. What in the world is going on?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, something's wrong with Zagiv. Shalli must have done something again.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20The darkness is controlling her.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Something is wrong... What have you done?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23What have you done to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Something's wrong... Is she being controlled?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What have you done to Lady Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Something is wrong... Is she being controlled?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Something's wrong...<end>What have you done?!<end>The birth of Dyneskal's new empress... A historic moment, but not one I'd want to immortalize in verse.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2당신, 자기브 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3네놈, 자기브에게 무슨 짓을 했느냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4자기브 님에게 뭔가 했지. 정말 못된 늙은이네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5네놈, 자기브한테 뭔가 한 거지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6자기브에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7자기브 정도의 마도사가 조종당하고 있어!? 샤리, 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8자기브 안에 숨어 있는 어둠에 힘을 줬군요. 끔찍한 짓을….<end><if><value:$01><equal>9자기브 상태가 이상하네. 샤리, 또 뭔가 했지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12뭔가에 조종당하는 건가? 영감, 당신 무슨 짓을 한 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13저 여자, 상태가 이상해…. 네놈, 무슨 짓을 했지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 자기브가 이상해. 이봐, 영감! 자기브한테 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14네놈, 자기브에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15자기브 씨… 상태가 이상하네. 샤리에게 조종당하는 건가…?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17정말 싫네, 저 여자, 자기를 잊어버렸잖아. 대체 어떻게 된 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, 자기브 씨가 이상해애. 또 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20어둠에 조종당하고 있군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21상태가 이상해… 네놈, 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23네놈, 자기브에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27상태가 이상해…. 조종당하는 건가?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26네놈, 자기브 공에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24상태가 이상해…? 조종당하고 있다는 거야…?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25상태가 이상해….<end>당신, 무슨 짓을 한 거야!?<end>딩갈 새 황제의 탄생…. 역사적인 순간이지만 이런 장면을 시로 쓰고 싶진 않아.<end>"

## 1900008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう騒ぐでない。これも運命。<line-break>ザギヴに巣くっておった闇が、<line-break>時を得て甦りつつあるだけのこと。<line-break>ネメアを失い、弱くなった心では、<line-break>闇を抑えきれなかったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Enough of this clamor. This too is fate. The darkness that nested within Zagiv has merely found its hour and begun to awaken. With Nemea gone, her weakened heart could no longer contain it.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 소란 피울 것 없다. 이것도 운명. 자기브에게 깃들어 있던 어둠이 때를 얻어 되살아나고 있을 뿐이다. 네메아를 잃고 약해진 마음으로는 어둠을 억누르지 못한 것이지.<end>"

## 1900009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、お主への想いが<line-break>どうやら最後の覚醒にいたらせぬ<line-break>原因のようじゃ。<end>"
EN: "And yet, it seems her feelings for you are what keep the final awakening at bay.<end>"
KO: "하지만 네게 품은 마음이 아무래도 마지막 각성에 이르지 못하는 원인인 듯하구나.<end>"

## 1900011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、それも幻想にすぎぬということを<line-break>教えてやらねばなるまい。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>よ、どうする？<line-break>ザギヴとともに闇を滅ぼすか？<line-break>じゃが、そのときはザギヴも死ぬぞ。<end>"
EN: "But she must be taught that even this is no more than an illusion. What will you do, <value:$28>? Will you destroy the darkness together with Zagiv? If you do, Zagiv will die as well.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그것도 환상에 지나지 않는다는 걸 가르쳐 줘야겠군. <value:$28>여, 어떻게 할 거냐? 자기브와 함께 어둠을 멸할 건가? 하지만 그때는 자기브도 죽는다.<end>"

## 1900012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、ザギヴ。<line-break>自らの手で試すがよい。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ<value:$28>であっても、<line-break>恐怖の存在には剣を向けるしかない<line-break>ということをな。<end>"
EN: "Now, Zagiv. See for yourself. Even <value:$28> can do nothing but raise a sword against something to be feared.<end>"
KO: "자, 자기브. 네 손으로 직접 시험해 봐라. 설령 <value:$28>이라도 공포의 존재 앞에서는 검을 겨눌 수밖에 없다는 걸.<end>"

## 1900013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2え、<value:$28>？<line-break>ひとりで戦うって…、ちょっと！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3なんじゃと？<line-break>お主、ひとりで戦うつもりか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4え、ひとりで戦うの？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ふーん…、ちょっとかっこいいかも。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5ひとりで戦うだって！？<line-break>おい、マジかよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6ひとりで戦うって…。<line-break><value:$28>…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7ひとりで戦うですって？<line-break>バカな！　死ぬ気なの！<line-break>ザギヴは強力な魔道士なのよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>8ひとりで戦うのですか？　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そうですか、わかりました。<line-break>手は出しません。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>9ひとりで戦うの？　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ふーん、ま、いいけどね。<line-break>あ、わかってると思うけど、<line-break>ほんとに死んだら、意味ないかんね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12そうか、ひとりで戦うか。　　　　<line-break>無茶はするなよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ほんとに死んじまったら、<line-break>意味がなくなっちまうんだからな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13ひとりで戦うだと！？<line-break>…正気か？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、ひとりで戦うの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14お主、ひとりで戦うつもりか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ひとりで戦うの？　本当に？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっとぉ、どういうことなのぉ！<line-break>ひとりで戦うですって！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19え、ひとりで戦うのぉ！？<line-break><value:$28>、大丈夫？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20ひとりで戦うのか？<line-break>…わかった。譲ろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21ひとりで戦うというのか？！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…わかった。 <line-break>だが、無理だけはしないでくれ<end><if><value:$01><equal>23ひとりで戦うつもりなのか？<line-break><value:$28>よ<end><if><value:$01><equal>27ひとりで戦うつもりなのか？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんじゃと？<line-break><value:$28>殿<line-break>ひとりで戦うつもりか？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>24おひとりで戦われるのですね…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ご無理だけはなさらないでください<line-break><value:$28>様…<end><if><value:$01><equal>25ひとりで戦うなんて…！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お願い、無理はしないで…<end>え、ひとりで戦うの！？<line-break>本当にそれでいいの？ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2What, <value:$28>? You're going to fight alone? Wait!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3What? You intend to fight alone?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4What, you're fighting alone? Hmm... That's actually kind of impressive.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5You're fighting alone?! Are you serious?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6You're going to fight alone... <value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>7You're going to fight alone? Don't be a fool! Are you trying to get yourself killed? Zagiv is a powerful sorceress!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8You intend to fight alone? Very well. I understand. I won't interfere.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9You're fighting alone? Hmm. Fine by me. But you do realize there's no point if you actually get yourself killed, right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12So you're fighting alone. Don't do anything reckless. None of this will mean a thing if you actually get yourself killed.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You mean to fight alone?! Have you lost your mind?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Are you going to fight alone?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14You intend to fight alone?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15You're going to fight alone? Really?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Wait just a moment! What's the meaning of this? You're fighting alone?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19What, you're fighting alone?! Are you sure you'll be all right, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20You're fighting alone? Very well. I'll stand aside.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21You mean to fight alone?! Very well. But please, don't push yourself too far.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Do you intend to fight alone, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27You intend to fight alone?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What? <value:$28>, do you intend to fight alone?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24So you mean to fight alone... Please, do not overexert yourself, <value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>25You're going to fight alone...! Please, don't push yourself too far...<end>What, you're fighting alone?! Are you sure about this?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2어, <value:$28>? 혼자 싸운다고… 잠깐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3뭐라고? 너 혼자 싸울 셈이냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어, 혼자 싸우는 거야? 흐음… 좀 멋있을지도.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5혼자 싸운다고!? 야, 진짜냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6혼자 싸운다니…. <value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>7혼자 싸운다고? 바보 같은! 죽을 셈이야! 자기브는 강력한 마도사라고!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8혼자 싸우시는 겁니까? 그렇군요, 알겠습니다. 손대지 않겠습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9혼자 싸워? 뭐, 좋아. 그래도 진짜 죽으면 의미 없으니까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12그래, 혼자 싸우겠다는 거군. 무리하지 마. 정말 죽으면 의미가 없어져.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13혼자 싸운다고!? …제정신인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 혼자 싸울 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14너 혼자 싸울 셈이냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15혼자 싸울 거야? 정말?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐, 무슨 소리야! 혼자 싸우겠다고!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19어, 혼자 싸우는 거야!? <value:$28>, 괜찮아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20혼자 싸울 건가? …알았다. 양보하지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21혼자 싸우겠다고?! …알았다. 하지만 무리는 하지 마라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23혼자 싸울 생각인가? <value:$28>여.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27혼자 싸울 생각인가?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26뭐라고? <value:$28> 님 혼자 싸우실 셈입니까?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24혼자 싸우시는군요…. 무리만은 하지 마십시오, <value:$28> 님….<end><if><value:$01><equal>25혼자 싸우다니…! 부탁이야, 무리하지 마….<end>어, 혼자 싸울 거야!? 정말 그래도 돼?<end>"

## 1900014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギウよ、自らの血で<line-break>闇に目覚めるがよい！<end>"
EN: "Zagiv, let your own blood awaken you to darkness!<end>"
KO: "자기브여, 자신의 피로 어둠에 눈을 떠라!<end>"

## 1900015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。<line-break>ウウ…ア…アア…アアグ…。<line-break>ガアアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Uhh... Ah... Aah... Aaagh... Gaaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "으으…. 우우… 아… 아아… 아악…. 가아아아악!<end>"

## 1900016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、倒しちゃったよ。<line-break>やっぱりね。そんなもんだよね。<line-break>フフフ…、あははは…！<end>"
EN: "Oh dear, you struck her down. Just as I thought. That's how it goes, isn't it? Heh heh... Ah ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "아아, 쓰러뜨려 버렸네. 역시나. 그런 거지 뭐. 후후후… 아하하하…!<end>"

## 1900017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり、これが答えか…。<line-break>さあ、目覚めよ。<line-break>ザギヴに潜みし闇よ！<end>"
EN: "So this is your answer after all... Now awaken, darkness that lurks within Zagiv!<end>"
KO: "역시 이것이 답인가… 자, 깨어나라. 자기브 안에 숨어 있는 어둠이여!<end>"

## 1900018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁーっと、くたばったか。<line-break>これでこのカラダは俺のモンだ！<end>"
EN: "Finally, she's kicked the bucket. This body is mine now!<end>"
KO: "드디어 뒈졌나. 이제 이 몸은 내 거다!<end>"

## 1900020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！　奴が死んで、<line-break>俺は本格的に活動できる！<line-break>ありがとよ。ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>まず手始めに、お前を殺す！<line-break>マゴス様の恐怖のフルコース、<line-break>とくと味わえ！　ギーヒッ！<end>"
EN: "Gihihihi! Now that she's dead, I can really get to work! Thanks for that. Gihihihi! And to start things off, I'll kill you! Prepare to savor the great Magos's full course of terror! Gihih!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 놈이 죽어서 이제 내가 본격적으로 움직일 수 있다! 고맙다. 기히히힛! 우선 시작으로 너부터 죽이겠다! 마고스 님의 공포 풀코스를 실컷 맛봐라! 기히힛!<end>"

## 1900022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>俺様を殺してどうするつもりなんだ？<line-break>何も変わりゃ…しないぜ…！<line-break>ザギヴって女も、<line-break>生きかえりは…しないぜ。クク…。<line-break>腹立ち紛れの復讐かァ…ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Gihihihi! What's killing me going to do? It won't change... a thing! That Zagiv woman... won't come back to life either. Heh heh... Just lashing out in anger, are you? Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 이 몸을 죽여서 어쩔 셈이냐? 아무것도 변하진… 않아…! 자기브라는 여자도 살아나진… 않아. 큭큭…. 화풀이 복수냐… 하하하!<end>"

## 1900023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>君の切り札は簡単に<line-break>やられちゃったけど、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Well, old Zofor? Your trump card went down rather easily. What will you do now?<end>"
KO: "자, 조포르 영감. 네 비장의 수가 너무 쉽게 당해 버렸는데, 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1900024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃが、シャリよ。<line-break>お主らの切り札は残っておろう？<line-break>あとは任せよう。<line-break>わしはここらで、<line-break>わしの運命に決着をつける。<end>"
EN: "But Shalli, your side still has its trump card, does it not? I shall leave the rest to you. It is time I settled matters with my own fate.<end>"
KO: "하지만 샤리여, 너희의 비장의 수는 남아 있겠지? 뒤는 맡기겠다. 나는 여기서 내 운명에 결판을 내겠다.<end>"

## 1900025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。じゃあね、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>あとは僕とエルファスでやるよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Goodbye, old Zofor. Elfas and I will take it from here.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 잘 있어, 조포르 할아버지. 나머지는 나와 엘파스가 할게.<end>"

## 1900026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの運命の幕を<line-break>降ろしてもらおうか。<line-break>　<line-break>…ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, come and bring down the curtain on my fate... Nemea.<end>"
KO: "자, 내 운명의 막을 내려다오. …네메아여.<end>"

## 1900027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっふ、久しいな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It has been a long time.<end>"
KO: "후후, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 1900028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ。<line-break>お前の予言が私の運命を示し、<line-break>私はその運命と戦ってきた。<end>"
EN: "Zofor. Your prophecy revealed my fate, and I have fought against it ever since.<end>"
KO: "조포르여. 네 예언이 내 운명을 가리켰고, 나는 그 운명과 싸워 왔다.<end>"

## 1900029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃったな。<line-break>じゃが、そろそろ終わりにしよう。<line-break>もう、お主も疲れたろう？<end>"
EN: "So you have. But it is time we brought this to an end. You must be tired by now.<end>"
KO: "그랬지. 하지만 이제 슬슬 끝내자꾸나. 너도 이제 지쳤겠지?<end>"

## 1900030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "受け入れられぬ運命であれば、<line-break>私はこれからも戦い続けるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If fate is unacceptable, then I will go on fighting it.<end>"
KO: "받아들일 수 없는 운명이라면 나는 앞으로도 계속 싸울 것이다.<end>"

## 1900031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっふ、そうか。<line-break>ならば最後の予言を残そうか。<line-break>それは…、<line-break>お主らの死じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I see. Then let me leave you with one final prophecy: your deaths!<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런가. 그럼 마지막 예언을 남기지. 그것은… 너희들의 죽음이다!<end>"

## 1900032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、これで眠れる…。<line-break>不吉な予言を見ることも、<line-break>もうあるまいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Now I can sleep. Never again shall I behold an ill-omened prophecy.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 이제 잠들 수 있겠군…. 불길한 예언을 보는 일도 이제 없겠지.<end>"

## 1900033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>お前は最後に何を見たのだ？<line-break>我々の死ではあるまい。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What did you see at the end? Surely it was not our deaths.<end>"
KO: "조포르… 너는 마지막에 무엇을 보았지? 우리의 죽음은 아닐 것이다.<end>"

## 1900034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ぐふっ。ムダだ…。<line-break>運命は…、変わらぬ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Guh! It's useless... Fate... does not change...<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 크흑. 소용없다…. 운명은… 바뀌지 않는다….<end>"

## 1900035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>私たちには進むことしかできぬ。<line-break>シャリはこの城から出たようだ。<line-break>エンシャントの町のどこかに<line-break>潜んでいるはず。捜そう。<end>"
EN: "Let's go, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can only press forward. Shalli appears to have left the castle. He must be hiding somewhere in the city of Ancient. Let us find him.<end>"
KO: "가자, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리는 앞으로 나아갈 수밖에 없다. 샤리는 이 성에서 나간 모양이다. 엔샨트 마을 어딘가에 숨어 있을 거야. 찾아보자.<end>"

## 1900036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな…。<line-break>勝てる戦いだったはず。<end>"
EN: "Impossible... I should have won that battle.<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…. 이길 수 있는 싸움이었을 텐데.<end>"

## 1900037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、さすが<value:$28>。<line-break>君を見ていると飽きないよ。<line-break>本当に負けたのか、わざとなのか…。<line-break>どちらにしろ、<line-break>彼女は救われたわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Just what I'd expect from <value:$28>. Watching you never grows dull. Did you truly lose, or was it deliberate...? Either way, she has been saved.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 역시 <value:$28>. 널 보고 있으면 질리지 않아. 정말 진 건지 일부러 그런 건지…. 어느 쪽이든 그녀는 구원받았군.<end>"

## 1900038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、ああ…。　　　<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Ah... Aah... <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아… 아아…. <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1900039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫…。<line-break>私は…、闇には…、<line-break>負けない。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right... I won't... surrender to the darkness.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아…. 나는… 어둠에는… 지지 않아.<end>"

## 1900040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴめ…。<line-break>闇を退けおったか！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, Zagiv... You drove back the darkness?!<end>"
KO: "자기브 녀석…. 어둠을 물리친 건가!<end>"

## 1900041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、惑わされないわ。<line-break>私は、私よ。闇にこの心を<line-break>明け渡したりはしない！<end>"
EN: "I won't be led astray again. I am myself. I will not surrender my heart to the darkness!<end>"
KO: "이제 속지 않아. 나는 나야. 어둠에게 이 마음을 내주지 않겠어!<end>"

## 1900042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>君の切り札は簡単に<line-break>やられちゃったけど、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Well, old Zofor? Your trump card went down rather easily. What will you do now?<end>"
KO: "자, 조포르 영감. 네 비장의 수가 너무 쉽게 당해 버렸는데, 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1900043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃが、シャリよ。<line-break>お主らの切り札は残っておろう。<line-break>あとは任せよう。<line-break>わしはここらで、<line-break>わしの運命に決着をつける。<end>"
EN: "But Shalli, your side still has its trump card. I shall leave the rest to you. It is time I settled matters with my own fate.<end>"
KO: "하지만 샤리여, 너희의 비장의 수는 남아 있겠지. 뒤는 맡기겠다. 나는 여기서 내 운명에 결판을 내겠다.<end>"

## 1900044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。じゃあね、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>あとは、僕とエルファスでやるよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Goodbye, old Zofor. Elfas and I will take it from here.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 안녕, 조포르 할아버지. 나머지는 나와 엘파스가 할게.<end>"

## 1900045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの運命の幕を<line-break>降ろしてもらおうか。<line-break>　<line-break>…ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, come and bring down the curtain on my fate... Nemea.<end>"
KO: "자, 내 운명의 막을 내려다오. …네메아여.<end>"

## 1900046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、ネメア様！<line-break>よかった、ご無事でしたか！<end>"
EN: "Ah... Lord Nemea! Thank goodness. You're unharmed!<end>"
KO: "아… 네메아 님! 다행입니다, 무사하셨군요!<end>"

## 1900047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらぬ心配をかけたな。<line-break>許せ、ザギヴ。<end>"
EN: "I caused you needless worry. Forgive me, Zagiv.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 걱정을 끼쳤군. 용서해라, 자기브.<end>"

## 1900048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっふ、久しいな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It has been a long time.<end>"
KO: "후후, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 1900049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ。<line-break>お前の予言が私の運命を示し、<line-break>私はその運命と戦ってきた。<end>"
EN: "Zofor. Your prophecy revealed my fate, and I have fought against it ever since.<end>"
KO: "조포르여. 네 예언이 내 운명을 가리켰고, 나는 그 운명과 싸워 왔다.<end>"

## 1900050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃったな。<line-break>じゃが、そろそろ終わりにしよう。<line-break>もう、お主も疲れたろう？<end>"
EN: "So you have. But it is time we brought this to an end. You must be tired by now.<end>"
KO: "그랬지. 하지만 이제 슬슬 끝내자꾸나. 너도 이제 지쳤겠지?<end>"

## 1900051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "受け入れられぬ運命であれば、<line-break>私はこれからも戦い続けるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If fate is unacceptable, then I will go on fighting it.<end>"
KO: "받아들일 수 없는 운명이라면 나는 앞으로도 계속 싸울 것이다.<end>"

## 1900052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうか。<line-break>ならば最後の予言を残そうか。<line-break>それは…<line-break>お主らの死じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I see. Then let me leave you with one final prophecy: your deaths!<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그런가. 그럼 마지막 예언을 남기지. 그것은… 너희의 죽음이다!<end>"

## 1900053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、これで眠れる…。<line-break>不吉な予言を見ることも、<line-break>もうあるまいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Now I can sleep. Never again shall I behold an ill-omened prophecy.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 이제 잠들 수 있겠군…. 불길한 예언을 보는 일도 이제 없겠지.<end>"

## 1900054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>お前は最後に何を見たのだ？<line-break>我々の死ではあるまい。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What did you see at the end? Surely it was not our deaths.<end>"
KO: "조포르… 너는 마지막에 무엇을 보았지? 우리의 죽음은 아닐 것이다.<end>"

## 1900055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっふっふ…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ぐふっ。ムダだ…。<line-break>運命は…、変わらぬ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Guh! It's useless... Fate... does not change...<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 크흑. 소용없다…. 운명은… 변하지 않아….<end>"

## 1900056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>私たちには進むことしかできぬ。<line-break>シャリはこの城から出たようだ。<line-break>エンシャントの町のどこかに<line-break>潜んでいるはず。捜そう。<end>"
EN: "Let's go, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can only press forward. Shalli appears to have left the castle. He must be hiding somewhere in the city of Ancient. Let us find him.<end>"
KO: "가자, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리는 앞으로 나아갈 수밖에 없다. 샤리는 이 성에서 나간 모양이다. 엔샨트 마을 어딘가에 숨어 있을 거야. 찾아보자.<end>"

## 1900057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ、ベルゼーヴァに伝えよ。<line-break>エンシャント奪還の準備を調えておけ<line-break>…とな。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv, tell Beelzeva to have preparations in place to retake Ancient.<end>"
KO: "자기브, 벨제바에게 전해라. 엔샨트 탈환 준비를 갖춰 두라고….<end>"

## 1900058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！　お任せください。<end>"
EN: "Yes! Leave it to me.<end>"
KO: "네! 맡겨 주세요.<end>"

## 1900059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ありがとう。<line-break>あなたのおかげで、<line-break>踏みとどまれたわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. Because of you, I was able to hold on.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 고마워. 네 덕분에 버틸 수 있었어.<end>"

## 1900060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の力が必要なら呼び出して。<line-break>今度は私が<line-break>あなたの力になる番よ。<line-break>気をつけて…。<line-break>必ず、また会いましょう。<end>"
EN: "If you need my power, call on me. This time, it's my turn to help you. Take care... We must meet again.<end>"
KO: "내 힘이 필요하면 불러. 이번엔 내가 네 힘이 될 차례야. 조심해…. 반드시 다시 만나자.<end>"

## 1900061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう騒ぐでない。これも運命。<line-break>ザギヴに巣くっておった闇が、<line-break>時を得て、甦りつつあるだけのこと。<line-break>ネメアを失い、弱くなった心では、<line-break>闇を抑えきれなかったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Do not make such a fuss. This too is fate. The darkness that dwelt within Zagiv has simply found its hour and begun to awaken. Her heart was weakened by the loss of Nemea and could no longer hold it back.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 소란 피우지 마라. 이것도 운명. 자기브에게 깃들어 있던 어둠이 때를 만나 되살아나고 있을 뿐이다. 네메아를 잃고 약해진 마음으로는 어둠을 억누르지 못한 거지.<end>"

## 1900062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、目覚めよ。<line-break>ザギヴに潜みし闇よ！<end>"
EN: "Now awaken, darkness that lurks within Zagiv!<end>"
KO: "자, 깨어나라. 자기브 안에 숨은 어둠이여!<end>"

## 1900063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。<line-break>ウウ…ア…アア…アアグ…。<line-break>ガアアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Uhh... Ah... Aah... Aaagh... Gaaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "으으…. 우우… 아… 아아… 아악…. 가아아아악!<end>"

## 1900064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！　長かったぜぇ！<line-break>ネメアにやられてから<line-break>ずっと待ってたんだよ！<line-break>この女の中に潜んで、<line-break>闇の力が強くなるのをよ！<line-break>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! I've waited so long! Ever since Nemea struck me down, I've been hiding inside this woman, waiting for the power of darkness to grow!"
KO: "기히히힛! 오래 기다렸다고! 네메아에게 당한 뒤로 계속 기다렸어! 이 여자 안에 숨어서 어둠의 힘이 강해지기를 말이야!"

## 1900065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついに復活したぜ！<line-break>これで本格的に活動できる！<line-break>ありがとよ。ギーヒッヒッ！　<line-break>まず手始めに、お前を殺す！<line-break>マゴス様の恐怖のフルコース、<line-break>とくと味わえ！　ギーヒッ！<end>"
EN: "At last, I'm back! Now I can really get to work! Thanks for that. Geh-heh-heh! And to start with, I'll kill you! Prepare to savor Lord Magos's full course of terror! Geh-heh!<end>"
KO: "드디어 부활했다! 이제 본격적으로 활동할 수 있어! 고맙다. 기히히힛! 우선 시작으로 네놈을 죽여 주마! 마고스 님의 공포 풀코스, 실컷 맛봐라! 기힛!<end>"

## 1900066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>俺様を殺してどうするつもりなんだ？　<line-break>何も変わりゃ…しないぜ…！<line-break>ザギヴって女も、<line-break>生きかえりは…しないぜ。<line-break>クク…。ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! What do you think killing me will accomplish? It won't... change a thing! That woman Zagiv... won't come back to life. Heh... Hahahaha!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 이 몸을 쓰러뜨려서 어쩔 셈이지? 아무것도 변하진… 않아…! 자기브라는 여자도, 다시 살아나진… 않아. 크크…. 하하하!<end>"

## 1900067

## 1900080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>そろそろ幕が近いよ。<line-break>君はついにここまで来たんだから。<line-break>僕を倒せばこの後ろの扉が開く。<line-break>そしてその先に、旅の終着があるよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, it's nearly time for the curtain to fall. You've finally made it this far. Defeat me, and the door behind me will open. Beyond it lies the end of your journey.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 슬슬 막이 내릴 때가 가까워. 넌 마침내 여기까지 왔으니까. 나를 쓰러뜨리면 뒤의 문이 열린다. 그리고 그 너머에 여행의 종착점이 있어.<end>"

## 1900081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリよ、お前の狙いがなんであれ、<line-break>生かしておくわけにはいかぬ。<end>"
EN: "Shalli, whatever your aim may be, I cannot allow you to live.<end>"
KO: "샤리, 네 목적이 무엇이든 살려 둘 수는 없다.<end>"

## 1900082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうだろうね。<line-break>さあ、はじめようか。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I thought as much. Shall we begin?<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그렇겠지. 자, 시작할까.<end>"

## 1900083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強くなったね、<value:$28>。<line-break>僕はうれしいよ。<line-break>僕の役目はこれで終わりだ。<end>"
EN: "You've grown strong, <value:$28>. I'm glad. My part in this is over now.<end>"
KO: "강해졌구나, <value:$28>. 난 기뻐. 내 역할은 이걸로 끝이야.<end>"

## 1900084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？<line-break>どういうことだ、虚無の子よ。<end>"
EN: "What? What do you mean, Child of the Void?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고? 무슨 뜻이냐, 허무의 아이여.<end>"

## 1900085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇と虚無のはざま。そこに<line-break>人の思いがたゆたう場所がある。<line-break>　<line-break>かなえられなかった願いが<line-break>集まる場所がね。<end>"
EN: "Between darkness and the Void lies a place where human thoughts drift―a place where unfulfilled wishes gather.<end>"
KO: "어둠과 허무의 틈. 그곳에는 사람의 마음이 떠도는 장소가 있어. 이루지 못한 소원이 모이는 곳이지.<end>"

## 1900086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は、そこから生まれた。　　<line-break>かなえられなかった願いを<line-break>かなえるために。<line-break>ただ、願いだけを救うために。<end>"
EN: "I was born from that place to fulfill those unfulfilled wishes. To save wishes, and nothing else.<end>"
KO: "나는 거기서 태어났어. 이루지 못한 소원을 이루기 위해. 그저 소원만을 구하기 위해.<end>"

## 1900087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんなに時が経っていようと、<line-break>どんな人の願いであろうと、<line-break>　<line-break>かなえられた願いが<line-break>どんな結果をもたらそうと、<line-break>願いの善悪に関係なく、<end>"
EN: "No matter how much time has passed, no matter whose wish it is, no matter what consequences fulfilling it may bring, and regardless of whether the wish is good or evil...<end>"
KO: "얼마나 오랜 시간이 지났든, 누구의 소원이든, 이루어진 소원이 어떤 결과를 낳든, 그 소원의 선악과 관계없이,<end>"

## 1900088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は、ただ願いを救う。<end>"
EN: "I simply save wishes.<end>"
KO: "나는 그저 소원을 구할 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1900089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人が死んでもその願いは残る。<line-break>そしてその願いが<line-break>僕という存在を産み出したんだ。　<line-break>光も闇も飲み込む絶対負の存在、<line-break>虚無の力を借りてね。<end>"
EN: "Even after a person dies, the wish remains. And that wish brought me into being through the power of the Void, the absolute negative that consumes both light and darkness.<end>"
KO: "사람이 죽어도 그 소원은 남는다. 그리고 그 소원이 나라는 존재를 낳았어. 빛도 어둠도 삼키는 절대적인 부의 존재, 허무의 힘을 빌려서 말이지.<end>"

## 1900090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>君もその願いのひとつなんだよ。　<line-break>　<line-break>そう、いつだって人は願っている。<line-break>世界に混沌が訪れたとき、世界を<line-break>守ってくれる勇者が現れるのをね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you too are one of those wishes. Yes, people are always wishing for the same thing: when chaos descends upon the world, a hero will appear to protect it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너도 그 소원 중 하나야. 그래, 사람은 언제나 바라지. 세계에 혼돈이 찾아왔을 때 세계를 지켜 줄 용사가 나타나기를.<end>"

## 1900091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この扉の向こうには、<line-break>世界の破滅を望む願いが待っている。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、君は、世界を守る願いだ。<line-break>どちらの願いが強いのかな？<line-break>結果を見られないのが、残念だよ。<end>"
EN: "Beyond this door waits a wish for the destruction of the world. And you are a wish to protect it. I wonder which wish is stronger. It's a shame I won't see the outcome.<end>"
KO: "이 문 너머에는 세계의 파멸을 바라는 소원이 기다리고 있어. 그리고 너는 세계를 지키려는 소원이지. 어느 쪽의 소원이 더 강할까? 결과를 보지 못하는 게 아쉽네.<end>"

## 1900092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Bye, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1900093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の門の島に向かえば、<line-break>もうあとに引き返すことは<line-break>ままならないでしょう。<line-break>充分に力をつけてから<line-break>向かってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Once you leave for the Island of the Dark Gate, you may not be able to turn back. Make sure you're strong enough before you go.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 문 섬으로 향하면 이제 되돌아오기가 쉽지 않을 겁니다. 충분히 힘을 기른 뒤에 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1900094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔の仲間の気配があるんだよな。<line-break>エンシャントから漂ってくる。<line-break>気をつけた方がいいぜ。<line-break>あそこで何者かが<line-break>なんかやろうとしてやがる。<line-break>ま、ただの俺のカンだけどよ。<end>"
EN: "I can sense the presence of some old comrades drifting in from Ancient. You'd better be careful. Someone over there is up to something. Well, that's just my gut talking.<end>"
KO: "옛 동료의 기척이 느껴져. 엔샨트 쪽에서 흘러오고 있어. 조심하는 게 좋겠어. 거기서 누군가 뭔가 꾸미고 있는 것 같아. 뭐, 그냥 내 감이지만.<end>"

## 1900095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "青竜将軍代理であります。<line-break>敵が攻めてきたらどうしようって<line-break>非常に心配であります。<end>"
EN: "I am the acting Azure Dragon General! I am extremely worried about what might happen if the enemy attacks!<end>"
KO: "청룡장군 대리입니다. 적이 쳐들어오면 어쩌나 몹시 걱정됩니다.<end>"

## 1900096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヒマだよ～。<line-break>役に立つから、早く誘ってね。<end>"
EN: "I'm so bored! I'll be useful, so hurry up and invite me along.<end>"
KO: "심심해~. 도움 될 테니까 빨리 불러 줘.<end>"

## 1900097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく、カルラ様ったら…。　<line-break>将軍の仕事ほっぽりだして、<line-break>冒険しにいくだなんて…。<line-break>いくらネメア様を捜すためでも<line-break>まずいと思うんだけど。<line-break>はああぁぁ。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, Lady Karula... Abandoning her duties as general to go adventuring... Even if it is to find Lord Nemea, I don't think she should be doing this. Haaaaah...<end>"
KO: "정말 카를라 님도…. 장군 일을 내팽개치고 모험하러 가다니…. 아무리 네메아 님을 찾기 위해서라도 좋지 않은 것 같은데. 하아아….<end>"

## 1900098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退屈なんだけどさ。<line-break>あたしも連れてってくんない？<end>"
EN: "I'm bored. Why don't you take me with you?<end>"
KO: "심심한데 말이야. 나도 데려가 주면 안 돼?<end>"

## 1900099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様！　将軍の仕事は！？<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula! What about your duties as general?!<end>"
KO: "카를라 님! 장군 업무는요!?<end>"

## 1900100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かに任せちゃうからいいの。<line-break>で、どうなの、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "I'll just leave them to someone else. So, what do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "누군가에게 맡기면 되니까 괜찮아. 그래서 어때, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1900101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、残念。今は、いっぱいか。<line-break>そんじゃ、必要になったら呼んでね。<line-break>力になるわよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, too bad. Your party's full right now, is it? Well, call me when you need me. I'll be glad to help.<end>"
KO: "어머, 아쉽네. 지금은 가득 찼구나. 그럼 필요해지면 불러 줘. 힘이 되어 줄게.<end>"

## 1900102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。じゃ、がんばっていこー！<end>"
EN: "All right! Let's give it our best!<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 힘내서 가자!<end>"

## 1900103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんですって！<line-break>ネメア様が、次元のはざまに！？<end>"
EN: "W-What?! Lord Nemea is in the Interdimensional Rift?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고요! 네메아 님이 차원의 틈새에!?<end>"

## 1900105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、闇の門に行くわけね。<line-break>なんかやばそうなところじゃない？<line-break>でも、おもしろそうね。<line-break>よかったら、力貸すけど、<line-break>どう？<end>"
EN: "Hmm, so you're going to the Dark Gate. Sounds like a dangerous place, doesn't it? But it also sounds interesting. I could lend you a hand, if you like. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "흐음, 어둠의 문으로 가는 거구나. 뭔가 위험해 보이는 곳 아니야? 그래도 재미있겠네. 괜찮으면 힘 빌려줄까?<end>"

## 1900106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カ、カルラ様！<line-break>軍はどうするんですか！？<end>"
EN: "L-Lady Karula! What about the army?!<end>"
KO: "카, 카를라 님! 군은 어떻게 하실 겁니까!?<end>"

## 1900107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンがいるじゃない。<line-break>あとは、よろしくね。<line-break>ってわけで、パーティーに空きある？<end>"
EN: "Irene's here, isn't she? I'll leave the rest to you. So, have you got room in your party?<end>"
KO: "아이린이 있잖아. 나머진 잘 부탁해. 그래서 말인데, 파티에 자리 있어?<end>"

## 1900108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、残念。今は、いっぱいか。<line-break>そんじゃ、必要になったら呼んでね。<line-break>力になるわよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, too bad. Your party's full right now, is it? Well, call me when you need me. I'll be glad to help.<end>"
KO: "어머, 아쉽네. 지금은 가득 찼구나. 그럼 필요해지면 불러 줘. 힘이 되어 줄게.<end>"

## 1900109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。じゃ、がんばっていこー！<end>"
EN: "All right! Let's give it our best!<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 힘내서 가자!<end>"

## 1900110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助ける方法はこれから探すの？<line-break>そう…、なんだったらアイリーン使う？<line-break>あたしは何か探すのって苦手でね。<end>"
EN: "You still have to find a way to save him? Hmm... If you like, why not use Irene? I'm no good at searching for things.<end>"
KO: "도울 방법은 이제부터 찾는 거야? 그래… 뭐하면 아이린을 써 볼래? 난 뭔가 찾는 건 서툴러서 말이야.<end>"

## 1900111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…！　カ、カルラ様？<end>"
EN: "What...?! L-Lady Karula?<end>"
KO: "어…! 카, 카를라 님?<end>"

## 1900112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともかくさ、<line-break>ネメア様帰ってこないんじゃ、　　　<line-break>あたしたちは動けないのよ。<line-break>だから、探してきてよ。<line-break>ってことで、パーティーに空きある？<end>"
EN: "Anyway, we can't make a move until Lord Nemea comes back. So go find him. Now, have you got room in your party?<end>"
KO: "아무튼 네메아 님이 돌아오지 않으면 우리는 움직일 수 없어. 그러니까 찾아와 줘. 그래서 말인데, 파티에 자리 있어?<end>"

## 1900113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、残念。今は、いっぱいか。<line-break>そんじゃ、必要になったら<line-break>呼んであげて。すぐに行かせるからさ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, too bad. Your party's full right now, is it? Well, call for her when you need her. I'll send her right over.<end>"
KO: "아, 아쉽네. 지금은 자리가 꽉 찼나. 그럼 필요해지면 불러 줘. 바로 보내 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 1900114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。じゃ、<line-break>がんばっていってらっしゃい！<end>"
EN: "All right, then. Good luck out there!<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 힘내서 다녀와!<end>"

## 1900115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様！<end>"
EN: "Lord Nemea!<end>"
KO: "네메아 님!<end>"

## 1900116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様！　ご無事でしたか！<end>"
EN: "Lord Nemea! Are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "네메아 님! 무사하셨습니까!<end>"

## 1900117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらぬ心配をかけた。許せ。<end>"
EN: "I caused you needless worry. Forgive me.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 걱정을 끼쳤군. 용서해라.<end>"

## 1900118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、生きていてさえくだされば。<end>"
EN: "No. All that matters is that you're alive.<end>"
KO: "아니요, 살아 계시기만 한다면.<end>"

## 1900119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私と<value:$28>たちは<line-break>敵の首魁を叩く。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お前たちはここを死守せよ。<line-break>決して敵を外に出してはならん。<end>"
EN: "I will strike at the enemy leader with <value:$28> and the others. You are to hold this position at all costs. Do not let any of the enemy get out.<end>"
KO: "나와 <value:$28> 일행은 적의 우두머리를 친다. 너희는 이곳을 사수하라. 절대로 적을 밖으로 내보내선 안 된다.<end>"

## 1900120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。<line-break>死守いたします。<end>"
EN: "Understood. We will hold this position at all costs.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 목숨 걸고 지키겠습니다.<end>"

## 1900121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1900122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傷付いたら戻るがいい。<end>"
EN: "Come back if you're wounded.<end>"
KO: "다치면 돌아오거라.<end>"

## 1900123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここはお任せください。<end>"
EN: "Leave this place to us.<end>"
KO: "여기는 맡겨주십시오.<end>"

## 1900124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとしてもエンシャントを<line-break>取り戻してください！<end>"
EN: "Please, you must reclaim Ancient at any cost!<end>"
KO: "어떻게 해서든 엔샨트를 되찾아 주세요!<end>"

## 1900125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫だった、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "괜찮았어, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1900126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理はしないのよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't push yourself too hard.<end>"
KO: "무리하지 마.<end>"

## 1900127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
