## 1900128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傷付いたら戻るがいい。<end>"
EN: "Come back if you're wounded.<end>"
KO: "다치면 돌아오거라.<end>"

## 1900129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここはお任せください。<end>"
EN: "Leave this place to us.<end>"
KO: "여기는 맡겨주십시오.<end>"

## 1900130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとしてもエンシャントを<line-break>取り戻してください！<end>"
EN: "Please, you must reclaim Ancient at any cost!<end>"
KO: "어떻게 해서든 엔샨트를 되찾아 주세요!<end>"

## 1900131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、必ずティアナに<line-break>会いにいらしてください。　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様がそばにいて<line-break>くだされば、ティアナはもっと<line-break>がんばれますから。<end>"
EN: "Please be sure to come and see Tiana again. When <value:$28> is by her side, Tiana can try even harder.<end>"
KO: "또 꼭 티아나를 만나러 와주세요. <value:$28>님이 곁에 계시면 티아나는 더 힘낼 수 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1900132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また会いにいらしてください。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様とお話しする<line-break>ことができれば、ティアナは<line-break>それだけで安心できるのです。<end>"
EN: "Please come and see Tiana again. Just being able to speak with <value:$28> is enough to put Tiana's mind at ease.<end>"
KO: "또 만나러 와 주세요. <value:$28>님과 이야기할 수 있으면 티아나는 그것만으로 안심할 수 있어요.<end>"

## 1900134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様！<line-break>ティアナ様が、いつになっても　<line-break>お戻りになられません。<line-break>どこにも見あたらないのです！<line-break>あああ…、いったいどこに<line-break>行かれてしまわれたのでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Lady Tiana still hasn't returned. She's nowhere to be found! Oh... Where could she possibly have gone?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님! 티아나 님이 아무리 기다려도 돌아오지 않으십니다. 어디에도 보이지 않아요! 아아아… 대체 어디로 가신 걸까요.<end>"

## 1900135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様は少し前から<line-break>すっかりお変わりになられて…　<line-break>　<line-break>地に足がついてらっしゃらない<line-break>というか…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana has been completely different for some time now... It's as though she no longer has her feet firmly on the ground.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님은 조금 전부터 완전히 달라지셔서… 땅에 발이 붙어 있지 않은 듯하다고 할까….<end>"

## 1900136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　心当たりですか？<line-break>…そう言えば、最近はよく、東方から<line-break>来られた博士とお会いになっていました。<line-break>たしか、シャリ様とおっしゃったと<line-break>思いますけど…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What? Do I have any idea where she might be? Come to think of it, lately she often met with a scholar from the East. I believe his name was Lord Shalli...<end>"
KO: "네? 짐작 가는 사람이 있냐고요? …그러고 보니 최근 동방에서 오신 박사님을 자주 만나셨어요. 아마 샤리 님이라고 했던 것 같은데…<end>"

## 1900137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それと…、ティアナ様のお姿が<line-break>見られなくなった日の前日、レムオン様が<line-break>訪ねてらっしゃっていました。<line-break>他には…、申しわけありません。<line-break>それ以外に思い当たる<line-break>ことはありません。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and one more thing... Lord Lemghon came to see Lady Tiana the day before she disappeared. Other than that... I'm sorry. Nothing else comes to mind.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고… 티아나 님이 보이지 않게 되기 전날 레무온 님께서 찾아오셨습니다. 그 밖에는… 죄송합니다. 달리 생각나는 것은 없습니다.<end>"

## 1900138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1900139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うれしい…。<line-break>また、ティアナに会いに来て<line-break>くださったのですね。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad... You came to see Tiana again.<end>"
KO: "기뻐요…. 또 티아나를 만나러 와 주셨군요.<end>"

## 1900140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>アトレイア様はまだ戻られません。　　<line-break>捜索もはかどっていないようです。<line-break>レムオン様もいなくなってしまわれて、<line-break>ティアナが知っている人がふたりも<line-break>急に姿を消してしまいました。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Lady Atleia still hasn't returned. The search doesn't seem to be making much progress, either. Now Lord Lemghon is gone as well. Two people Tiana knows have suddenly disappeared.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 아트레이아 님은 아직 돌아오지 않으셨어요. 수색도 진척이 없는 모양입니다. 레무온 님까지 사라져서 티아나가 아는 두 사람이 갑자기 모습을 감춰 버렸어요.<end>"

## 1900141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>ティアナは不安なのです。　<line-break>　<line-break>今度は<value:$28>様が<line-break>急にいなくなって<line-break>しまうのではないかと…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Tiana is afraid. What if <value:$28> suddenly disappears next...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 티아나는 불안해요. 이번엔 <value:$28>님이 갑자기 사라져 버리는 건 아닐까 하고….<end>"

## 1900142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お願いがあります。<line-break>ティアナと約束してください。<line-break><value:$28>様は<line-break>ティアナに何も告げずに<line-break>いなくなったりはしないと。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Tiana has a favor to ask. Please promise Tiana that <value:$28> will never disappear without saying a word.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 부탁이 있습니다. 티아나와 약속해 주세요. <value:$28> 님은 티아나에게 아무 말도 없이 사라지지 않겠다고.<end>"

## 1900143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何があっても必ず、<line-break>ティアナの元へ戻ってくると…。<end>"
EN: "Promise that, no matter what happens, you will always come back to Tiana...<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이 있어도 반드시 티아나에게 돌아오겠다고….<end>"

## 1900144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必ず戻ると約束する<end>"
EN: "Promise to return without fail<end>"
KO: "반드시 돌아오겠다고 약속한다<end>"

## 1900145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できるだけそうすると言う<end>"
EN: "Say you'll do your best to return<end>"
KO: "가능한 한 그렇게 하겠다고 말한다<end>"

## 1900146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break><value:$28>様が約束して<line-break>くださるのですもの、私も信じます。<line-break>本当はティアナは、<line-break><value:$28>様には<line-break>もう、どこにも行かないでほしい…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. You gave me your word, so I believe you. The truth is, <value:$28>, Tiana doesn't want you to go away ever again...<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. <value:$28> 님이 약속해 주셨으니 저도 믿을게요. 사실 티아나는 <value:$28> 님이 이제 어디에도 가지 않았으면 해요….<end>"

## 1900147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナのそばにずっと<line-break>いてほしいのです。そういう<line-break>わがままな女なのです、ティアナは。<line-break>ですから、せめて今日の約束だけは<line-break>忘れないでください。必ずティアナの<line-break>元へ戻ってくると。<end>"
EN: "I want you to stay by Tiana's side forever. That is the selfish sort of woman Tiana is. So please, at least remember the promise you made today: that you will come back to Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나는 계속 곁에 있어 주셨으면 해요. 그런 제멋대로인 여자랍니다, 티아나는. 그러니 적어도 오늘의 약속만은 잊지 말아 주세요. 반드시 티아나에게 돌아오겠다는 약속을요.<end>"

## 1900148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの帰りを待っている私が<line-break>ここにいるということを…。<end>"
EN: "Please remember that I am here, waiting for you to return...<end>"
KO: "당신이 돌아오기를 기다리는 내가 여기 있다는 걸….<end>"

## 1900149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様は<line-break>意地悪な方ですね。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>こういうときは、ウソでも必ず戻ると<line-break>言ってくだされば、<line-break>ティアナも安心できますのに…。<end>"
EN: "You can be so unkind, <value:$28>. At a time like this, you could tell me you'll definitely come back, even if it isn't true. Then Tiana could rest easy...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님은 심술궂은 분이시네요. 이런 때는 거짓말이라도 반드시 돌아온다고 해 주시면 티아나도 안심할 텐데…<end>"

## 1900150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様は冒険者ですもの。<line-break>必ず戻ってこれるという保証はない　　<line-break>ということはわかっています。<line-break>でも、<value:$28>様。<line-break>忘れないでください。あなたの帰りを<line-break>待っている私がここにいることを。<end>"
EN: "You are an adventurer, <value:$28>. I know there can be no guarantee that you will return. But please, <value:$28>, don't forget that I am here, waiting for you to come back.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님은 모험자시니까요. 반드시 돌아오실 수 있다는 보장은 없다는 건 알고 있어요. 하지만 <value:$28> 님. 잊지 말아 주세요. 당신이 돌아오기를 기다리는 제가 여기 있다는 걸.<end>"

## 1900151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、ティアナは最近<line-break>ロストールの政治のことを、<line-break>少しずつ勉強しているのです。<line-break>それで、気づいたことがあります。<line-break>それは、お母様がこの国を<line-break>愛してらっしゃるということです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Tiana has been studying Rostorl's politics lately, a little at a time. And I've realized something: Mother loves this country.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 티아나는 요즘 로스톨의 정치에 대해 조금씩 공부하고 있어요. 그러다 깨달은 게 있습니다. 어머님께서 이 나라를 사랑하고 계신다는 거예요.<end>"

## 1900152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、ロストールの国民すべてが　<line-break>満足のいくような生活を送れる<line-break>レベルには至っていません。<line-break>でも、貴族との微妙な関係が続く中で<line-break>お母様は並々ならない努力を<line-break>されてきたようです。<end>"
EN: "It is true that Rostorl is not yet at a point where all its citizens can lead lives they are satisfied with. But while relations with the nobility remain delicate, Mother seems to have made extraordinary efforts.<end>"
KO: "확실히 로스톨의 모든 국민이 만족스러운 생활을 누릴 수준에는 이르지 못했습니다. 하지만 귀족들과 미묘한 관계가 계속되는 가운데 어머님은 남다른 노력을 해 오신 것 같아요.<end>"

## 1900153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、ティアナも<line-break>お母様のように、国のために　　<line-break>がんばってみようと思います。<line-break>ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ティアナのことを、<line-break>応援してくださいますか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Tiana is going to try to do her part for the country too, just like Mother. So, <value:$28>, will you support Tiana?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 티아나도 어머님처럼 나라를 위해 힘내 보려고 해요. 그러니 <value:$28> 님. 티아나를 응원해 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 1900154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナはひとりでも大丈夫<end>"
EN: "Tiana will be fine on her own.<end>"
KO: "티아나는 혼자서도 괜찮아<end>"

## 1900155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けが必要なら言ってほしい<end>"
EN: "Please let me know if help is needed.<end>"
KO: "도움이 필요하면 말해 줬으면 해<end>"

## 1900156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様は、ティアナを<line-break>過大評価されています。ティアナは　　<line-break>そんなに強くはありません。<line-break>…今のティアナではムリだと思います。<line-break>ティアナは、<value:$28>様を<line-break>すぐに頼ってしまいますもの。<end>"
EN: "You think too highly of Tiana, <value:$28>. Tiana isn't that strong. As she is now, I don't think she can do it. She's far too quick to rely on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님은 티아나를 너무 높게 평가하세요. 티아나는 그렇게 강하지 않아요. …지금의 티아나로는 무리라고 생각해요. 티아나는 금방 <value:$28> 님께 의지해 버리니까요.<end>"

## 1900157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>これからもこうして、<line-break>ティアナを訪ねてらしてください。<line-break><value:$28>様がそばにいて<line-break>くだされば、ティアナはもっと<line-break>がんばれますから。<end>"
EN: "So please, <value:$28>, keep coming to visit Tiana like this. With you by her side, <value:$28>, she can try even harder.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28> 님. 앞으로도 이렇게 티아나를 찾아와 주세요. <value:$28> 님이 곁에 계시면 티아나는 더 힘낼 수 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1900158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>私、こうして<value:$28>様に<line-break>助けてもらえれば、お母様のように<line-break>がんばれると思うのです。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. With your help like this, <value:$28>, I think I can follow Mother's example and do my best.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 저, 이렇게 <value:$28>님께 도움을 받으면 어머님처럼 힘낼 수 있을 것 같아요.<end>"

## 1900159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>これからも、こうしてティアナを<line-break>勇気づけてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "So please, <value:$28>, keep encouraging Tiana like this.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>님. 앞으로도 이렇게 티아나에게 용기를 주세요.<end>"

## 1900160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>私に会いに来てくださったのですね。<line-break>うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You came to see me. I'm so glad.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 저를 만나러 와 주셨군요. 기뻐요.<end>"

## 1900161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国軍が去り、<line-break>ロストールの危機は、<line-break>ひとまず避けられました。　　　　　　<line-break>ですが、人々は不安に包まれています。<line-break>いつ、ディンガル帝国軍がまた<line-break>進軍してくるかわかりません。<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Imperial Army has withdrawn, so the immediate danger to Rostorl has passed. But the people are still gripped by fear. No one knows when the Dyneskal Imperial Army might march on us again.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국군이 떠나 로스톨의 위기는 일단 피했습니다. 하지만 사람들은 불안에 휩싸여 있습니다. 딘갈 제국군이 언제 다시 진군할지 모르니까요.<end>"

## 1900162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、いざというときに、<line-break>王家と貴族が協力しあえなかった　　　<line-break>という経緯があります。<line-break>今度、同じような事態が起きたとき<line-break>果たして、誰を頼っていけばいいのか…<line-break>町の人々は不安だと思うのです。<end>"
EN: "And when the crisis came, the royal house and the nobility failed to work together. If the same thing happens again, who can the townspeople turn to? I think that uncertainty is weighing heavily on them.<end>"
KO: "그리고 정작 위기가 닥쳤을 때 왕가와 귀족이 협력하지 못했던 일도 있습니다. 다음에 같은 일이 생기면 대체 누구를 의지해야 하는지… 마을 사람들은 불안할 겁니다.<end>"

## 1900163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>私にできるのでしょうか？<line-break>彼らの不安を取り除くことが…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, can I really do it? Can I take away their fears?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 제가 할 수 있을까요? 그들의 불안을 없애는 일을….<end>"

## 1900164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナならできると言う<end>"
EN: "Say Tiana can do it<end>"
KO: "티아나라면 할 수 있다고 말한다<end>"

## 1900165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの王女ならできると言う<end>"
EN: "Say the Princess of Rostorl can do it<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 왕녀라면 할 수 있다고 한다<end>"

## 1900167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当は<value:$28>様がいつも<line-break>そばにいらしてくだされば、ティアナは<line-break>もっとがんばれるのですけれど、<line-break>それは、ティアナのわがままと<line-break>いうものですね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "If only you could always be by my side, <value:$28>, Tiana could try even harder. But that would be selfish of Tiana, wouldn't it?<end>"
KO: "사실은 <value:$28> 님이 늘 곁에 계셔 주시면 티아나는 더 힘낼 수 있지만, 그건 티아나의 제멋대로인 바람이겠지요.<end>"

## 1900168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>…また、こうしてティアナに<line-break>会いに来てくださいね。<end>"
EN: "So, <value:$28>… please come and see Tiana again.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>님. …또 이렇게 티아나를 만나러 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1900169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですよね。…ティアナは<line-break>ロストールの王女ですもの。<line-break>王女として、がんばらねばなりません。<line-break>でも、<value:$28>様。<line-break>王女である前に、私はひとりの<line-break>女の子なのです。<end>"
EN: "You're right. Tiana is the princess of Rostorl, after all. I must do my best as a princess. But <value:$28>, before I am a princess, I am still just a girl.<end>"
KO: "그렇죠. …티아나는 로스톨의 왕녀니까요. 왕녀로서 열심히 해야 해요. 하지만 <value:$28> 님. 왕녀이기 전에 저는 한 명의 여자아이예요.<end>"

## 1900170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>これからも、ティアナのことを<line-break>見守っていてください。<end>"
EN: "So, <value:$28>, please continue to watch over Tiana.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>님. 앞으로도 티아나를 지켜봐 주세요.<end>"

## 1900171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>レムオン様もアトレイア様も　　　　　<line-break>まだ戻られません。<line-break>おふたりは一緒にいるのでしょうか。<line-break>レムオン様はともかく、アトレイア様は<line-break>これまで、お城の外に出られたことが<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, neither Lemghon nor Atleia has returned yet. Do you suppose they are together? Lemghon is one thing, but Atleia has hardly ever been outside the castle,<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 레무온 님도 아트레이아 님도 아직 돌아오지 않으셨습니다. 두 분은 함께 계신 걸까요. 레무온 님은 그렇다 쳐도 아트레이아 님은 지금까지 성 밖으로 나가신 적이<end>"

## 1900172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほとんどないはずですから、<line-break>心配なのです。早く、お戻りに<line-break>なられるとよいのですが…<end>"
EN: "so I cannot help worrying. I only hope they return soon...<end>"
KO: "거의 없을 테니 걱정됩니다. 빨리 돌아오시면 좋을 텐데….<end>"

## 1900173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配しすぎだと言う<end>"
EN: "Say there's no need to worry so much<end>"
KO: "걱정이 지나치다고 말한다<end>"

## 1900174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分も捜索に協力すると言う<end>"
EN: "Offer to help with the search too<end>"
KO: "자신도 수색에 협력하겠다고 한다<end>"

## 1900175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうでしょうか。　　　　　　　<line-break>私、不安なのです。<line-break>　<line-break>これまで、おふたりとも私の近くに<line-break>おられたのに、急にいなくなって<line-break>しまわれたのですもの。<end>"
EN: "…Do you really think so? I can't help feeling anxious. You'd both always been close to me, and then suddenly, one of you was gone.<end>"
KO: "…그럴까요. 저는 불안해요. 지금까지 두 분 모두 제 곁에 계셨는데 갑자기 사라져 버리셨으니까요.<end>"

## 1900176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、約束して<line-break>ください。<value:$28>様は<line-break>急にいなくなったりはしないと。　<line-break>私に何も言わずに、どこかへ行って<line-break>しまわれたりはしないと。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please promise me. Promise me you won't suddenly disappear, <value:$28>, or go off somewhere without saying a word to me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 약속해 주세요. <value:$28> 님은 갑자기 사라지지 않는다고. 제게 아무 말도 없이 어디론가 가 버리지 않겠다고.<end>"

## 1900177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言っていただけると<line-break>助かります。でも、危険なことは　<line-break>なさらないでくださいね。<line-break>私、嫌な予感がするのです。<line-break>おふたりが、何かとてつもないことに<line-break>巻き込まれているような予感が…。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad to hear you say that. But please, don't do anything dangerous. I have a terrible feeling that the two of you are caught up in something far beyond anything we can imagine…<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말씀해 주시면 도움이 됩니다. 하지만 위험한 일은 하지 마세요. 저, 불길한 예감이 들어요. 두 분이 뭔가 엄청난 일에 휘말리고 있는 듯한 예감이….<end>"

## 1900178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お願いです。<value:$28>様は<line-break>必ず帰ってきてください。<line-break>ティアナに何も言わずに<line-break>いなくなったりは、決して<line-break>なさらないでくださいね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please. You must come back, <value:$28>. And whatever happens, don't disappear without saying a word to Tiana.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 부탁이에요. <value:$28> 님은 반드시 돌아와 주세요. 티아나에게 아무 말도 없이 사라지거나 하시면 절대 안 돼요.<end>"

## 1900179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様はご存じですか？<line-break>リベルダムやロセンでは、町を追われた<line-break>人たちが難民となっているそうです。<line-break>ですから私、お母様にお願いしに<line-break>あがろうと思うのです。両方の町に<line-break>支援を送れないかと。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, have you heard? They say that people driven out of Liberdam and Rocen have become refugees. So I intend to ask Mother whether we can send aid to both cities.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님은 알고 계신가요? 리벨덤과 로센에서는 도시에서 쫓겨난 사람들이 난민이 되었다고 해요. 그래서 저는 어머님께 부탁드리러 가려고 합니다. 두 도시 모두에 지원을 보낼 수 없겠느냐고요.<end>"

## 1900180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールもそうなる可能性が<line-break>あったのです。放っておくことは　<line-break>できませんもの。<line-break>実は、ある方から聞いたのですが、<line-break>解放軍と名乗る方たちが、密かに<line-break>支援を行っているそうなのです。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl could have suffered the same fate. I simply cannot ignore what has happened. In fact, I heard from someone that a group calling itself the Liberation Army has been providing aid in secret.<end>"
KO: "로스톨도 그렇게 될 가능성이 있었습니다. 그냥 둘 수는 없잖아요. 사실 어떤 분께 들었는데, 해방군이라는 사람들이 몰래 지원을 하고 있다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1900181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その方たちと協力するば、きっと<line-break>うまくいくと思うのです。<line-break>どうでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "I believe it will succeed if we work with them. What do you think?<end>"
KO: "그분들과 협력한다면 분명 잘될 거라고 생각해요. 어떠신가요?<end>"

## 1900182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはよい考えだと同意する<end>"
EN: "Agree that it's a good idea<end>"
KO: "좋은 생각이라고 동의한다<end>"

## 1900183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス王妃が承知するだろうか…<end>"
EN: "Will Queen Eris approve…?<end>"
KO: "에리스 왕비가 받아들일까….<end>"

## 1900184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様ならそう言って<line-break>いただけると思っていました。　　　　<line-break>相談してみてよかったです。<line-break>ロストールも戦争が終わったばかりで<line-break>財政的にも苦しい状況と聞いています。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I thought you might say that, <value:$28>. I'm glad I asked your advice. I hear Rostorl is also struggling financially, with the war only just over.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이라면 그렇게 말씀해 주실 줄 알았습니다. 상담해 보길 잘했어요. 로스톨도 전쟁이 끝난 지 얼마 되지 않아 재정적으로 어려운 상황이라고 들었습니다.<end>"

## 1900185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、だからといって困っている　　　<line-break>人たちを放っておくわけには<line-break>いきませんもの。<line-break>それでは、さっそく<line-break>お母様にお願いしてこようと思います。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "But that doesn't mean we can simply abandon those in need. I'll go and appeal to Mother at once.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇다고 곤란한 사람들을 내버려 둘 수는 없잖아요. 그럼 바로 어머님께 부탁드리러 가겠습니다.<end>"

## 1900186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、また今度<line-break>ティアナの相談を聞いてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Please let Tiana come to you for advice again sometime, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 다음에도 티아나의 상담을 들어 주세요.<end>"

## 1900187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…実はティアナも同じ事を　　　　　　<line-break>考えていました。<line-break>　<line-break>戦争が終わったばかりでロストールの<line-break>財政は厳しいと聞いています。お母様を<line-break>説得するのは難しいかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "To tell the truth, Tiana was thinking the same thing. I hear Rostorl's finances are strained, with the war only just over. It may be difficult to persuade Mother.<end>"
KO: "…사실 티아나도 같은 생각을 하고 있었습니다. 전쟁이 끝난 지 얼마 되지 않아 로스톨의 재정이 어렵다고 들었습니다. 어머님을 설득하기는 힘들지도 몰라요.<end>"

## 1900188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、リベルダムやロセンの人たちを<line-break>放っておくことは、ティアナには<line-break>どうしてもできないのです。<line-break>…以前は女が政治に口出しするのは<line-break>よくないと思っていましたが、<line-break>ふふ、私もお母様の娘なのですね。<end>"
EN: "But Tiana simply cannot abandon the people of Liberdam and Rocen. I used to think women shouldn't meddle in politics, but... heh. I suppose I am my mother's daughter after all.<end>"
KO: "하지만 리벨덤이나 로센 사람들을 내버려 두는 건 티아나로선 도저히 못 해요. …예전엔 여자가 정치에 끼어드는 건 좋지 않다고 생각했는데, 후후, 저도 어머님의 딸인가 봐요.<end>"

## 1900189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様にお話ししたら<line-break>やる気が出てきました。私、これから<line-break>お母様を説得しに行こうと思います。<line-break><value:$28>様、また今度<line-break>ティアナの相談を聞いてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Talking to you has given me the resolve to act, <value:$28>. I'm going to persuade Mother now. Please let Tiana come to you for advice again sometime, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님께 말씀드리니 의욕이 생겼어요. 이제 어머니를 설득하러 가 보려고 해요. <value:$28> 님, 다음에도 티아나의 고민을 들어 주세요.<end>"

## 1900190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ、この戦い、<line-break>お前の力、借りるやもしれぬ。<line-break>心構えはしておけ。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv, I may have need of your strength in this battle. Be prepared.<end>"
KO: "자기브, 이번 싸움에서 네 힘을 빌릴지도 모른다. 각오는 해 둬라.<end>"

## 1900191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>こんなときになんですが、<line-break>あなたに訊きたいことがあったんです。<line-break>自分がいったい何者なのか、<line-break>何を為すべきなのか、<line-break>今のあなたなら答えられますか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I know this is hardly the time, but there's something I've been meaning to ask you. Who are you, and what should you do? Can you answer those questions now?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이런 때지만 당신에게 묻고 싶은 게 있었습니다. 자신이 대체 누구인지, 무엇을 해야 하는지, 지금의 당신이라면 답할 수 있습니까?<end>"

## 1900192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>決意が固まったのは喜ばしいことです。<line-break>　<line-break>出会った頃のあなたは、自分の過去と<line-break>未来のどちらにも不安を抱いている<line-break>頼りない印象でした。<line-break>でも、今のあなたにそんな面影は<line-break>どこにもありません。<end>"
EN: "I see. I'm glad you've made up your mind. When we first met, you seemed adrift, anxious about both your past and your future. There is no trace of that person in you now.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 결심이 선 것은 기쁜 일입니다. 처음 만났을 때의 당신은 자신의 과거와 미래 모두를 불안해하는 미덥지 못한 인상이었습니다. 하지만 지금의 당신에게 그런 모습은 조금도 남아 있지 않습니다.<end>"

## 1900193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがどんな経験をしてきたか、<line-break>私は推し量ることしかできません。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、何か大切なものを見つけたことは<line-break>確かなようですね。<end>"
EN: "I can only imagine what you have been through. But one thing seems certain: you have found something precious to you.<end>"
KO: "당신이 어떤 경험을 해왔는지는 제가 짐작할 수밖에 없습니다. 하지만 무언가 소중한 걸 찾은 건 분명해 보이는군요.<end>"

## 1900194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<line-break>たしかに急ぐ必要はありませんね。<line-break>　<line-break>すべてが終わってから、ゆっくり<line-break>考えてみるのもいいでしょう。<line-break>　<line-break>焦ることなく、じっくり探せば、<line-break>新しい道が見えてくるかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "You're right. There is no need to rush. You can take your time and think about it once everything is over. If you search patiently, you may discover a new path.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 확실히 서두를 필요는 없겠네요. 모든 일이 끝난 뒤 천천히 생각해 보는 것도 좋겠죠. 조급해하지 않고 차분히 찾다 보면 새로운 길이 보일지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1900195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何者かになろうとするその意志は<line-break>あなただけのものです。<line-break>　<line-break>決して他人から与えられたり、<line-break>運命などという抽象的なものから<line-break>授けられたりしたわけではありません。<line-break>その意志を大切にしてください。<end>"
EN: "The will to become someone belongs to you alone. It was not given to you by anyone else, nor bestowed upon you by something as abstract as fate. Cherish that will.<end>"
KO: "무언가가 되려는 그 의지는 오직 당신의 것입니다. 결코 다른 사람이 준 것도, 운명 같은 추상적인 것이 내려 준 것도 아닙니다. 그 의지를 소중히 하세요.<end>"

## 1900196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec190-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風雲急を告げるＶ７の２だ。<line-break>予備メッセージの７だな。<end>"

KO: "풍운이 급박한 V7의 2다. 예비 메시지 7이군.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風雲急を告げるＶ７の２だ。<line-break>予備メッセージの９だな。<end>"

## 1910000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "Ｖ７その３開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "V7 파트 3 시작<end>"

## 1910001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、会いにいらしてください。<line-break>私の不安を取り除けるのは<line-break><value:$28>様だけなのですから。<end>"
EN: "Please come and see me again. You're the only one who can put my fears to rest, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "또 만나러 와 주세요. 제 불안을 없애 줄 수 있는 분은 <value:$28>님뿐이니까요.<end>"

## 1910002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また会いにいらしてください。<line-break><value:$28>様とお話しできれば、<line-break>私の不安は解消されますから。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please come and see me again. Being able to talk to you would put my mind at ease.<end>"
KO: "또 만나러 와 주세요. <value:$28>님과 이야기하면 제 불안도 사라지니까요.<end>"

## 1910003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1910004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、私に会いに来て<line-break>くださったのですね。<line-break>…私、うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "You came to see me again. I'm so glad.<end>"
KO: "또 저를 만나러 와 주셨군요. …저, 기뻐요.<end>"

## 1910005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…実は私、あのときのメニューに<line-break>あったお料理を作ってみました。<line-break>お城の方に作り方を聞いてみたんです。<line-break>…まだ、あまり上手ではないのです<line-break>けれど、今度ちゃんと作れたら<line-break>…食べていただけますか？<end>"
EN: "Actually, I tried making one of the dishes that was on the menu that time. I asked at the castle how to make it. I'm still not very good at it, but if I manage to make it properly next time... would you try it?<end>"
KO: "…실은 저, 그때 메뉴에 있던 요리를 만들어 봤어요. 성의 분께 만드는 법을 물어봤거든요. …아직은 별로 잘하진 못하지만 다음에 제대로 만들 수 있게 되면… 드셔 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 1910006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん食べに来る<end>"
EN: "Of course I'll come by for a meal.<end>"
KO: "물론 먹으러 온다<end>"

## 1910007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時間があったら来られると思う<end>"
EN: "I think I can come if I have time.<end>"
KO: "시간이 있으면 올 수 있다고 말한다<end>"

## 1910008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当ですか？<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>…よかった。<end>"
EN: "Really? Thank you. I'm so glad.<end>"
KO: "정말인가요? 감사합니다. …다행이다.<end>"

## 1910009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お料理の作り方を　　<line-break>教えてくださった方が<line-break>言っておられましたから…。<line-break>お料理がうまくなるには<line-break>…好きな人に食べてもらうために<line-break>作るのが一番だって…。<end>"
EN: "The person who taught me to cook said the best way to get better at it is to cook for someone you care about...<end>"
KO: "…요리법을 가르쳐 주신 분이 말씀하셨거든요…. 요리를 잘하려면… 좋아하는 사람에게 먹여 주기 위해 만드는 게 제일이라고….<end>"

## 1910010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>よかったです。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私、<value:$28>様に<line-break>食べていただくために、<line-break>がんばってお料理覚えます。<end>"
EN: "I see. I'm glad. I'll do my best to learn how to cook, so I can make meals for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 다행이에요. 저, <value:$28>님께 드시게 하려고 열심히 요리를 배울게요.<end>"

## 1910011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、そのときは<line-break>なるべく食べにいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "So when I do, please come and eat whenever you can.<end>"
KO: "그러니 그때는 되도록 먹으러 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1910012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様、<line-break>この王宮の人たちも、外見では<line-break>以前と変わりありませんが、<line-break>心の中では不安なのですね。<line-break>…ティアナ様とレムオン様が<line-break>いなくなってしまいましたから。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, the people here at the palace look just the same as before, but underneath, they're anxious too, aren't they? Tiana and Lemghon are gone, after all.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님, 이 왕궁 사람들도 겉모습은 예전과 다르지 않지만 마음속으로는 불안한 거군요. …티아나 님과 레무온 님이 사라져 버렸으니까요.<end>"

## 1910013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の不安も…<line-break>消えることはありません。　　<line-break>　<line-break>あの深い闇の中に<line-break>また吸い込まれてしまうのでは<line-break>ないかという不安が…。<end>"
EN: "My own fear hasn't gone away, either. I'm still afraid that deep darkness will swallow me up again...<end>"
KO: "제 불안도… 사라지지 않습니다. 저 깊은 어둠 속으로 다시 빨려 들어가는 건 아닐까 하는 불안이….<end>"

## 1910014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分がついていると言う<end>"
EN: "Say, “I'm with you.”<end>"
KO: "자신이 함께하겠다고 말한다<end>"

## 1910015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本人が解決するしかないと言う<end>"
EN: "Say the person concerned will have to resolve it<end>"
KO: "본인이 해결할 수밖에 없다고 말한다<end>"

## 1910016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うれしいです。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様が一緒であれば<line-break>私、あの闇にうち勝つことが<line-break>できると思います。<end>"
EN: "…I'm glad. With you beside me, <value:$28>, I think I can overcome that darkness.<end>"
KO: "…기뻐요. <value:$28>님이 함께라면 저, 그 어둠을 이겨낼 수 있을 것 같아요.<end>"

## 1910018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私にできるでしょうか。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様がそばに<line-break>いてくださるなら、<line-break>できるのかもしれませんが…。<end>"
EN: "…Do you think I can? Perhaps I could, if you stayed by my side, <value:$28>…<end>"
KO: "…제가 할 수 있을까요. <value:$28>님이 곁에 있어 주신다면 할 수 있을지도 모르지만….<end>"

## 1910020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ティアナ様はまだ戻られない<line-break>そうですね。お城の方はみんな　　　　<line-break>心配してらっしゃいます。<line-break>でも、もし私がいなくなったら<line-break>こんなに心配してくれるのでしょうか？<line-break>ふと、そんなことを考えてしまいます。<end>"
EN: "…I hear Lady Tiana still hasn't returned. Everyone at the castle is worried about her. But if I disappeared, would anyone worry this much about me? Sometimes I can't help wondering.<end>"
KO: "…티아나 님은 아직 돌아오지 않으셨다고 들었습니다. 성 사람들은 모두 걱정하고 있어요. 하지만 만약 제가 사라진다면 이렇게까지 걱정해 줄까요? 문득 그런 생각을 하게 됩니다.<end>"

## 1910021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私には、ティアナ様のような華やかさが<line-break>ありませんから。誰も気づいてくれない<line-break>のではないかと不安になります。<end>"
EN: "I don't have Lady Tiana's radiance. I worry that no one would even notice.<end>"
KO: "저에게는 티아나 님 같은 화려함이 없으니까요. 아무도 알아봐 주지 않는 건 아닐까 불안해져요.<end>"

## 1910022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな心配すると言う<end>"
EN: "Say everyone will worry.<end>"
KO: "모두 걱정한다고 말한다<end>"

## 1910023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰よりも自分が心配すると言う<end>"
EN: "Say you'll worry more than anyone.<end>"
KO: "누구보다 자신이 걱정한다고 말한다<end>"

## 1910024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだといいのですが。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、私、信じていますから。<line-break>もし、私がいなくなっても、<line-break><value:$28>様なら…。<end>"
EN: "I hope so. But I do believe. Even if I were to disappear, <value:$28>, I know you would...<end>"
KO: "…그렇다면 좋겠지만요. 그래도 저는 믿고 있어요. 혹시 제가 없어져도 <value:$28>님이라면….<end>"

## 1910025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様がきっと<line-break>私を見つけてくださると<line-break>信じていますから。<end>"
EN: "I believe you would find me, <value:$28>. I know you would.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이 분명 저를 찾아 주실 거라 믿으니까요.<end>"

## 1910026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うれしいです。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>本当は私、お城の人が<line-break>気づいてくれなくてもいいのです。<line-break><value:$28>様が…。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad. The truth is, I don't mind if no one at the castle notices. As long as <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…기뻐요. 사실은 성 사람들이 알아주지 않아도 괜찮아요. <value:$28>님이….<end>"

## 1910027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が私のことを<line-break>心配してくださるのなら、<line-break>それで十分ですから。<end>"
EN: "As long as you're worried about me, <value:$28>, that's enough.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이 저를 걱정해 주신다면 그것만으로 충분해요.<end>"

## 1910028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>私に会いに来てくださったのですね。<line-break>…うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You came to see me. I'm so glad.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 저를 만나러 와 주셨군요. …기뻐요.<end>"

## 1910029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>ロストールは戦争を免れて<line-break>平和になったはずです。でも…　　<line-break>人々の心は不安にかられています。<line-break>…最近、私の心に人々の不安が<line-break>流れ込んでくるのです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Rostorl was spared the war and should be at peace now. And yet, people's hearts are gripped by anxiety. Lately, I can feel their anxiety flowing into my own heart.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 로스톨은 전쟁을 피해서 평화로워졌어야 해요. 하지만… 사람들의 마음은 불안에 사로잡혀 있어요. …요즘 사람들의 불안이 제 마음속으로 흘러들어와요.<end>"

## 1910030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、ずっと闇の中にいましたから。<line-break>だから、負の感情に敏感に<line-break>なっているのですね。<end>"
EN: "I spent so long in the darkness. That must be why I'm so sensitive to negative feelings.<end>"
KO: "저는 계속 어둠 속에 있었으니까요. 그래서 부정적인 감정에 민감해진 거겠죠.<end>"

## 1910031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私には何もできません。　　<line-break>私にできることは…<line-break>お祈りをするくらいです。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>この世界は、人々の不安に包まれて<line-break>どうなってしまうのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "But there's nothing I can do. All I can do is pray. <value:$28>, what will become of this world, surrounded by people's anxiety?<end>"
KO: "하지만 저는 아무것도 할 수 없습니다. 제가 할 수 있는 건… 기도하는 정도예요. <value:$28> 님, 이 세계는 사람들의 불안에 둘러싸여 어떻게 되는 걸까요?<end>"

## 1910032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイアは気にしなくていい<end>"
EN: "Atleia needn't worry about it.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아는 신경 쓰지 않아도 된다<end>"

## 1910033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイアにもできることはある<end>"
EN: "There are things Atleia can do too.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아에게도 할 수 있는 일은 있어<end>"

## 1910034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですよね。<line-break>私には何もできませんもの。<line-break>私はただ、ここで祈りを捧げるだけ…。<end>"
EN: "You're right. There's nothing I can do. All I can do is stay here and pray...<end>"
KO: "…그렇죠. 저는 아무것도 할 수 없으니까요. 저는 그저 여기서 기도를 올릴 뿐….<end>"

## 1910035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、何もしないよりは<line-break>ましかもしれません。ですから私、<line-break>お祈りは続けるつもりです。<line-break>人々の不安が<line-break>少しでも軽くなるようにと。<end>"
EN: "Still, perhaps that's better than doing nothing. So I intend to keep praying, in the hope that it might ease the people's fears, if only a little.<end>"
KO: "그래도 아무것도 하지 않는 것보다는 나을지도 몰라요. 그래서 저는 계속 기도할 생각입니다. 사람들의 불안이 조금이라도 가벼워지도록.<end>"

## 1910036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私にもできること？<line-break>あの、それはいったい<line-break>どんなことなのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Something I can do...? Um, what exactly do you mean?<end>"
KO: "…저에게도 할 수 있는 일? 그건 대체 어떤 일인가요?<end>"

## 1910037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不安にかられた人々を元気づける…<line-break>私がですか？　私にそのような<line-break>ことができるでしょうか？<line-break>私には、お話を聞いたり<line-break>歌をうたってあげることくらいしか<line-break>できませんが…。<end>"
EN: "Cheer up those who are overcome with fear...? Me? Do you really think I could? All I can do is listen to them or sing for them...<end>"
KO: "불안에 빠진 사람들을 격려하라고요… 제가요? 제가 그런 일을 할 수 있을까요? 저는 이야기를 들어 주거나 노래를 불러 주는 것 정도밖에 못 하는데….<end>"

## 1910038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですよね。そうすれば<line-break>私の周りの人たちだけでも、<line-break>勇気づけてあげられるかもしれません。<line-break>悩んでいるよりは、私にできることを<line-break>した方がいいですよね。<end>"
EN: "You're right. If I did that, perhaps I could at least give courage to the people around me. It's better to do what I can than simply sit here worrying.<end>"
KO: "…그렇네요. 그렇게 하면 적어도 제 주변 사람들만이라도 용기를 북돋아 줄 수 있을지도 몰라요. 고민만 하는 것보다 제가 할 수 있는 일을 하는 게 낫겠죠.<end>"

## 1910039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。王妃様にお願いして、<line-break>お城の外に行ってみます。少しでも<line-break>不安を取り除いてあげられるのなら…。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>私、がんばってみます。<end>"
EN: "All right. I'll ask the Queen for permission to leave the castle. If I can ease their fears, even a little... Thank you, <value:$28>. I'll do my best.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 왕비님께 부탁드려 성 밖으로 나가 보겠습니다. 조금이라도 불안을 덜어 드릴 수 있다면…. <value:$28> 님, 감사합니다. 저, 해 볼게요.<end>"

## 1910040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>ティアナ様はまだ見つからない　　<line-break>ようなのです。<line-break>レムオン様もどこかへ行って<line-break>しまわれて…。おふたりに間違いが<line-break>起こらなければいいのですが…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, it seems Tiana still hasn't been found. Lemghon has gone off somewhere as well... I only hope nothing has happened to either of them.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 티아나 님은 아직 찾지 못한 모양입니다. 레무온 님도 어디론가 가 버리셨고…. 두 분께 무슨 일이라도 생기지 않으면 좋겠는데….<end>"

## 1910041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのふたりなら大丈夫<end>"
EN: "Those two will be fine.<end>"
KO: "그 둘이라면 괜찮다<end>"

## 1910042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必ず探しだしてみせる<end>"
EN: "I'll find what I'm looking for, no matter what.<end>"
KO: "반드시 찾아내고 말겠다<end>"

## 1910043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだといいのですが…。<line-break>早くおふたりの元気な姿が<line-break>見られたらと思います。<end>"
EN: "I hope so... I hope it won't be long before I see both of you looking well.<end>"
KO: "그러면 좋겠네요…. 빨리 두 분의 건강한 모습을 볼 수 있으면 좋겠어요.<end>"

## 1910044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おふたりとも、この国の平和のために<line-break>必要な方ですから。<end>"
EN: "This country needs both of you if it is to know peace.<end>"
KO: "두 분 모두 이 나라의 평화를 위해 필요한 분들이니까요.<end>"

## 1910045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、はい。<line-break>私も<value:$28>様が<line-break>捜しだしてくださると信じています。<line-break><value:$28>様は私を闇から<line-break>救い出してくださいましたから。<line-break>ですから、今度もきっと…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. I also believe <value:$28> will succeed in the search. <value:$28> rescued me from the darkness, after all. So I'm sure this time, too...<end>"
KO: "아, 네. 저도 <value:$28> 님이 찾아 주실 거라 믿고 있어요. <value:$28> 님은 저를 어둠에서 구해 주셨으니까요. 그러니 이번에도 분명….<end>"

## 1910046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、ときどき不安になります。<line-break>…<value:$28>様が、遠くに<line-break>行ってしまわれるのではないかと。　<line-break>今、世界は不安に包まれています。<line-break>その世界の中を<value:$28>様は<line-break>駆け回っている…。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes I grow afraid... afraid that <value:$28> might go somewhere far away. The whole world is gripped by uncertainty, and <value:$28> is rushing from place to place through it all...<end>"
KO: "저는 가끔 불안해져요. …<value:$28> 님이 멀리 가 버리시는 건 아닐까 하고. 지금 세계는 불안에 휩싸여 있습니다. 그 세계 속을 <value:$28> 님은 뛰어다니고 계시고….<end>"

## 1910047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が、世界を覆う<line-break>不安に飲み込まれてしまうのでは<line-break>ないかと心配なのです。<end>"
EN: "I worry that <value:$28> may be swallowed up by the uncertainty engulfing the world.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이 세상을 뒤덮은 불안에 삼켜질까 걱정됩니다.<end>"

## 1910048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様は世界のために<line-break>がんばってらっしゃいます。でも、<line-break>本当は私、そばにいてほしいのです。<line-break>わがままですね、私は…。<line-break>でも、約束してほしいのです。<line-break>また会いに来てくださるって。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is working so hard for the sake of the world. But the truth is, I want you here with me. How selfish of me... Still, I want you to promise that you'll come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님은 세상을 위해 애쓰고 계십니다. 하지만 사실 저는 곁에 있어 주셨으면 해요. 제 욕심이겠지요…. 그래도 약속해 주세요. 다시 만나러 와 주시겠다고.<end>"

## 1910049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必ずまた会いに来る<end>"
EN: "I'll definitely come see you again.<end>"
KO: "반드시 다시 만나러 온다<end>"

## 1910050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たぶんまた会いにこられる<end>"
EN: "I'll probably be able to come see you again.<end>"
KO: "아마 다시 만나러 올 수 있을 거야<end>"

## 1910051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>そう言っていただけると、　　　<line-break>ほっとします。<line-break>ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>私がまた不安にならないうちに<line-break>会いに来てください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm relieved to hear you say that. So, <value:$28>, please come and see me again before I start to worry.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 그렇게 말씀해 주시니 안심돼요. 그러니 <value:$28> 님, 제가 다시 불안해지기 전에 만나러 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1910052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとうございます。<line-break>でも、必ず会いに来てくださるとは　　<line-break>言っていただけないのですね…。<line-break>あ、すみません。<value:$28>様は<line-break>お忙しい方ですから、私なんかが<line-break>ひきとめることなんてできませんよね。<end>"
EN: "…Thank you. But you can't promise that you'll come and see me, can you? Oh, I'm sorry. I know how busy you are, <value:$28>. Someone like me can't ask you to stay.<end>"
KO: "…감사합니다. 하지만 꼭 만나러 와 주시겠다고는 말씀해 주지 않으시는군요…. 아, 죄송해요. <value:$28>님은 바쁘신 분이니까 저 같은 사람이 붙잡을 수는 없겠죠.<end>"

## 1910053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私、<value:$28>様が<line-break>来てくださるのを待っていますから。<line-break>ですから、また会いに来てください。<end>"
EN: "But I'll be waiting for you, <value:$28>. So please come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "그래도 저는 <value:$28>님이 와 주시길 기다리고 있을게요. 그러니 또 만나러 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1910055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様！<line-break>アトレイア様が、いつになっても<line-break>お戻りになられません。<line-break>どこにも見あたらないのです！<line-break>あああ…、いったいどこに<line-break>行かれてしまわれたのでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Atleia still hasn't returned. She's nowhere to be found! Oh… Where in the world could she have gone?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님! 아트레이아 님이 아무리 기다려도 돌아오지 않으십니다. 어디에도 보이지 않아요! 아아아… 대체 어디로 가신 걸까요.<end>"

## 1910056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し前からアトレイア様は、すっかり<line-break>お元気をなくされてしまって…お部屋に　<line-break>閉じこもってらっしゃいましたから…。<end>"
EN: "For a while now, Atleia had been utterly dispirited… She'd shut herself away in her room.<end>"
KO: "조금 전부터 아트레이아 님은 완전히 기운을 잃고 방에 틀어박혀 계셨습니다….<end>"

## 1910057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　心当たりですか？<line-break>…そう言えば、このところ、東方から<line-break>来られた博士にお会いになっていました。<line-break>たしか、シャリ様とおっしゃったと<line-break>思いますけど…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What? Do I know anything that might help? Come to think of it, she'd recently been meeting with a scholar from the East. I believe his name was Shalli…<end>"
KO: "네? 짐작 가는 사람이 있냐고요? …그러고 보니 요즘 동방에서 오신 박사님을 만나셨어요. 아마 샤리 님이라고 했던 것 같은데…<end>"

## 1910058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それと…、アトレイア様のお姿が<line-break>見られなくなった日の前日、レムオン様が<line-break>訪ねてらっしゃっていました。<line-break>他には…、申しわけありません。<line-break>それ以外に思い当たる<line-break>ことはありません。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and one more thing… Lemghon came to see Atleia the day before she disappeared. Beyond that… I'm sorry. Nothing else comes to mind.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고… 아트레이아 님이 보이지 않게 된 전날 레무온 님께서 찾아오셨습니다. 다른 건… 죄송합니다. 그 외에는 짐작 가는 게 없습니다.<end>"

## 1910059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あああ…、いったいどこに<line-break>行かれてしまわれたのでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Oh… Where in the world could she have gone?<end>"
KO: "아아… 대체 어디로 가 버리신 걸까요.<end>"

## 1910060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君たちがここまで来たということは、<line-break>シャリはやられてしまったのか…。<end>"
EN: "If you've made it this far, then Shalli must have been defeated…<end>"
KO: "너희가 여기까지 왔다는 건 샤리가 당한 건가….<end>"

## 1910061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残るはお前だけだ、エルファス。<end>"
EN: "You're the only one left, Elfas.<end>"
KO: "남은 건 너뿐이다, 엘파스.<end>"

## 1910062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕だけ…？<line-break>フフフ。違うな、ネメア。<line-break>　<line-break>シャリがいいモノを<line-break>残してくれていてね。<end>"
EN: "Only me…? Heh heh. No, Nemea. Shalli left something rather useful behind.<end>"
KO: "나만…? 후후후. 아니야, 네메아. 샤리가 좋은 걸 남겨 줬거든.<end>"

## 1910063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고?<end>"

## 1910064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の王と、闇の王女さ。<line-break>この世に絶望し、<line-break>居場所を無くした魂の器だ。　　<line-break>ひとりは、ダルケニスであることを<line-break>政敵に知られ、<line-break>破滅をまぬがれえなくなり、<end>"
EN: "The King of Darkness and the Princess of Darkness. Vessels for souls that despaired of this world and lost any place in it. One was exposed as a Darkenith by political enemies and could no longer escape ruin.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 왕과 어둠의 왕녀지. 이 세상에 절망하고 있을 곳을 잃은 영혼의 그릇이다. 한 명은 다르케니스라는 사실을 정적에게 들켜 파멸을 피할 수 없게 되고,<end>"

## 1910065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとりは、何もかも与えられていたが<line-break>本当にほしいものは奪われ、<line-break>その絶望から闇に堕ちた。<end>"
EN: "One had been given everything, only to have the one thing truly desired taken away. That despair brought a fall into darkness.<end>"
KO: "한 사람은 모든 것을 받았지만 정말 원했던 것은 빼앗겼고, 그 절망으로 어둠에 떨어졌다.<end>"

## 1910066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとりは、長く闇に包まれていたが<line-break>ある日、光を与えられた。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、その光がもたらしたものも<line-break>また闇であることに絶望し、<line-break>真の闇に堕ちた。<end>"
EN: "One had long been shrouded in darkness, but was granted light one day. Then, in despair upon discovering that the light had brought only more darkness, that soul fell into true darkness.<end>"
KO: "한 사람은 오랫동안 어둠에 싸여 있다가 어느 날 빛을 받았다. 하지만 그 빛이 가져온 것 또한 어둠임을 절망해 진정한 어둠에 떨어졌다.<end>"

## 1910067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふたりとも新しい世界を望んでいた。<line-break>だから、力を与えた。<line-break>今の世界を無に帰せる力をね。<line-break>おかげでふたりとも<line-break>自意識を失ってしまった。<line-break>強力な闇の力を流し込んだから。<end>"
EN: "Both longed for a new world. So I gave them the power to reduce this one to nothing. As a result, they both lost their sense of self. I poured too much dark power into them.<end>"
KO: "둘 다 새로운 세계를 원했어. 그래서 힘을 줬지. 지금 세계를 무로 돌릴 수 있는 힘을. 그 탓에 둘 다 자아를 잃었어. 강력한 어둠의 힘을 흘려 넣었으니까.<end>"

## 1910068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんということを…。<end>"
EN: "What have you done…?<end>"
KO: "이럴 수가….<end>"

## 1910069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふたりとも世界の破滅を望んでいた。<line-break>このような世界はない方が<line-break>いいと思っていた。<line-break>この世のすべてを無に帰し、<line-break>新しい世に再生せねばならない。<end>"
EN: "Both wanted the world destroyed. Both believed a world like this would be better off not existing. Everything must be reduced to nothing, so that the world may be born anew.<end>"
KO: "둘 다 세계의 파멸을 원하고 있었어. 이런 세계는 없는 편이 낫다고 생각했지. 세상의 모든 것을 무로 돌리고 새로운 세상으로 되살려야 한다고.<end>"

## 1910070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君たちも無に帰るがいい。<end>"
EN: "You too shall return to nothing.<end>"
KO: "너희도 무로 돌아가라.<end>"

## 1910071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、さすがだ。<line-break>ネメア、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I expected no less from you, Nemea, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 역시 대단하군. 네메아, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1910072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス、お前も望むのか？<line-break>世界の破滅を。<end>"
EN: "Elfas, is that what you want too? The destruction of the world?<end>"
KO: "엘파스, 너도 바라는 건가? 세계의 파멸을.<end>"

## 1910073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当たり前だよ、ネメア。<line-break>姉さんのいない世界に何の価値がある？<line-break>　<line-break>愛するもののほほえみのない世界など<line-break>存在する意味はない。<end>"
EN: "Of course it is, Nemea. What value is there in a world without my sister? A world without the smile of the one I love has no reason to exist.<end>"
KO: "당연하지, 네메아. 누나가 없는 세상에 무슨 가치가 있어? 사랑하는 사람의 미소가 없는 세상 따윈 존재할 의미가 없어.<end>"

## 1910074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべてを無に。<line-break>そして、再生を。<end>"
EN: "Let all return to nothing. Then let it be born anew.<end>"
KO: "모든 것을 무로. 그리고 재생을.<end>"

## 1910075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…、施文院は…、古来…<line-break>数…多くの闇を…封印してきた。　<line-break>　<line-break>その中で…、最も強力なものは…<line-break>闇の巨人…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh… Since ancient times… the Inscription Order has sealed away… countless forms of darkness. And the most powerful of them… is the Giant of Darkness…<end>"
KO: "후후후… 시문원은… 예로부터… 수많은 어둠을… 봉인해 왔다. 그중… 가장 강력한 것은… 어둠의 거인….<end>"

## 1910076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の…あらゆる…負の感情を集め、　<line-break>闇の王と…闇の王女…<line-break>　<line-break>死体を…媒体として、産み出される、<line-break>負の力の…、集合体…。<end>"
EN: "A mass of negative power… created by gathering every negative emotion in the human heart… using the corpses of the King of Darkness… and the Princess of Darkness… as its medium…<end>"
KO: "인간의… 모든… 부정적인 감정을 모아, 어둠의 왕과… 어둠의 왕녀… 시체를… 매개로 만들어지는 부정적인 힘의… 집합체….<end>"

## 1910077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が…封印だと…思う？<line-break>それは…、施文院の長の…命…。<line-break>そう…、僕の…命だ…。<end>"
EN: "What do you think… serves as its seal? The life… of the head of the Inscription Order. Yes… my life…<end>"
KO: "무엇이… 봉인이라고… 생각해? 그건… 시문원 수장의… 목숨…. 그래… 내… 목숨이야…<end>"

## 1910078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうことか。<line-break>くっ…！　やってくれる！<line-break>来るぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "So that's it. Damn…! He's done it! It's coming, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그런 거였나. 큭…! 제법이군! 온다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1910079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ…、よみがえれ…闇の巨人よ。<line-break>世界を無に返し、<line-break>再生を神の手にゆだねよ！<end>"
EN: "Now… arise, O Giant of Darkness. Return the world to nothing, and leave its rebirth in divine hands!<end>"
KO: "자… 되살아나라… 어둠의 거인이여. 세계를 무로 돌리고 재생을 신의 손에 맡겨라!<end>"

## 1910080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て。様子が変だ…。<end>"
EN: "Wait. Something's wrong…<end>"
KO: "기다려. 상태가 이상해….<end>"

## 1910081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…。<end>"
EN: "This is…<end>"
KO: "이건….<end>"

## 1910082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の聖母神…、ティラ…。　　　　　　<line-break>この大地を産んだ精霊の母。<line-break>　<line-break>大地を汚す人を滅ぼさんとし、<line-break>闇に落とされた女神。<line-break>なぜここに？　封印されていたはず…。<end>"
EN: "The Mother Goddess of Darkness… Tira… Mother of the spirits who gave birth to this land. A goddess who sought to destroy the humans defiling the earth, and was cast into darkness. Why is she here? She was supposed to be sealed away…<end>"
KO: "어둠의 성모신… 티라…. 이 대지를 낳은 정령의 어머니. 대지를 더럽히는 인간을 멸하려다 어둠으로 떨어진 여신. 왜 여기에? 봉인되어 있었을 텐데….<end>"

## 1910083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしかして、<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたは封印の巨人をすべて<line-break>倒してしまったのですか？<end>"
EN: "Could it be, <value:$28>? Did you defeat every one of the sealed giants?<end>"
KO: "혹시 <value:$28>. 봉인의 거인을 전부 쓰러뜨린 겁니까?<end>"

## 1910084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　封印の巨人の…呪縛から…　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　解き放たれ…、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　復讐の時を…得たり…。<end>"
EN: "I am freed... from the sealed giant's curse... and the hour of vengeance... is mine.<end>"
KO: "봉인의 거인의… 속박에서… 풀려나… 복수의 때를… 얻었도다….<end>"

## 1910085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　強い…ソウル…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　我が身に取り込み…、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　我が…糧とせん…。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　許さぬぞ…ノトゥーンの子らよ！　　　<line-break>　　　　我が大地を…荒らす者たちよ！<line-break>　　　　この大地は、我が子らのもの！<end>"
EN: "A powerful... Soul.... I shall absorb it into my being... and make it... my sustenance.... I shall not forgive you... children of Notun! You who ravage... my land! This land belongs to my children!<end>"
KO: "강한… 소울…. 내 몸에 받아들여… 나의… 양식으로 삼으리라…. 용서하지 않겠다… 노툰의 자식들이여! 내 대지를… 어지럽히는 자들이여! 이 대지는 나의 자식들의 것이다!<end>"

## 1910086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の巨人の死によってできた<line-break>強力な闇の力場を通って復活したか。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "So that's how the resurrection happened: by passing through the powerful field of darkness created by the Dark Giant's death. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "어둠의 거인의 죽음으로 생긴 강력한 어둠의 역장을 통해 부활했나. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1910087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　おのれ…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　だが、これでは…終わらぬ…。　　　<line-break>　　　　必ず…、この大地を…取り戻す…。<line-break>　　　　　　いつの日か…、必ず…！<end>"
EN: "Damn... But this... is not the end. This land... will surely be reclaimed. Someday... without fail...!<end>"
KO: "이놈…. 하지만 이걸로… 끝나지 않는다…. 반드시… 이 대지를… 되찾겠다…. 언젠가… 반드시…!<end>"

## 1910088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さすがです、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>やっぱり、勝ち残って<line-break>しまったんですね。<end>"
EN: "I should have known, <value:$28>. You really did survive to the end.<end>"
KO: "…역시 대단하세요, <value:$28>님. 결국 끝까지 이기셨군요.<end>"

## 1910089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、竜王様の意志…。<end>"
EN: "Such is the Dragon King's will...<end>"
KO: "그것이 용왕님의 뜻….<end>"

## 1910090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの活躍で、<line-break>世界は救われました。<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、そのかわりに、<line-break>世界は新たなる脅威に<line-break>さらされています。<end>"
EN: "Your deeds saved the world. But in return, the world now faces a new threat.<end>"
KO: "당신의 활약으로 세계는 구원받았습니다. 하지만 그 대신 세계는 새로운 위협에 노출되었습니다.<end>"

## 1910091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはあなたです。<line-break>闇をすべて消し去ってしまうほどの<line-break>強烈な光の存在。<line-break>世界の均衡を保つため、<line-break>わたしは、あなたを<line-break>…とめなければなりません。<end>"
EN: "That threat is you: a light so powerful it could banish all darkness. To preserve the Balance of the World, I must... stop you.<end>"
KO: "그건 당신입니다. 어둠을 모두 지워 버릴 만큼 강렬한 빛의 존재. 세계의 균형을 지키기 위해 저는 당신을… 막아야 합니다.<end>"
