루루안타가 아는 <value:$28>는 절대 그런 짓 안 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 건가? 바보 같은 소리. 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 어리석지 않다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28>가 새로운 세계의 위협이 된다는 거냐?! 용왕이여… 어리석구나. 나의 <value:$28>가 그런 인간일 리 없다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23용왕이여, <value:$28>가 새로운 세계의 위협이 된다는 거냐?! 어리석군…. 내가 모든 것을 맡긴 인간이 그런 인간일 리 없다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 생각하는 건가? 하지만 그럴 리 없어. <value:$28>는 그런 인간이 아니다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26잠깐 기다려. 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계의 위협이 된다는 거냐? 그럴 리 없다! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 인간이 아니다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24그럴 수가…! <value:$28> 님이 세계의 위협이 된다는 겁니까?! 그럴 리 없습니다! 제가 아는 <value:$28> 님은 그런 사람이 아닙니다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25잠깐 기다려! 새로운 위협이라니 무슨 말이야!? 아무것도 모르면서!! 그런… 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 될 리 없잖아!<end>어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 거야? 그럴 리 없어! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 어리석지 않아!<end>"

## 1910093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはどうかな？<line-break><value:$28>は今や<line-break>地上最強の存在となりつつある。<line-break>もし、その<value:$28>が<line-break>心変わりをしたら<line-break>誰がとめられるというのだ？<end>"
EN: "Can you be so sure? <value:$28> is fast becoming the most powerful being in the world. If <value:$28> should have a change of heart, who could possibly stand in the way?<end>"
KO: "그건 어떨까? <value:$28>는 지금 지상 최강의 존재가 되어 가고 있다. 만약 그 <value:$28>가 마음을 바꾸면 누가 막을 수 있겠나?<end>"

## 1910094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなる前に排除せよとの　　　　<line-break>竜王様の賢明なるご判断だ。<line-break>　<line-break>さぁ、ノエル、カフィン、レイヴン、<line-break>竜王様の加護を受けし者たちよ。<line-break>今こそその恩に報いるときだ。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King has wisely decreed that the threat be eliminated before that can happen. Come, Noel, Coffin, Raven―you who have received the Dragon King's blessing. Now is the time to repay that debt.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 되기 전에 배제하라는 용왕님의 현명한 판단이다. 자, 노엘, 카핀, 레이븐, 용왕님의 가호를 받은 자들이여. 지금이야말로 그 은혜에 보답할 때다.<end>"

## 1910095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺はノエルに従うと決めている。<line-break>ノエルの前に立つ敵は<line-break>排除する。…それだけだ。<end>"
EN: "I have chosen to follow Noel. Anyone who stands against her will be eliminated. That is all.<end>"
KO: "…나는 노엘을 따르기로 정했다. 노엘 앞을 막는 적은 제거한다. …그뿐이다.<end>"

## 1910096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなことになってしまって<line-break>残念だけど、<line-break>全力でいかせてもらうわ！<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry it's come to this, but I won't hold back!<end>"
KO: "이렇게 돼서 유감이지만, 전력으로 상대해 주겠어!<end>"

## 1910097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん！<line-break>ノエルの最後の剣、<line-break>受けとめてください！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Face Noel's final strike!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨! 노엘의 마지막 검을 받아 주세요!<end>"

## 1910098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぅ…、やっぱり<line-break>つよい…。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... You really are strong...<end>"
KO: "으으…, 역시 강해….<end>"

## 1910099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、わかっているだろうな。<line-break>お前に敗北は許されてはいない。<line-break>お前が退いたそのときは…。<end>"
EN: "Noel, you understand, don't you? You are not permitted to lose. The moment you retreat...<end>"
KO: "노엘, 알고 있겠지. 네게 패배는 허용되지 않는다. 네가 물러나는 순간에는….<end>"

## 1910100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめて、ナーシェス。<line-break><value:$28>さんに<line-break>そのことは言わないで！<end>"
EN: "Stop, Narscess! Don't tell <value:$28> about that!<end>"
KO: "그만해, 나셰스. <value:$28>에게 그 얘기는 하지 마!<end>"

## 1910101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、よいではないか。<line-break><value:$28>にも<line-break>聞かせてやるとしよう。　　　　　<line-break>そうすれば、人間の持つ<line-break>哀れみの感情とやらで、<line-break>竜王様に従う気になるかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Why not? Let <value:$28> hear it. Perhaps that vaunted human capacity for pity will inspire obedience to the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "후후, 좋지 않은가. <value:$28>에게도 들려주지. 그러면 인간이 가진 연민이라는 감정으로 용왕님을 따를 마음이 생길지도 모르니.<end>"

## 1910102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>お前を慕っていたノエルが<line-break>ここに来た理由がわかるか？<line-break>お前が死なない限り、<line-break>カフィンとレイヴンが死人に<line-break>戻ってしまうからだ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, do you know why Noel, who cared so deeply for you, came here? Because unless you die, Coffin and Raven will be dead once more!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너를 따르던 노엘이 왜 여기 왔는지 아나? 네가 죽지 않는 한 카핀과 레이븐이 다시 죽은 자로 돌아가 버리기 때문이다!<end>"

## 1910103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の均衡を崩すほどに<line-break>成長してしまった<line-break>自らの命を絶つ気にな。フフ…。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps it will even persuade you to take your own life―the life that has grown powerful enough to upset the Balance of the World. Heh...<end>"
KO: "세계의 균형을 무너뜨릴 만큼 성장해 버린 자신의 목숨을 끊을 생각이었지. 후후….<end>"

## 1910104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…。<line-break>あたしたち…もう…いくわ…<end>"
EN: "Noel... It's time for us... to go...<end>"
KO: "노엘…. 우리… 이제… 갈게….<end>"

## 1910105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィンも、レイヴンも、<line-break>一度死んでいる。竜王様のお力で<line-break>ソウルを体に留めているにすぎぬ。<line-break>竜王様のご意志にそえないとき、<line-break>それがどうなるか…。<line-break>フフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Coffin and Raven have both died once already. The Dragon King's power merely keeps their Souls bound to their bodies. If they should fail to obey his will, you know what will happen... Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "카핀도 레이븐도 한 번 죽었다. 용왕님의 힘으로 소울을 몸에 붙잡아 두고 있을 뿐이다. 용왕님의 뜻에 따르지 못할 때 그것이 어떻게 될지…. 후후후….<end>"

## 1910106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、どうする？<line-break>お前が死ななければ、<line-break>このふたりは死ぬぞ！<end>"
EN: "Well? What will you do? Unless you die, these two will!<end>"
KO: "자, 어떻게 할래? 네가 죽지 않으면 이 둘이 죽는다!<end>"

## 1910107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、レイヴン…！？<end>"
EN: "Ah... Raven?!<end>"
KO: "아…, 레이븐…!?<end>"

## 1910108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、バカな！<line-break>貴様、正気か…！？　　<line-break>　<line-break>私に刃向かうのは<line-break>竜王様に剣を向けるのと<line-break>同じことだぞ！<end>"
EN: "I-impossible! Have you lost your mind?! To defy me is no different from raising your sword against the Dragon King!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼! 네놈, 제정신이냐…!? 내게 대드는 것은 용왕님께 칼을 겨누는 것과 같은 일이다!<end>"

## 1910109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんたの言いぐさは<line-break>聞くに耐えないんでな。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>殺人機械だった俺を、<line-break>助け出してくれたことには感謝してる。<line-break>だが、ノエルを悲しませるのは許さん。<end>"
EN: "I couldn't stand to hear another word from you. I'm grateful that you rescued me when I was nothing but a killing machine. But I won't let you make Noel suffer.<end>"
KO: "…네 말투는 도저히 듣고 있을 수가 없군. 살인 기계였던 나를 구해 준 건 고맙게 생각한다. 하지만 노엘을 슬프게 하는 건 용서 못 해.<end>"

## 1910110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…。<line-break>今の俺はお前の重荷にすぎない。<line-break>それは…俺には許されないことなのだ。<line-break>お前のそばにいるようになって、<line-break>俺は人間に戻ることができた。<line-break>感謝している。…さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "Noel... As I am now, I am nothing but a burden to you. I cannot allow that. At your side, I became human again. Thank you... Farewell.<end>"
KO: "노엘…. 지금의 나는 네게 짐일 뿐이다. 그건… 내겐 허락할 수 없는 일이야. 네 곁에 있게 된 뒤 나는 인간으로 돌아올 수 있었다. 감사한다. …잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1910111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴン！<end>"
EN: "Raven!<end>"
KO: "레이븐!<end>"

## 1910112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル。<line-break>あたしのノエル。<line-break>もう泣かないで…<line-break>あたしたちは一度死んだ身だったの。<line-break>そのあたしたちが生きているなんて<line-break>いけないことだったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Noel. My Noel. Don't cry anymore... We had already died once. It was wrong for us to go on living.<end>"
KO: "노엘. 내 노엘. 이제 울지 마…. 우리는 한 번 죽었던 몸이야. 그런 우리가 살아 있는 건 해서는 안 되는 일이었던 거야.<end>"

## 1910113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一度死んだって、祈れば生き返る。<line-break>…そんなことになってごらんなさい。<line-break>命の価値がどんどん低くなってしまう。<end>"
EN: "Imagine if anyone who died could simply pray and come back to life. Life itself would become worth less and less.<end>"
KO: "한번 죽어도 기도하면 살아난다…. 그런 세상이 되어 보세요. 생명의 가치는 점점 낮아지고 말 겁니다.<end>"

## 1910114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン！<end>"
EN: "Coffin!<end>"
KO: "카핀!<end>"

## 1910115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしはあたしの人生に、<line-break>合格点をあげられる。　　<line-break>　<line-break>あたしを拾ってくれた<line-break>町の人に恩返しができて…。<end>"
EN: "I can give my life a passing grade. I managed to repay the townspeople who took me in...<end>"
KO: "나는 내 인생에 합격점을 줄 수 있어. 나를 거둬 준 마을 사람들에게 은혜를 갚을 수 있었으니까….<end>"

## 1910116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン…。<end>"
EN: "Coffin...<end>"
KO: "카핀….<end>"

## 1910117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル。<line-break>あたしのノエル。<line-break>もう泣かないで…<end>"
EN: "Noel. My Noel. Don't cry anymore...<end>"
KO: "노엘. 나의 노엘. 이제 울지 마….<end>"

## 1910118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぅ…カフィン…<line-break>レイヴン…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Coffin... Raven...<end>"
KO: "으으… 카핀… 레이븐….<end>"

## 1910119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、ノエル。<line-break>あたしがダルケニスなのを知ってても<line-break>暖かく迎えてくれたあなたを守れて…。<end>"
EN: "And Noel... You welcomed me warmly even though you knew I was a Darkenith. I was able to protect you...<end>"
KO: "그리고 노엘. 내가 다르케니스라는 걸 알면서도 따뜻하게 맞아 준 너를 지킬 수 있어서….<end>"

## 1910120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　愚かなり、ナーシェス…　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　この竜王が与えし、<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者を<line-break>　　　　　　使いこなせぬとは…<end>"
EN: "How foolish, Narscess... To think you could not make use of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul granted by this Dragon King...<end>"
KO: "어리석구나, 나셰스…. 이 용왕이 내려 준 무한의 소울을 가진 자를 제대로 다루지 못하다니….<end>"

## 1910121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王…様、<line-break>わたしは…<line-break>もう戦えません。<end>"
EN: "Dragon King... my lord... I can't fight anymore.<end>"
KO: "용왕…님, 저는… 이제 싸울 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1910122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　我が力を受けし少女、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　ノエルよ…　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　竜王との契約を　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　果たせぬ者に用はない…<end>"
EN: "Noel, girl who received my power... I have no use for one who cannot fulfill the pact with the Dragon King...<end>"
KO: "나의 힘을 받은 소녀, 노엘이여…. 용왕과의 계약을 완수하지 못하는 자에게 볼일은 없다….<end>"

## 1910123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　ならばノエルよ…　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　汝のもつ<line-break>　　　　　　　無限のソウルは、<line-break>　　　　　この竜王が役立てよう…<end>"
EN: "Then, Noel... I, the Dragon King, shall put your Infinite Soul to use...<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 노엘이여… 그대가 지닌 무한의 소울은 이 용왕이 유용하게 쓰리라…<end>"

## 1910124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　いまひとりの　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者…<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>よ…<line-break>　　　　　　　　聞くがよい…<end>"
EN: "There is another Bearer of the Infinite Soul... <value:$28>... Hear me...<end>"
KO: "또 한 명의 무한의 소울을 지닌 자… <value:$28>여… 들어라….<end>"

## 1910125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　汝の力は強大すぎる…　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　世界の均衡を崩しかねぬ…<line-break>　　　　　　ゆえに、我みずから<line-break>　　　　　その強大な力を封じよう…<end>"
EN: "Your power is too great... It could upset the Balance of the World... Therefore, I shall seal away that immense power myself...<end>"
KO: "그대의 힘은 너무 강대하다… 세계의 균형을 무너뜨릴 수도 있다… 그러므로 내가 직접 그 강대한 힘을 봉인하겠다…<end>"

## 1910126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　さぁ、くるがよい…　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　汝の前に開けし門が…<line-break>　　　　　　　汝を、我が前へと<line-break>　　　　　　　　呼びよせん…<end>"
EN: "Come, then... The gate that has opened before you... shall summon you into my presence...<end>"
KO: "자, 오너라…. 그대 앞에 열린 문이 그대를 내 앞으로 불러오리라….<end>"

## 1910127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>わたしにはこんなことを言う<line-break>資格はないですけど…<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I know I have no right to say this, but...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 제가 이런 말을 할 자격은 없지만…<end>"

## 1910128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がんばってください…。<end>"
EN: "Please... keep going.<end>"
KO: "힘내세요….<end>"

## 1910129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが、本当に最後の戦いね。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>ちゃんと準備はできた？<end>"
EN: "So this really is the final battle. Are you fully prepared, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "이게 정말 마지막 싸움이네. <value:$28>, 준비는 제대로 됐어?<end>"

## 1910130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、もう一踏ん張りじゃ。<line-break>最後の決戦、気を引き締めて<line-break>ゆかねばな。<end>"
EN: "Come, one last push. We must keep our wits about us for this final battle.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 한 번 더 힘내자. 마지막 결전이다. 마음 단단히 먹고 가야 해.<end>"

## 1910131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついに、このお話も最後だよね？<line-break><value:$28>、ガンバろうね。<line-break>　<line-break>竜王様を倒したら、<line-break>もっと有名になっちゃうかも。<line-break>きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "This is finally the end of the story, isn't it? Let's do our best, <value:$28>. Once we defeat the Dragon King, we might get even more famous. Eek!<end>"
KO: "드디어 이 이야기도 마지막이지? <value:$28>, 힘내자. 용왕님을 쓰러뜨리면 더 유명해질지도 몰라. 꺄!<end>"

## 1910132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今さらビビったりしてないよな？<line-break>この戦いでケリがつくんだ。<line-break>腕がなるぜ！<end>"
EN: "You're not getting cold feet now, are you? This battle will settle everything. I've been itching for this!<end>"
KO: "이제 와서 겁먹은 건 아니지? 이번 싸움으로 끝장이야. 몸이 근질거리네!<end>"

## 1910133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついにここまで来てしまったね。<line-break><value:$28>と初めて会ったとき、<line-break>今の状況は予測もつかなかった…。<line-break>でも、ここまできたら、<line-break>最後までついていくよ！<end>"
EN: "We've finally made it this far. When I first met you, <value:$28>, I never imagined we'd end up in a situation like this. But now that we're here, I'm with you to the very end!<end>"
KO: "마침내 여기까지 왔네. <value:$28>과 처음 만났을 때는 지금 상황을 상상도 못했어…. 하지만 여기까지 왔으니 끝까지 따라갈게!<end>"

## 1910134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様と一緒にもう一度<line-break>戦えるなんて思ってなかったわ。<line-break>あと一息ね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I never thought I'd have the chance to fight alongside Lord Nemea again. Just a little farther, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네메아 님과 함께 다시 싸우게 될 줄은 몰랐어. 이제 한숨만 더 내면 돼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1910135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが猫屋敷を訪れてから<line-break>始まった冒険も、これでようやく<line-break>ひとつの終わりを迎えます。<line-break>でも、<value:$28>。<line-break>冒険はあなたの人生のほんの<line-break>一部でしかありません。<end>"
EN: "The adventure that began when you visited Cat Manor is finally coming to an end. But remember, <value:$28>: this adventure is only one small part of your life.<end>"
KO: "당신이 고양이 저택을 방문하면서 시작된 모험도 이제 하나의 끝을 맞습니다. 하지만 <value:$28>. 모험은 당신 인생의 아주 작은 일부일 뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1910137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついにここまで来ちゃったねぇ。<line-break>こんなすごいことになるなんて、<line-break>あたしには想像もつかなかったよ。<line-break>さぁ、そんじゃ、ついでに<line-break>竜王もまとめてたたんじゃうと<line-break>しますか。<end>"
EN: "Well, we finally made it this far. I never imagined things would get this big. So, while we're at it, how about we take care of the Dragon King too?<end>"
KO: "드디어 여기까지 왔네. 이렇게 대단한 일이 될 줄은 상상도 못 했어. 자, 그럼 내친김에 용왕도 같이 두들겨 패 볼까.<end>"

## 1910138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王の力はどれほどのものか<line-break>想像もつかないわ。<line-break>最後まで気を抜かないで。<end>"
EN: "I can't begin to imagine how powerful the Dragon King is. Don't let your guard down until it's over.<end>"
KO: "용왕의 힘이 어느 정도인지 상상도 안 돼. 마지막까지 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 1910139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が竜王の言うようにはならないと<line-break>俺は信じている。だから、<line-break>こうしてお前についてきたんだ。<end>"
EN: "I believe you won't become what the Dragon King says you will. That's why I've come this far with you.<end>"
KO: "난 네가 용왕이 말한 것처럼 되지 않을 거라고 믿어. 그래서 이렇게 널 따라온 거야.<end>"

## 1910140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配しなさんな。<line-break>もしもお前がヤバくなったときは<line-break>俺がとめてやるさ。<line-break>だから、<value:$28>。<line-break>自分を信じて戦おうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. If you ever become dangerous, I'll stop you myself. So believe in yourself, <value:$28>, and fight.<end>"
KO: "걱정 마. 네가 위험해지면 내가 막아 줄 테니까. 그러니 <value:$28>, 자신을 믿고 싸우자.<end>"

## 1910141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした。<line-break>最後の戦いの準備は<line-break>もういいのか？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Are you ready for the final battle?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래. 마지막 싸움 준비는 벌써 끝났나?<end>"

## 1910142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は姉ちゃんを信じてるぜ。<line-break>だから、最後までついていく！<end>"
EN: "I believe in you, sis. That's why I'm sticking with you to the very end!<end>"
KO: "난 누나를 믿어. 그러니 끝까지 따라갈 거야!<end>"

## 1910143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、あと一息じゃ。<line-break>戦いの準備はできたのか、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Come, just a little farther. Are you ready for battle, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 한숨만 더 내쉬면 된다. 전투 준비는 됐느냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1910144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネを出るとき、<line-break>こんなすごい冒険が待ってるなんて<line-break>思ってもいなかったよ。<line-break>その旅もこれで終わるんだね。<line-break>…<value:$28>、<line-break>もう準備はいいの？<end>"
EN: "When I left Terane, I never imagined such an incredible adventure lay ahead. And now that journey is coming to an end. <value:$28>... are you ready?<end>"
KO: "테라네를 나올 때 이런 엄청난 모험이 기다리고 있을 줄은 몰랐어. 그 여행도 이제 끝나는구나. …<value:$28>, 준비는 다 됐어?<end>"

## 1910145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命と戦い、そして、勝て。<line-break>竜王の力は未知数だが、<line-break>命を賭けて挑む価値はあろう。　<line-break>その結果どうなるかはわからぬが、<line-break>神の時代に終止符を打つのも<line-break>悪くはあるまい。<end>"
EN: "Fight fate, and prevail. The Dragon King's power is unknown, but this challenge is worth risking your life for. I cannot say what will come of it, but bringing the age of the gods to an end would not be such a bad thing.<end>"
KO: "운명과 싸워라. 그리고 이겨라. 용왕의 힘은 미지수지만 목숨을 걸고 도전할 가치는 있겠지. 그 결과가 어찌 될지는 모르나 신의 시대에 종지부를 찍는 것도 나쁘지 않을 것이다.<end>"

## 1910146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここまで来て諦めるなんて、<line-break>ぜーーったいに許せなくってよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>自分の力を信じて、<line-break>最後まで戦うのよ！<line-break>いいわね、<value:$28>！？<end>"
EN: "After coming this far, I absolutely will not allow you to give up, <value:$28>! Believe in your own strength and fight to the very end! Is that clear, <value:$28>?!<end>"
KO: "여기까지 와서 포기하다니 절대로 용서 못 해, <value:$28>! 자신의 힘을 믿고 끝까지 싸워! 알겠지, <value:$28>!?<end>"

## 1910147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いが終わったら、もう<line-break>戦わなくてもいいんだよね？<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタもがんばるから、<line-break><value:$28>も絶対に<line-break>負けちゃダメだよ。<end>"
EN: "Once this battle's over, that means no more fighting, right? Lulluantha will do her best, so you can't lose either, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이 싸움이 끝나면 이제 싸우지 않아도 되는 거지? 루루안타도 힘낼 테니까 <value:$28>도 절대 지면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1910148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王…、<line-break>不足のない相手だ。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King... A worthy opponent.<end>"
KO: "용왕… 부족함 없는 상대다.<end>"

## 1910150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、<value:$28>。<line-break>必ず帰ってこようね。<end>"
EN: "So let's make sure we come back, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 <value:$28>. 반드시 돌아오자.<end>"

## 1910151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　愚かな者たちよ…　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　死こそ、汝らにとって<line-break>　　　　　最高の結末であったろうに…<end>"
EN: "Fools... Death would have been the best end you could have hoped for...<end>"
KO: "어리석은 자들이여… 죽음이야말로 너희에게 최고의 결말이었을 텐데….<end>"

## 1910152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　我は竜王…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　世界の牧者、神の代理なり…。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>よ…<line-break>　　　我の息吹にかかって消えるがよい…！　　<end>"
EN: "I am the Dragon King... shepherd of the world, representative of God... <value:$28>... Vanish before my breath...!<end>"
KO: "나는 용왕…. 세계의 목자, 신의 대리자다…. <value:$28>여… 나의 숨결에 휩쓸려 사라져라…!<end>"

## 1910153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　汝は、その力をどうするつもりなのだ…　　<line-break>　　　世界すら滅ぼす可能性のある力を…　　　<line-break>　　生ある限り、そこには葛藤あるのみぞ…　　<line-break>　　　　　　愚かなり…、愚かなり…　　　　　<end>"
EN: "What do you intend to do with that power... a power that could destroy the world itself? As long as life exists, there will be nothing but conflict... How foolish... How foolish...<end>"
KO: "그대는 그 힘을 어찌할 셈인가…. 세계조차 멸망시킬 가능성이 있는 힘을…. 살아 있는 한 그곳에는 갈등뿐…. 어리석구나…, 어리석구나….<end>"

## 1910154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　我は、うつつの身を持つ最後の神…　　　<line-break>　　　　人の子よ…、すべての神を殺し、　　　<line-break>　　　人はおのが足で歩めると言えるのか？　　<line-break>　　　　　　愚かなり…、愚かなり…　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I am the last god embodied in this world... Child of man... having slain all the gods, can humanity claim to walk on its own two feet? How foolish... How foolish...<end>"
KO: "나는 현세의 육신을 지닌 마지막 신…. 인간의 아이여…, 모든 신을 죽이고 인간이 자기 발로 걸을 수 있다고 말할 수 있느냐? 어리석구나…, 어리석구나….<end>"

## 1910156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終わったな。<end>"
EN: "It's over.<end>"
KO: "끝났군.<end>"

## 1910157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、戻ろう。<line-break>戦いが終わっても、<line-break>やらねばならぬことは残っている。<end>"
EN: "Come, let's go back. The battle may be over, but there is still work to be done.<end>"
KO: "자, 돌아가자. 싸움이 끝나도 해야 할 일은 남아 있다.<end>"

## 1910158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王が倒れましたか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>うつしよの最後の神が消え、<line-break>人は自分たちの力だけで<line-break>歩まねばならなくなりましたね。<end>"
EN: "So the Dragon King has fallen... The last god in the mortal world is gone, and humankind must now walk forward under its own power.<end>"
KO: "용왕이 쓰러졌습니까…. 현세의 마지막 신이 사라지고 인간은 자기 힘만으로 걸어가야 하게 됐군요.<end>"

## 1910159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この日が訪れたことを<line-break>喜ぶべきか悲しむべきか…。<end>"
EN: "Should we rejoice that this day has come, or mourn it...?<end>"
KO: "이날이 찾아온 것을 기뻐해야 할지 슬퍼해야 할지….<end>"

## 1910160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>今は、人の可能性を信じて、<line-break>時の流れに身を任せましょうか。<line-break>あなたが開いた新たな未来の扉は、<line-break>無限の可能性に満ちていますものね。<end>"
EN: "Yes. For now, perhaps we should trust in humanity's potential and let time run its course. The door you opened to a new future is filled with limitless possibilities.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 지금은 인간의 가능성을 믿고 시간의 흐름에 몸을 맡겨 보죠. 당신이 연 새로운 미래의 문은 무한한 가능성으로 가득하니까요.<end>"

## 1910161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とりあえず、闇の侵攻は、<line-break>くい止められましたね。<line-break>よくやってくれました。<line-break>でも、これであなたの旅が、<line-break>終わったわけではありません。<end>"
EN: "For now, the advance of darkness has been halted. You have done well. But that does not mean your journey is over.<end>"
KO: "일단 어둠의 침공은 막았습니다. 잘해 주셨어요. 하지만 이걸로 당신의 여행이 끝난 건 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1910162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。これからあなたは、<line-break>どんな道を進むのでしょうね？<line-break>楽しみです。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I wonder what path you will take from here. I look forward to finding out.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 앞으로 당신은 어떤 길을 걷게 될까요? 기대되네요.<end>"

## 1910163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、戻りましょうか。<line-break>無事な姿をみんなに見せないと。<line-break>さあ、行きましょう。<end>"
EN: "Shall we go back, then? We need to show everyone we're safe and sound. Come, let's go.<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌아갈까요. 무사한 모습을 모두에게 보여 줘야죠. 자, 갑시다.<end>"

## 1910164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　神聖暦<value:$24>年<value:$25>月　　　　<line-break>後に多くの吟遊詩人に歌われるこの騒乱の後<line-break>　　ディンガル帝国とロストール王国は　　<line-break>　　　　　和平条約を締結した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Year <value:$24>, Month <value:$25> of the Sacred Calendar. In the aftermath of this turmoil, which would later be sung of by many bards, the Dyneskal Empire and the Kingdom of Rostorl concluded a peace treaty.<end>"
KO: "신성력 <value:$24>년 <value:$25>월 후에 수많은 음유시인이 노래하게 될 이 소란 뒤 딩갈 제국과 로스톨 왕국은 평화조약을 체결했다.<end>"

## 1910165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "Ｖ７の３か、終幕は近い。<line-break>予備メッセージの８だ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "V7의 3인가, 종막이 가깝군. 예비 메시지 8이다.<end>"

## 1910166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec191-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "Ｖ７の３か、終幕は近い。<line-break>予備メッセージの９だ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "V7의 3인가, 종막이 가깝군. 예비 메시지 9다.<end>"

## 1910167

## 1920004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "起きなさい、<value:$28>！<line-break>もう、お昼よ！<end>"

KO: "일어나, <value:$28>! 벌써 점심이야!<end>"

## 1920005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何してるのよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ベッドから落ちたりして。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ほんっとに、いつまでたっても<line-break>変わらないわねぇ。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing on the floor, <value:$28>? You fell out of bed again? Honestly, you never change.<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>. 침대에서 떨어지다니. 정말 언제까지나 변함없네.<end>"

## 1920006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、<value:$28>。<line-break>どう？　目は覚めた？<end>"
EN: "Good morning, <value:$28>. Feeling awake now?<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, <value:$28>. 어때? 잠은 깼어?<end>"

## 1920007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、さっさと行くわよ。　　　　<line-break>はやく準備しなさい。<line-break>　<line-break>あ、ちゃんと服を着替えないとダメよ。<line-break>外で待ってるから、すぐに準備を<line-break>すませること。いいわね？<end>"
EN: "All right, let's get going. Hurry up and get ready. Oh, and you have to change your clothes first. I'll wait outside, so don't take long. Understood?<end>"
KO: "그럼 얼른 가자. 빨리 준비해. 아, 옷도 제대로 갈아입어야 해. 밖에서 기다릴 테니 바로 준비 끝내. 알겠지?<end>"

## 1920008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>。<line-break>目が覚めたみたいね。<line-break>朝ご飯はできてるわよ。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Looks like you're awake. Breakfast is ready.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>. 깨어난 모양이네. 아침밥은 다 됐어.<end>"

## 1920009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンが待ってるから　　　<line-break>朝ご飯を食べていく時間がない？<line-break>ふふ、仕方ないわね。<line-break>それじゃ、行ってらっしゃい、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>ほどほどにね。<end>"
EN: "No time for breakfast because Irene's waiting? Heh, I suppose it can't be helped. Off you go, then, <value:$28>. And don't overdo it.<end>"
KO: "아이린이 기다려서 아침 먹을 시간도 없어? 후후, 어쩔 수 없네. 그럼 다녀와, <value:$28>. 적당히 해.<end>"

## 1920010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの日…。アイリーンと　　　　<line-break><value:$28>が町を出た<line-break>あの日…。<line-break>ふたりがこっそりと出ていくのを、<line-break>黙って見送ってしまったことを<line-break>何度も後悔したけれど…。<end>"
EN: "That day... the day Irene and <value:$28> left town... I regretted it time and again, simply watching in silence as the two of them slipped away...<end>"
KO: "그날…. 아이린과 <value:$28>이 마을을 떠난 그날…. 두 사람이 몰래 떠나는 걸 말없이 배웅한 걸 몇 번이나 후회했지만….<end>"

## 1920011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あの子たちは　　　　　　<line-break>帰ってきてくれた。<line-break>この家に…。<line-break>あのころと変わらない毎日。<line-break>でも、こんなにうれしいことは<line-break>ないわ。<end>"
EN: "But those two came back. Back to this house... Now every day is just as it was then. Nothing could make me happier.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 아이들은 돌아와 줬어. 이 집으로…. 그때와 다르지 않은 매일. 그런데 이렇게 기쁠 수가 없어.<end>"

## 1920012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、あなた…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Isn't that right, dear...?<end>"
KO: "저기, 당신….<end>"

## 1920013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たわね、<value:$28>。<line-break>言っておくけど、今日こそは　　<line-break>絶対に一本取ってみせるわ！<line-break>あなたから一本取るまで<line-break>王宮の近衛騎士の任務は<line-break>受けないって決めてるんだから。<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>. Just so you know, today I'm finally going to score a point against you! I've decided I won't accept any duties with the palace's Royal Guard until I do.<end>"
KO: "왔구나, <value:$28>. 말해 두는데 오늘이야말로 반드시 한 판 따낼 거야! 네게 한 판 따낼 때까지 왕궁 근위기사 임무는 안 받기로 했거든.<end>"

## 1920014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、さっさと道場に行くわよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, let's get to the training hall.<end>"
KO: "자, 얼른 도장으로 가자.<end>"

## 1920015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのころと変わらない毎日…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ううん、今では私が<line-break><value:$28>の背中を<line-break>追いかけてる…。<end>"
EN: "Every day is just as it was then... No. Now I'm the one chasing after <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "그때와 다르지 않은 나날…. 아니, 지금은 내가 <value:$28>의 등을 쫓고 있어….<end>"

## 1920016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それ以外は同じ。　　　<line-break>私は帰ってきたんだ。<line-break>この町に…。<line-break>私の一番大切な、あのころに…。<end>"
EN: "But everything else is the same. I've come home... to this town, and to those days I treasure most...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 외에는 똑같아. 나는 돌아왔어. 이 마을로…. 내가 가장 소중히 여기던 그 시절로….<end>"

## 1920017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "좋아, 거기까지. <value:$28>의 승리다.<end>"

## 1920018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがゴブ。<line-break>これで<value:$28>の<line-break>７８連勝ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Impressive, gob! That makes seventy-eight wins in a row for <value:$28>, gob!<end>"
KO: "역시 고브. 이걸로 <value:$28>의 78연승 고브!<end>"

## 1920020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン、<line-break>もうやめた方がいいゴブよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>一本とれなくったって、<line-break><value:$28>はアイリーンの<line-break>よさをちゃんとわかっているゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Irene, you should give it up now, gob. Even if you don't score a point, <value:$28> knows what's good about you, gob.<end>"
KO: "아이린, 이제 그만하는 게 좋다 고브. 한 번도 못 이겨도 <value:$28>는 아이린의 좋은 점을 제대로 알고 있다 고브.<end>"

## 1920021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々はオッシ先生に<line-break>弟子入りした身ゴブ。<line-break>アイリーンとは兄弟弟子ゴブ。<end>"

## 1920024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "시끄러워!<end>"

## 1920027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生、また今日も<line-break>稽古をつけてください！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>明日こそ、<value:$28>から<line-break>一本取ってみせます！<end>"
EN: "Master, please train me again today! Tomorrow, I swear I'll score a point against <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1920028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920030

## 1920032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…정말 긴장했어요. 주변 사람들이 모두 저를 보고 있었으니까요.<end>"

## 1920034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはきっと、王女様のことを<line-break>とても魅力的な方だと、<line-break>みなさまが思われたからですわ。　　<end>"

## 1920035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（私のことを、そんな風に<line-break>　思ってくれる方が<line-break>　あの方以外にいるなんて…。）<line-break>（でも、私は、あの方だけが<line-break>　そう思ってくれれば十分…。）<end>"
EN: "(To think someone besides that person would see me that way... But it's enough for me if that person alone feels that way about me...)<end>"

## 1920038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아, 아니요. 아무것도 아닙니다. 잠깐 멍하니 있었어요.<end>"

## 1920040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "분명 처음 겪으신 일이라 피곤하셨을 겁니다. 오늘은 푹 쉬세요.<end>"

## 1920041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Yes. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "응. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 1920042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は本当に、疲れたわ…。<line-break>文書の最後に署名を書くだけだった<line-break>けれど、少し曲がってしまったし…。<line-break>でも、私、がんばらなくては。<line-break>もっと勉強して、エリス様に<line-break>文書を読んでもらわなくても、<end>"
EN: "Today truly was exhausting... All I had to do was sign my name at the end of the document, but even that came out a little crooked... Still, I must do my best. I need to study more, so that even without Lady Eris reading documents to me,<end>"
KO: "오늘은 정말 피곤했어…. 문서 끝에 서명만 하면 됐는데 조금 삐뚤어지기도 했고…. 그래도 나, 힘내야 해. 더 공부해서 에리스 님께 문서를 읽어 달라고 하지 않아도,<end>"

## 1920044

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I can understand what is written in them.<end>"
KO: "적혀 있는 내용을 알아볼 수 있게 되어야 해.<end>"

## 1920045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml

## 1920046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、<value:$28>様も<line-break>今日は一日、ご苦労様でした。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そうそう。<value:$28>様を<line-break>お呼びしたのは、これを<line-break>受け取ってもらいたかったからです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You've worked hard today too, <value:$28>. Oh, yes. <value:$28>, I asked you here because I wanted you to have this.<end>"

## 1920047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が初めて誰の助けも借りずに<line-break>焼いたクッキーです。<line-break>召しあがってください。<end>"
EN: "These are the first cookies I've ever baked all by myself. Please, have some.<end>"
KO: "제가 처음으로 누구 도움도 받지 않고 구운 쿠키예요. 드셔 주세요.<end>"

## 1920048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、待ってください。<line-break>感想はあとで…、<line-break>ここでは食べないで…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, wait. Tell me what you think later... Don't eat them here...<end>"
KO: "아, 기다려 주세요. 감상은 나중에… 여기서는 먹지 마세요….<end>"

## 1920049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>どう、ですか？<end>"
EN: "U-um... How are they?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 어때요?<end>"

## 1920050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よかった。<end>"
EN: "...I'm glad.<end>"
KO: "…다행이다.<end>"

## 1920051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、これからも、いろんなことを<line-break>ひとりでできるようになりたいのです。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様と一緒にいても<line-break>迷惑にならないように<line-break>なりたいのです。<end>"
EN: "I want to keep learning to do all sorts of things on my own. I want to become someone who won't be a burden when I'm with you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저, 앞으로도 여러 가지 일을 혼자 할 수 있게 되고 싶어요. <value:$28> 님과 함께 있어도 폐를 끼치지 않게 되고 싶어요.<end>"

## 1920052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>どうか、いつまでも、私の近くに<line-break>いてください。<end>"
EN: "So please, <value:$28>... stay close to me forever.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>님. 부디 언제까지나 제 곁에 있어 주세요.<end>"

## 1920053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>…うれしいです、<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm so happy, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고마워요. …기뻐요, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1920054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>こっち、こっち。<line-break><line-break>今日は私が考案した<line-break>新しい魔法のお披露目があるの。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Over here! I'm unveiling a new spell I came up with today.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 이쪽, 이쪽. 오늘은 내가 고안한 새로운 마법을 선보일 거야.<end>"

## 1920055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、ぜひ見てもらおうと思って<line-break><value:$28>のこと<line-break>わざわざ呼び出しちゃった。<end>"
EN: "I wanted you to see it, so I went out of my way to call you here, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래서 꼭 보여 주고 싶어서 <value:$28>을 일부러 불렀어.<end>"

## 1920056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　本当に安全なのかって？<line-break>えっと…<line-break>た、たぶん、大丈夫。<line-break>そんなに細かいことに<line-break>こだわってちゃダメよ。<line-break>さっ、行きましょ！<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know if it's really safe? Um... p-probably. Don't get hung up on every little detail. Come on, let's go!<end>"
KO: "뭐? 정말 안전하냐고? 음… 아, 아마 괜찮아. 그런 사소한 것까지 신경 쓰면 안 돼. 자, 가자!<end>"

## 1920057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私ね、ときどき思うんだけど、　　　　<line-break>無名で終わる天才だって<line-break>たくさんいるのに、<line-break><value:$28>と知り合って<line-break>一躍有名になった私って、もしかしたら<line-break>とっても運がいいのかも！<end>"
EN: "You know, sometimes I think about all the geniuses who never become famous. Maybe I was incredibly lucky to meet you and become an overnight sensation, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "나 가끔 생각하는데, 이름도 못 알리고 끝나는 천재도 많잖아? 그런데 <value:$28>와 알게 돼서 단숨에 유명해진 나는 어쩌면 엄청 운이 좋은 걸지도 몰라!<end>"

## 1920058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アカデミーにも戻れたし、<line-break>これもきっと日ごろの行いが<line-break>いいせいよね！<end>"
EN: "I even got back into the Academy. It must be a reward for all my good deeds!<end>"
KO: "아카데미에도 돌아왔고, 이것도 평소 행실이 좋아서겠지!<end>"

## 1920059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ユーリス、大変だ！<line-break>お前の装置、暴走してるぞ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Julls, we've got trouble! Your device is out of control!<end>"
KO: "유리스, 큰일이야! 네 장치가 폭주하고 있어.<end>"

## 1920060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ…ええっ！<end>"
EN: "What... What?!<end>"
KO: "어… 어어!?<end>"

## 1920061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どっ、どうしよう<line-break><value:$28>！？<end>"
EN: "Wh-what do I do, <value:$28>?!<end>"
KO: "어, 어떡하지, <value:$28>!?<end>"

## 1920062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、またやっちゃった…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>せっかく魔道アカデミーに<line-break>戻れたと思ったのに。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I've done it again... Just when I thought I'd finally made it back into the Magic Academy.<end>"
KO: "아아, 또 해버렸어…. 겨우 마도 아카데미로 돌아왔다고 생각했는데.<end>"

## 1920063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよね。<line-break>大失敗は成功の元って言うし！<line-break>　<line-break>ねっ、<value:$28>。<line-break>また旅をはじめない？<line-break>私、ついていくから。<end>"
EN: "That's right! They say every spectacular failure is a step toward success! So, <value:$28>, want to set off on another journey? I'll come with you.<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 큰 실패는 성공의 어머니라고 하잖아! 있지, <value:$28>. 다시 여행을 시작하지 않을래? 나도 따라갈게.<end>"

## 1920064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>旅をしていれば、きっとまた何か<line-break>すごいことがあるでしょ。<line-break>今回も、それでたぶん<line-break>アカデミーに許してもらえると<line-break>思うんだけど…。<end>"
EN: "If I travel with you, <value:$28>, something amazing is bound to happen again. And then the Academy will probably forgive me this time too... I think.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>와 함께 여행하면 분명 또 굉장한 일이 생기겠지. 이번에도 그걸로 아카데미에서 용서해 줄 거라고 생각하는데….<end>"

## 1920065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたぞ！　ユーリスだ。　　<line-break>捕まえろ！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "There! It's Julls! Get her!<end>"
KO: "있다! 유리스다. 잡아라!<end>"

## 1920066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…って、今は<line-break>逃げ出す方が先かもね。<end>"
EN: "Then again, getting away may have to come first.<end>"
KO: "…라고 해도 지금은 도망치는 게 먼저일지도 모르겠네.<end>"

## 1920067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそいなぁ、<value:$28>。<line-break>まさか、忘れちゃったんじゃ<line-break>ないよなぁ。<end>"
EN: "You're late, <value:$28>. You didn't forget, did you?<end>"
KO: "늦네, <value:$28>. 설마 잊은 건 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1920068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　ようやく、みんなを説得して<line-break>自由に冒険できるようになったって<line-break>言うのにぃ！<line-break>何やってるんだよぉ、<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Come on! I finally won everyone over, and now I'm free to go adventuring! What's taking you so long, <value:$28>...?<end>"
KO: "정말! 겨우 모두를 설득해서 자유롭게 모험할 수 있게 됐는데! 뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1920069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が来てくれないと<line-break>冒険なんて、できないんだぞ…。<end>"
EN: "I can't go adventuring if you don't come, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 와 주지 않으면 모험 같은 건 할 수 없다고….<end>"

## 1920070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　ボク、はじめて冒険の旅に<line-break>出たときは、ひとりでも<line-break>やっていけるって思ってたのに…。<line-break>今では、<value:$28>が<line-break>いないと、旅に出るのが<line-break>なんだか、恐くなってる…。<end>"
EN: "That's strange. When I first set out on an adventure, I thought I'd be fine on my own... But now, the thought of setting off without you is a little frightening, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "어라? 난 처음 모험 여행을 떠났을 땐 혼자서도 잘할 수 있다고 생각했는데…. 지금은 <value:$28>가 없으면 여행을 떠나는 게 왠지 무서워졌어….<end>"

## 1920071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつのまにか、<line-break>ボクにとっての<line-break>冒険の旅っていうのは、<line-break><value:$28>と<line-break>一緒にいることに<line-break>なっちゃたんだな…。<end>"
EN: "Somewhere along the way, going on adventures came to mean being with you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "어느새 내게 모험 여행이라는 건 <value:$28>과 함께 있는 게 되어 버렸네….<end>"

## 1920072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はやく来てよぉ<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Hurry up and get here, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "빨리 와 줘, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1920073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、<value:$28>のバカ！<line-break>ボクをこんなに不安にさせて！<line-break>嫌いになっちゃうぞ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, you idiot, <value:$28>! Making me worry like this! I'm going to start hating you!<end>"
KO: "정말, <value:$28> 바보! 날 이렇게 걱정하게 하고! 싫어질 거야!<end>"

## 1920074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あれ！？<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "W-what!? <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "어, 어라!? <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1920075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あはははは…。<line-break>今の、もしかして聞いてた？<end>"
EN: "Ah... ahahaha... Did you happen to hear all that?<end>"
KO: "아, 아하하하…. 방금 거 혹시 들었어?<end>"

## 1920076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、さっきのは、<line-break>その、間違いだから。<line-break>…ゴメン。<end>"
EN: "Um, about what I said just now... that was a mistake. Sorry.<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, 아까 건 그, 실수였어. …미안.<end>"

## 1920077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと。<end>"
EN: "Thanks.<end>"
KO: "고마워.<end>"

## 1920078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、でも、元はと言えば<line-break><value:$28>が約束の時間に<line-break>来ないのが原因だったんだよ。<line-break>ボク、すっごく長い間、<line-break>ここで、待ってたんだから。<end>"
EN: "But this is all your fault for not arriving at the time we agreed on, <value:$28>. I've been waiting here for ages!<end>"
KO: "아, 하지만 애초에 <value:$28>이 약속 시간에 안 온 게 원인이야. 나 정말 오랫동안 여기서 기다렸다고.<end>"

## 1920079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？<line-break>まだ約束の時間になってない？<line-break>あれ？<line-break>あの、約束の時間って<line-break>いつだったっけ？<end>"
EN: "What? It's not time yet? Wait... what time did we agree on?<end>"
KO: "어? 아직 약속 시간이 안 됐다고? 어라? 그 약속 시간이 언제였더라?<end>"

## 1920080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あは、あはははは…。<line-break>ゴメン、ボクが勘違いしてたみたい。<line-break>　<line-break>ボク、少しでも早く<line-break><value:$28>と冒険の旅に<line-break>出たくて、うずうずしてたからさ。<end>"
EN: "Ah... ahahaha... Sorry, I suppose I got the time wrong. I was just so eager to set off on an adventure with you as soon as possible, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아하, 아하하하…. 미안, 내가 착각했나 봐. 조금이라도 빨리 <value:$28>과 모험을 떠나고 싶어서 몸이 근질거렸거든.<end>"

## 1920081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>行こうか！<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>. Shall we go?<end>"
KO: "그럼, <value:$28>. 가자!<end>"

## 1920082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや～、はやいもんだよねぇ。　　<line-break>もう、バイアシオン大陸なんて<line-break>ちっちゃくて見えないわ。<line-break>あんな、ちっぽけな陸の上で<line-break>あたしたちは戦ってたんだよね。<line-break>ここからこっちは自分の国だって。<end>"
EN: "Well, that was quick, wasn't it? The Vyashion Continent's already so tiny I can't even see it. And to think we fought on that little scrap of land, saying, “Everything from here on is our country.”<end>"
KO: "이야~ 빠르네. 벌써 바이아시온 대륙은 작아서 안 보일 정도야. 저 조그만 땅 위에서 우리는 싸우고 있었던 거지. 여기서부터는 자기 나라라고 말하면서.<end>"

## 1920083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ディンガルとロストールが<line-break>仲よくなっちゃったから、<line-break>戦争はもうおしまい。<line-break>これから、バイアシオンは<line-break>ひとつになっていくんだろうね。<end>"
EN: "But now that Dyneskal and Rostorl have made peace, the war's over. I suppose Vyashion will gradually become one.<end>"
KO: "하지만 딘갈과 로스톨이 사이좋아졌으니 전쟁은 이제 끝이야. 앞으로 바이아시온은 하나가 되어 가겠지.<end>"

## 1920084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ってなると、あたしたちみたいな<line-break>戦うことが仕事っていう物騒な連中は<line-break>出て行くしかないよねぇ、やっぱ。<end>"
EN: "Which means dangerous sorts like us, the ones who fight for a living, have no choice but to move on.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 우리처럼 싸우는 게 직업인 위험한 녀석들은 결국 떠날 수밖에 없겠네.<end>"

## 1920085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや～、力を持ちすぎた者の宿命って<line-break>やつですか？　しょうがないよね。<end>"
EN: "Ah, the fate of those with too much power. Is that what this is? Well, it can't be helped.<end>"
KO: "이야~, 힘을 너무 많이 가진 자의 숙명이라는 건가요? 어쩔 수 없죠.<end>"

## 1920086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす。<line-break>あたしと一緒にするなって　　　　　<line-break>顔したって、ダメだよん。<line-break>なんてったって、<value:$28>は<line-break>世界を救った最強の勇者様だもんね。<line-break>要するに、一番物騒だってこと。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Don't give me that look, as if you're nothing like me. It won't work. After all, <value:$28> is the mightiest Hero, savior of the world. In other words, the most dangerous one of all.<end>"
KO: "킥킥. 나랑 같은 취급 하지 말라는 표정을 지어도 소용없어. 어쨌든 <value:$28>는 세계를 구한 최강의 용사님이잖아. 한마디로 제일 위험한 사람이란 뜻이지.<end>"

## 1920087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いいじゃないですか？<line-break>英雄って言われてたネメア様だって<line-break>またどっかに行っちゃったし。<line-break>こうなったら、あたしたちも<line-break>次の大陸に渡って、国のひとつや<line-break>ふたつ手に入れてみようじゃん？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong with that? Even Lord Nemea, who was hailed as a hero, has gone off somewhere again. So why don't we cross over to the next continent and take a country or two for ourselves?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 괜찮지 않아요? 영웅이라 불리던 네메아 님도 또 어디론가 가 버렸고. 이왕 이렇게 된 거 우리도 다음 대륙으로 건너가 나라 하나둘쯤 손에 넣어 보자고요?<end>"

## 1920088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、別に国なんてどうでもいいよ。　　<line-break>うん。あたしは<value:$28>と<line-break>一緒に行くだけだから。<line-break>あたしはただ、<value:$28>と<line-break>一緒にいれば、ずっと笑って<line-break>いられるんじゃないかって思っただけ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I don't really care about countries. I'm just going with <value:$28>. I only thought that if I stayed with <value:$28>, maybe I could go on laughing forever.<end>"
KO: "아, 나라는 별로 상관없어. 응. 난 그냥 <value:$28>와 같이 갈 뿐이야. 난 그저 <value:$28>와 함께 있으면 계속 웃고 있을 수 있을 것 같다고 생각했어.<end>"

## 1920089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心から笑うことができるあたしを<line-break>見つけられるんじゃないかってね。<end>"
EN: "I thought maybe I'd find the me who can laugh from the heart.<end>"
KO: "진심으로 웃을 수 있는 나를 찾을 수 있지 않을까 해서.<end>"

## 1920090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "빈칸<end>"

## 1920091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、元気出していこう～！<line-break>次の大陸があたしたちをよんでる～！<end>"
EN: "Come on, let's keep our spirits up! The next continent is calling us!<end>"
KO: "자, 기운 내서 가자~! 다음 대륙이 우리를 부르고 있어~!<end>"

## 1920092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日もいいお天気ね。<line-break>よかったわ、晴れてくれて。　<line-break>　<line-break>雲がほとんど見えない<line-break>きれいな青空。<line-break>いい船旅になりそうね。<end>"
EN: "Lovely weather again today. I'm so glad it cleared up. There's hardly a cloud in that beautiful blue sky. It should be a wonderful voyage.<end>"
KO: "오늘도 날씨가 좋네. 맑아서 다행이야. 구름이 거의 없는 맑고 푸른 하늘. 좋은 뱃길이 되겠어.<end>"

## 1920093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争が終わって３年。<line-break>ようやくリベルダムも、<line-break>元の姿に戻ってきたわ。　　<line-break>ロストール王家に加えて<line-break>ディンガル帝国も、<line-break>町の復興を援助してくれて…。<end>"
EN: "It's been three years since the war ended, and Liberdam is finally beginning to look like itself again. The Dyneskal Empire has joined the Rostorl Royal House in helping to rebuild the city as well.<end>"
KO: "전쟁이 끝난 지 3년. 드디어 리벨덤도 원래 모습으로 돌아오고 있어. 로스톨 왕가에 더해 딩갈 제국도 도시 복구를 지원해 주고 있고….<end>"

## 1920094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、復興をここまで進めたのは<line-break>何よりも、町の人たちの<line-break>故郷に対する想いの強さよね…。　<line-break>市民と非市民の隔たりがなくなった<line-break>リベルダムに続いて、ロセンの復興も<line-break>もうすぐ終わる。<end>"
EN: "But more than anything, we've come this far because the townspeople care so deeply for their home. Liberdam has done away with the divide between citizens and noncitizens, and now the restoration of Rocen is nearly complete as well.<end>"
KO: "그래도 복구가 여기까지 진척된 건 무엇보다 마을 사람들이 고향을 향해 품은 마음이 강했기 때문이겠지…. 시민과 비시민의 장벽이 사라진 리벨덤에 이어 로센의 복구도 이제 곧 끝나겠네.<end>"

## 1920095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうしたら、わたくし、<line-break>世界を回ってみたい。<line-break>いろいろな町を見てみたい。　<line-break>できれば、あの人と一緒に…。<end>"
EN: "Once it is, I'd like to travel the world. I want to see all sorts of towns. If I could, I'd like to go with that special someone...<end>"
KO: "그러면 저도 세계를 돌아보고 싶어요. 여러 도시를 보고 싶어요. 가능하다면 그 사람과 함께….<end>"

## 1920096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<line-break>こちらよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Over here.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 이쪽이야.<end>"

## 1920097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>１０分の遅刻よ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you're ten minutes late.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 10분 늦었어.<end>"

## 1920098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。　　　　　　　　<line-break>冗談よ、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>昨日、言っていた<line-break>ボゲーさんの家の修理を<line-break>手伝ってきたのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I'm only teasing, <value:$28>. You were helping repair Bogee's house, just as you said yesterday, weren't you?<end>"
KO: "후후. 농담이야, <value:$28>. 어제 말했던 보게 씨 집 수리를 도와주고 온 거지?<end>"

## 1920099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしも今来たばかりだもの。<line-break>気にしないで。<line-break>　<line-break>さぁ、甲板に上がりましょう。<line-break>今日はきっと、<line-break>きれいな景色が見えるわ。<end>"
EN: "I only just arrived myself, so don't worry. Come, let's go up on deck. I'm sure we'll have a beautiful view today.<end>"
KO: "나도 방금 왔으니 신경 쓰지 마. 자, 갑판으로 올라가자. 오늘은 분명 멋진 풍경을 볼 수 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1920100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いたいた。<line-break>待ってー。クリュセイスお姉ちゃん、<line-break><value:$28>お兄ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "There you are! Wait up, Khryuseis! <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, 여기 있었네. 기다려ー. 크류세이스 언니, <value:$28> 오빠.<end>"

## 1920101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、これ。<line-break>母ちゃんが、今日のお礼だって。　　<line-break>ふたりで食べてくれってさ。<end>"
EN: "Here, this is for you. Ma says it's to thank you for today. She said you two should share it.<end>"
KO: "자, 이거. 엄마가 오늘 답례래. 둘이서 먹으라고 하셨어.<end>"

## 1920102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、こんなにたくさん。<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>お母さんによろしく言ってね。<end>"
EN: "My, there's so much. Thank you. Give your mother my regards.<end>"
KO: "어머, 이렇게나 많이. 고마워. 어머니께 안부 전해 줘.<end>"

## 1920103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、<line-break>クリュセイスお姉ちゃんたち、　　　<line-break>いつも一緒にいるね。<line-break>今日もこれから、でーと？<end>"
EN: "You know, Khryuseis, you two are always together. Are you going on another da-a-ate today?<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 크류세이스 언니네는 늘 같이 다니네. 오늘도 이제부터 데이트야?<end>"

## 1920104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと、何を言っているの？<line-break>わたくしと<value:$28>は…。<end>"
EN: "W-wait, what are you saying? <value:$28> and I are...<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐, 무슨 말을 하는 거예요? 저와 <value:$28>는…<end>"

## 1920105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あかくなった！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh, you're blushing!<end>"
KO: "아, 빨개졌다!<end>"

## 1920106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、大人をからかうものでは<line-break>なくてよ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you shouldn't tease your elders!<end>"
KO: "어, 어른을 놀리면 안 돼요!<end>"

## 1920107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行きましょう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "가죠, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1920108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いってらっしゃーい！　　　　　　　<line-break>お幸せにー！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Have a good time! Be happy together!<end>"
KO: "잘 다녀와—! 행복해야 해—!<end>"

## 1920109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>これから向かうロセンの復旧も<line-break>もうすぐ終わるわ。<line-break>町は生きかえった…。<line-break>もう、わたくしたちがいなくても<line-break>大丈夫よね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... The restoration work in Rocen, where we're headed now, is almost finished. The town has come back to life. It will be all right without us now, won't it?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 앞으로 향할 로센의 복구도 곧 끝나. 마을은 되살아났어… 이제 우리가 없어도 괜찮겠지.<end>"

## 1920110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくし、もうしばらくしたら　<line-break>旅に出ようかと思うの。<line-break>　<line-break>世界を回って、<line-break>いろいろな町を見て回りたい…。<end>"
EN: "I've been thinking that, before long, I might set out on a journey. I'd like to travel the world and see all sorts of towns.<end>"
KO: "저, 조금만 더 지나면 여행을 떠날까 해요. 세계를 돌아다니며 여러 도시를 보고 싶어요….<end>"

## 1920111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのとき…　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>あなたも…<line-break>あなたも、わたくしと一緒に<line-break>行ってくれる…？<end>"
EN: "When I do... <value:$28>, would you... would you come with me?<end>"
KO: "그때… <value:$28>, 당신도… 당신도 저와 함께 가 주실래요…?<end>"

## 1920112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>…うれしい。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm... so happy.<end>"
KO: "고마워. …기뻐.<end>"

## 1920113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしは、まだ<line-break>世界のほんの一部しか知らない。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、<value:$28>が<line-break>一緒にいてくれれば、<line-break>どこに行っても平気よ。<end>"
EN: "I've still seen only a tiny part of the world. But if you're with me, <value:$28>, I know I'll be all right wherever we go.<end>"
KO: "저는 아직 세상의 아주 일부밖에 몰라요. 하지만 <value:$28>이 함께 있어 준다면 어디든 괜찮아요.<end>"

## 1920114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、<value:$28>。<line-break>これからも、わたくしの<line-break>そばにいて…。<end>"
EN: "So, <value:$28>... please stay by my side, from now on.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>. 앞으로도 제 곁에 있어 주세요….<end>"

## 1920115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふぅ。少し疲れたわね。<line-break>この辺りで、休憩ね。<end>"
EN: "Whew... I'm getting a little tired. I think I'll take a break.<end>"
KO: "…후우. 조금 피곤하네. 이쯤에서 쉬자.<end>"

## 1920116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれから、もう１年がたつのね。<line-break>あっという間だったわ。そう感じるのは<line-break>仕事仕事の連続だったからかしらね。<line-break>でも、荒れ果てた帝都も、<line-break>ようやく昔の姿を取り戻しつつある。<line-break>あと、もう一息よね。<end>"
EN: "It's already been a year since then. It passed in the blink of an eye. Perhaps it only feels that way because it's been one task after another. But even the ravaged imperial capital is finally beginning to look as it once did. We're almost there.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤로 벌써 1년이 지났네. 순식간이었어. 그렇게 느끼는 건 계속 일만 했기 때문일까. 그래도 황폐했던 제도도 드디어 예전 모습을 되찾아 가고 있어. 이제 한숨만 더 힘내면 돼.<end>"

## 1920117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼します、陛下。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Pardon me, Your Majesty.<end>"
KO: "실례하겠습니다, 폐하.<end>"

## 1920118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お入りなさい。<end>"
EN: "Come in.<end>"
KO: "들어오세요.<end>"

## 1920119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下、近衛将軍が面会を求めて　　<line-break>おられます。お通しして<line-break>よろしいですか？<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, the commander of the Imperial Guard requests an audience. Shall I show the commander in?<end>"
KO: "폐하, 근위장군께서 면회를 청하고 계십니다. 들여보내도 되겠습니까?<end>"

## 1920120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が呼んだのです。<line-break>すぐにお通ししなさい。<end>"
EN: "I sent for the commander. Show the commander in at once.<end>"
KO: "제가 부른 겁니다. 바로 들여보내세요.<end>"

## 1920121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かしこまりました。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "At once, Your Majesty.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다.<end>"

## 1920122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下、差し出がましいようですが、<line-break>最近はお休みになっておられない<line-break>ご様子。<line-break>どうか、ご自愛くださいますよう。<line-break>陛下がお倒れにでもなられては、<line-break>今の帝国は立ちゆきませぬ。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, forgive my presumption, but you do not seem to have been resting of late. Please, take care of yourself. If you were to collapse, the Empire as it stands could not carry on.<end>"
KO: "폐하, 외람된 말씀입니다만 최근 쉬지 못하고 계신 듯합니다. 부디 몸을 돌보시옵소서. 폐하께서 쓰러지기라도 하시면 지금의 제국은 버틸 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1920123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心得ています。<line-break>今日は早めに切り上げようと　　<line-break>思っていますから。<line-break>あなたにも、心配をかけますね。<end>"
EN: "I understand. I intend to finish early today. I'm sorry to have worried you as well.<end>"
KO: "알고 있습니다. 오늘은 일찍 마칠 생각입니다. 당신에게도 걱정을 끼치네요.<end>"

## 1920124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もったいないお言葉。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それでは、近衛将軍を<line-break>お呼びして参ります。<end>"
EN: "You honor me, Your Majesty. I shall fetch the commander of the Imperial Guard.<end>"
KO: "과분한 말씀입니다. 그럼 근위장군을 불러오겠습니다.<end>"

## 1920125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いします。<end>"
EN: "Please do.<end>"
KO: "부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1920126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ザギヴがディンガルを統治する。<line-break>ゾフォルの予言の通りになった。<line-break>私にとっては、呪われた予言…。<line-break>でも、今は違う。<line-break>大勢の人が、私を応援してくれる。<line-break>皇帝となった私を受けいれてくれる。<end>"
EN: "“Zagiv shall rule Dyneskal.” Zofor's prophecy has come to pass. To me, it was a cursed prophecy... But things are different now. So many people support me. They accept me as their emperor.<end>"
KO: "…자기브가 딘갈을 통치한다. 조포르의 예언대로 됐어. 나에게는 저주받은 예언이었지…. 하지만 지금은 달라. 많은 사람이 나를 응원해 줘. 황제가 된 나를 받아들여 줘.<end>"

## 1920127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、あの人が…<line-break>私を闇から救ってくれたあの人が、<line-break>私を支えていてくれる…。<end>"
EN: "And then there's the one who saved me from the darkness... always there for me.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그 사람이… 나를 어둠에서 구해 준 그 사람이 나를 지탱해 주고 있어…<end>"

## 1920128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "眠りにつこうとするとき、<line-break>今でも、ときどき恐くなる。<line-break>　<line-break>朝、目を覚ましたとき、<line-break>また、あの闇が私を覆ってしまう<line-break>のではないか、って。<end>"
EN: "Even now, I sometimes feel afraid as I'm falling asleep. Afraid that when I wake in the morning, the darkness will have swallowed me again.<end>"
KO: "잠들려고 할 때면 지금도 가끔 무서워져. 아침에 눈을 떴을 때 또 그 어둠이 나를 덮어 버리는 게 아닐까, 하고.<end>"

## 1920129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、もう大丈夫よね。<line-break>たとえ、闇にとらわれたとしても<line-break>私の騎士が救い出してくれるから。<line-break>私を絶望から救ってくれた<line-break><value:$28>が、いつも<line-break>そばにいてくれるのだから。<end>"
EN: "But I'll be all right now. Even if the darkness takes me, my knight will rescue me. <value:$28>, who saved me from despair, will always be by my side.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이제 괜찮아. 설령 어둠에 사로잡혀도 내 기사가 구해 줄 테니까. 나를 절망에서 구해 준 <value:$28>가 언제나 곁에 있어 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 1920130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "開いているわ。<line-break>入って。<end>"
EN: "It's open. Come in.<end>"
KO: "열려 있어. 들어와.<end>"

## 1920131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<line-break><value:$28>ったら。<line-break>　<line-break>私の部屋で、そんなことは<line-break>しなくていいって、<line-break>言わなかったかしら？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Oh, <value:$28>. Didn't I tell you there was no need for that in my chambers?<end>"
KO: "후후후. <value:$28>도 참. 내 방에서는 그런 거 안 해도 된다고 말하지 않았나?<end>"

## 1920132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、それでは、近衛将軍閣下。<line-break>私はこれから、帝都の巡察に<line-break>出かけます。<line-break>あなたには道中の護衛を命じます。<line-break>よろしいかしら？<end>"
EN: "Now then, Your Excellency, Commander of the Imperial Guard. I'm going out to inspect the imperial capital, and I order you to escort me while I'm out. Is that understood?<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼 근위장군 각하. 저는 지금부터 제도를 순찰하러 갑니다. 당신에게 길 안내와 호위를 명합니다. 괜찮겠죠?<end>"

## 1920133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。<line-break>では、行きましょう。<line-break>私のナイトさん。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Then let us be off, my knight.<end>"
KO: "후후. 그럼 가죠, 나의 기사님.<end>"

## 1920134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（<value:$28>。<line-break>　あなたがいてくれれば、<line-break>　私、大丈夫よね…。）<end>"
EN: "(<value:$28>... As long as you're here, I'll be all right, won't I?)<end>"
KO: "(<value:$28>. 네가 있어 주면 나, 괜찮겠지….)<end>"

## 1920135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ。なんでもないわ。<line-break>…ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. It's nothing... Thank you.<end>"
KO: "후후. 아무것도 아니야. …고마워.<end>"

## 1920136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、リベルダム、ロセンへの援助は<line-break>それぞれの要求通りの規模に<line-break>拡大する方針でよろしいですか？<end>"
EN: "Then shall we proceed with expanding our aid to Liberdam and Rocen to the levels each has requested?<end>"
KO: "그러면 리벨덤과 로센에 대한 지원은 각각의 요구대로 규모를 확대하는 방침으로 괜찮겠습니까?<end>"

## 1920137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。そのように<line-break>取りはからってください。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Please make the necessary arrangements.<end>"
KO: "네. 그렇게 처리해 주세요.<end>"

## 1920138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かまいませんね、お母様？<end>"
EN: "You have no objection, Mother?<end>"
KO: "괜찮으시죠, 어머니?<end>"

## 1920139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "援助のことは、そなたに任せている。<line-break>好きにするといい。<end>"
EN: "I have entrusted the matter of aid to you. Do as you see fit.<end>"
KO: "지원 문제는 그대에게 맡겼다. 원하는 대로 하도록 해.<end>"

## 1920140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃も、こうおっしゃっておられます。<line-break>よろしく、頼みましたよ。<end>"
EN: "You have heard the Queen. I leave it in your hands.<end>"
KO: "왕비께서도 이렇게 말씀하셨습니다. 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1920141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かしこまりました。<line-break>直ちに手配してまいります。<end>"
EN: "Understood. I shall make the arrangements at once.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 즉시 준비하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1920142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたも大胆なことをする。<line-break>財務官が青くなる様が目に浮かぶわ。<end>"
EN: "You are bold. I can already picture the treasurer turning pale.<end>"
KO: "그대도 대담한 짓을 하는군. 재무관의 얼굴이 파래지는 모습이 눈에 선하다.<end>"

## 1920143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、お母様。リベルダム、ロセンには<line-break>今のうちに恩を売った方がいい<line-break>ということはご存じのはずですわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Mother. You know it is best to place Liberdam and Rocen in our debt while we can.<end>"
KO: "어머, 어머님. 리벨덤과 로센에는 지금 미리 은혜를 베풀어 두는 편이 좋다는 건 알고 계시겠지요.<end>"

## 1920144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ。そなたが、売った恩の<line-break>見返りを取り戻してくるなどとは<line-break>とうてい期待できぬがな。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I can hardly expect you to ever collect on those favors.<end>"
KO: "후. 네가 베푼 은혜의 대가를 되찾아 오리라곤 도저히 기대할 수 없지만 말이다.<end>"

## 1920145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国内の利益だけを見ていては、　<line-break>これからのロストールは、<line-break>いや、この世界は治まらぬ。<line-break>そなたの思うようにするがいい。<end>"
EN: "If we look only to our own country's interests, we cannot govern the Rostorl of tomorrow―no, the world of tomorrow. Do as you think best.<end>"
KO: "국내의 이익만 바라봐서는 앞으로의 로스톨은, 아니 이 세계는 다스릴 수 없다. 네 뜻대로 하거라.<end>"

## 1920146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様…。<end>"
EN: "Mother...<end>"
KO: "어머님….<end>"

## 1920147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたという後継者がいるおかげで、<line-break>このままでは私も、当分の間<line-break>この座から降りられぬわ。<line-break>そなたが、いずこかの<line-break>優秀な貴公子でも連れてくるなら<line-break>話は別だがな。<end>"
EN: "With an heir like you, it seems I shall be unable to step down from this throne for some time yet. Unless, of course, you bring home some capable young prince from somewhere.<end>"
KO: "그대라는 후계자가 있는 탓에 이대로라면 나도 당분간 이 자리에서 내려올 수 없겠구나. 그대가 어디선가 훌륭한 귀공자라도 데려온다면 이야기가 다르겠지만.<end>"

## 1920148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お母様！<end>"
EN: "M-Mother!<end>"
KO: "어, 어머님!<end>"

## 1920149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、案ずるな。<line-break>そなたを、私と同じ目に<line-break>あわせるつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Do not worry. I have no intention of subjecting you to the same fate as mine.<end>"
KO: "후후, 걱정 마라. 그대를 나와 같은 꼴로 만들 생각은 없다.<end>"

## 1920150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、国内のことだけを<line-break>見ていてはダメ…。<line-break>　<line-break>戦争で、それぞれの国の関係は<line-break>大きく変わってしまったもの。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "From now on, I cannot look only at affairs within our borders... The war has greatly changed the relations between every country.<end>"
KO: "앞으로는 국내만 보고 있어선 안 돼…. 전쟁으로 각 나라의 관계가 크게 변했으니까.<end>"

## 1920151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いえ、もう国という考えに　　<line-break>縛られていては、いけない…<line-break>そんな気がする…。<line-break>こんなに大変な時代のこの国を<line-break>導く力が、ティアナにはあるの？<line-break>やっぱり、お母様の助けが…<end>"
EN: "No... I feel we can no longer allow ourselves to be bound by the very idea of nations... Does Tiana have the strength to guide this country through such troubled times? Perhaps I do need Mother's help after all...<end>"
KO: "…아니, 이제 나라라는 생각에 얽매여선 안 돼… 그런 느낌이 들어…. 이렇게 힘든 시대에 이 나라를 이끌 힘이 티아나에게 있을까? 역시 어머님의 도움이….<end>"

## 1920152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメよ、ティアナ。<line-break>いつまでもお母様に頼っていては　　<line-break>女王になる資格なんてないわ。<line-break>お母様はお母様。私は私。<line-break>私に期待してくれる人たちのためにも<line-break>私は成長しなければならないわ。<end>"
EN: "No, Tiana. If you keep relying on Mother forever, you have no right to become queen. Mother is Mother, and I am myself. I must grow, for the sake of everyone who believes in me.<end>"
KO: "안 돼, 티아나. 언제까지나 어머님께 의지해선 여왕이 될 자격이 없어. 어머님은 어머님, 나는 나야. 나를 기대해 주는 사람들을 위해서라도 나는 성장해야 해.<end>"

## 1920153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、不安。…不安なの。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテス様は旅に出てしまわれたし、<line-break>あの方も、しばらく会いに来て<line-break>くださらない。<end>"
EN: "But I'm afraid... I am. Lord Xenetes has gone on a journey, and that person has not come to see me in some time.<end>"
KO: "하지만 불안해요… 불안합니다. 제네테스 님은 여행을 떠나셨고, 그분도 한동안 만나러 와 주지 않으세요.<end>"

## 1920154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこに行ってしまわれたの…？<line-break>早く、会いたい…。<end>"
EN: "Where have you gone...? I want to see you soon...<end>"
KO: "어디로 가 버리신 거야…? 빨리 만나고 싶어….<end>"

## 1920155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　この音は…？<line-break>もしかすると…。<end>"
EN: "Hm? That sound... Could it be...?<end>"
KO: "어? 이 소리는…? 설마….<end>"

## 1920156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님!<end>"

## 1920157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ。<value:$28>様ったら<line-break>正面から入ってこられれば<line-break>よろしいのに…。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Oh, <value:$28>. You could simply come in through the front...<end>"
KO: "후후. <value:$28>님도 참, 정면으로 들어오시면 될 텐데….<end>"

## 1920158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、そんなことよりも、<line-break>どこに行ってらしたのです？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様が<line-break>しばらく会いに来てくださらないから<line-break>私、不安だったのですよ。<end>"
EN: "No, more importantly, where have you been? You hadn't come to see me for so long that I was worried, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아니, 그것보다 어디에 다녀오신 겁니까? <value:$28> 님이 한동안 만나러 와 주지 않으셔서 저, 불안했어요.<end>"

## 1920159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　町はずれの湖に<line-break>渡り鳥の群が来ているのですか？<end>"
EN: "What? A flock of migratory birds has come to the lake outside town?<end>"
KO: "네? 마을 외곽 호수에 철새 떼가 왔다고요?<end>"

## 1920160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうなのですか。<line-break>でも、ティアナには…<end>"
EN: "I-is that so? But Tiana cannot...<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가요. 하지만 티아나는….<end>"

## 1920161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が<line-break>連れていってくださるのですか？<line-break>　<line-break>うれしいのですけれど、<line-break>こんな時間に…。<end>"
EN: "You'll take me there, <value:$28>? I would be delighted, but at this hour...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이 데려가 주시는 건가요? 기쁘긴 하지만 이런 시간에….<end>"

## 1920162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強引なのですね、<line-break><value:$28>様って。<line-break>　<line-break>わかりました。それでは、<line-break>今すぐにティアナを<line-break>連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "You can be rather forceful, can't you, <value:$28>? Very well. Then take Tiana with you at once.<end>"
KO: "…정말 강하게 밀어붙이시는군요, <value:$28>님은. 알겠습니다. 그럼 지금 바로 티아나를 데려가 주세요.<end>"

## 1920163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナは見てみたいのです。<line-break>あなたが心を動かされるものを、<line-break>あなたと一緒に見ていきたい。<line-break>これからも、ずっと…。<end>"
EN: "Tiana wants to see them. I want to see the things that move your heart, together with you. From now on, forever...<end>"
KO: "티아나는 보고 싶어요. 당신의 마음을 움직이는 것을 당신과 함께 보고 싶습니다. 앞으로도 계속….<end>"

## 1920164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと、誰にも気づかれなかったよね。<line-break>よかった。昨日のうちから<line-break>準備しておいて。<end>"
EN: "Um, no one noticed me, right? Good. I'm glad I got everything ready yesterday.<end>"
KO: "저기, 아무도 눈치 못 챘지? 다행이다. 어제부터 준비해 둬.<end>"

## 1920165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしの生まれた町。<line-break>わたしとカフィンとレイヴンと<line-break>そして、あの人とで、取り戻した町。<line-break>リベルダムやロストールみたいに<line-break>おっきくて、にぎやかじゃないけど、<line-break>わたしの大好きなオズワルド。<end>"
EN: "The town where I was born. The town Coffin, Raven, that person, and I won back together. It isn't as big or bustling as Liberdam or Rostorl, but Oswald is the town I love.<end>"
KO: "내가 태어난 마을. 나와 카핀과 레이븐, 그리고 그 사람과 함께 되찾은 마을. 리벨덤이나 로스톨처럼 크고 번화하진 않지만 내가 정말 좋아하는 오즈월드.<end>"

## 1920166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、みんな知らない。<line-break>カフィンとレイヴンが、命をかけて<line-break>ソウルを取り戻してくれたことを。<line-break>わたしだけが命の恩人で、<line-break>カフィンたちは記憶の外…。<line-break>そんなの、もう耐えられない。<end>"
EN: "But no one knows. No one knows that Coffin and Raven risked their lives to recover the Soul. I'm the only one remembered as the savior, while Coffin and the others have been forgotten... I can't bear it anymore.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아무도 몰라. 카핀과 레이븐이 목숨을 걸고 소울을 되찾아 준 걸. 나만 생명의 은인이고 카핀들은 기억 밖에 있어…. 그런 건 이제 못 견디겠어.<end>"

## 1920167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急に旅に出るなんて<line-break>書き置きを見たら<line-break>びっくりするだろうな。<line-break>ごめんなさい、<line-break>お父様、お母様。<line-break>でも、わたしは…。<end>"
EN: "They'll be so surprised when they find my note saying I've suddenly left on a journey. I'm sorry, Father. Mother. But I...<end>"
KO: "갑자기 여행을 떠난다는 메모를 보면 놀라시겠지. 죄송해요, 아버지, 어머니. 그래도 저는….<end>"

## 1920168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>おはようございます！<end>"
EN: "Oh, good morning, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 좋은 아침이에요!<end>"

## 1920169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>本当にわたし、旅に連れていって<line-break>もらっていいんですか？<line-break>その、わたし<line-break><value:$28>さんに<line-break>迷惑かけちゃうだろうし…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, um, <value:$28>... Are you sure you don't mind taking me with you? I mean, I'll probably only cause you trouble, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, <value:$28> 씨. 정말 저도 여행에 데려가 주셔도 괜찮은가요? 그게, 제가 <value:$28> 씨께 폐를 끼칠 것 같아서….<end>"

## 1920170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、あの、<line-break><value:$28>さんが誘ってくれて<line-break>とってもうれしいんです。　　　　　　<line-break>カフィンもレイヴンも、<line-break><value:$28>さんと一緒だったら<line-break>安心して見守ってくれると思いますし。<end>"
EN: "No, I mean, I'm so happy you invited me, <value:$28>. And if I'm traveling with you, <value:$28>, I think Coffin and Raven can watch over me without worrying.<end>"
KO: "아뇨, 저, <value:$28> 씨가 권해 줘서 정말 기뻐요. 카핀도 레이븐도 <value:$28> 씨와 함께라면 안심하고 지켜봐 줄 것 같고요.<end>"

## 1920171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、お父様とお母様にも内緒で<line-break>こうして出てきちゃったんです。<line-break>　<line-break>その、本当にわたしなんかで<line-break>いいんでしたら、お願いします。<line-break>一緒に連れていってください！<end>"
EN: "That's why I slipped away without even telling Father and Mother. So, if you're really sure you want someone like me... please, take me with you!<end>"
KO: "그래서 아버님과 어머님께도 비밀로 하고 이렇게 나와 버렸어요. 그, 정말 저 같은 사람도 괜찮다면 부탁드릴게요. 함께 데려가 주세요!<end>"

## 1920172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたし、がんばりますから！<end>"
EN: "I'll do my very best!<end>"
KO: "저, 열심히 할 테니까요!<end>"

## 1920173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ええ。実はお父様とお母様の　　<line-break>ことは心残りなんです。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、旅に出るって言っちゃったら<line-break>絶対ダメって言われるから…！<end>"
EN: "...Yes. I do feel bad about leaving Father and Mother behind. But if I told them I was going on a journey, they'd never let me go!<end>"
KO: "…네. 사실 아버지와 어머니가 마음에 걸려요. 하지만 여행을 떠난다고 말하면 절대 안 된다고 하실 테니까…!<end>"

## 1920174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お父様…お母様。<end>"
EN: "F-Father... Mother.<end>"
KO: "아, 아버님… 어머님.<end>"

## 1920175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…その<line-break>これは…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, um... well, this is...<end>"
KO: "아, 저기… 그게….<end>"

## 1920176
