jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920177

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…어!?<end>"

## 1920178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様は行ってもいいって<line-break>おっしゃってるのよ、ノエル。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Your father is saying you may go, Noel.<end>"
KO: "아버지는 가도 좋다고 하셨어, 노엘.<end>"

## 1920179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…。<end>"
EN: "But...<end>"
KO: "하지만….<end>"

## 1920180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親を甘く見るものじゃないよ、ノエル。<line-break>お前がずっと、冒険の旅に出たがって<line-break>いるのはお見通しだ。<line-break>お前は、すぐに思っていることが<line-break>顔に出るからね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You should never underestimate your parents, Noel. We have always known how badly you wanted to set out on an adventure. Your thoughts show on your face at once.<end>"
KO: "부모를 얕보지 마라, 노엘. 네가 줄곧 모험 여행을 떠나고 싶어 했다는 건 다 알고 있어. 넌 생각하는 게 금세 얼굴에 드러나니까.<end>"

## 1920181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってきなさい、ノエル。<line-break>お前が信頼している<line-break>この方と一緒に。<end>"
EN: "Go, Noel. Go with the person you trust.<end>"
KO: "다녀오너라, 노엘. 네가 신뢰하는 이분과 함께.<end>"

## 1920182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1920184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こんなにたくさん…。<end>"

## 1920185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのかわり、これだけは約束しなさい。<line-break>必ず、私たちの元へ、帰ってくると。<line-break>　<end>"

KO: "대신 이것만은 약속해. 반드시 우리 곁으로 돌아오겠다고.<end>"

## 1920186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes!<end>"
KO: "네, 네!<end>"

## 1920187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、お父様、お母様。<line-break>行ってきます！<end>"

## 1920188

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>行きましょう！<end>"

KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 갑시다!<end>"

## 1920189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天気も上々だし、<line-break>これだけ海が穏やかなら　　　　<line-break>問題なさそうね。<line-break>今日こそ、父さんが遺した財宝を<line-break>引き上げてみせるわ。<end>"
EN: "The weather's fine, and with the sea this calm, we shouldn't have any trouble. Today's the day we finally bring up the treasure Father left behind.<end>"
KO: "날씨도 좋고 바다도 이만큼 잔잔하면 문제없겠네. 오늘이야말로 아버지가 남긴 보물을 건져 올리겠어.<end>"

## 1920190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>解読した父さんの暗号によると、<line-break>この辺りで目印が見つかるはず。<end>"
EN: "According to Father's code, the one <value:$28> and I deciphered together, we should find the marker somewhere around here.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>과 함께 풀어낸 아버지의 암호대로라면, 이 근처에서 표식을 찾을 수 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1920191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたたち、<line-break>気合い入れて探すように<line-break>見張りの連中に言ってきな！<end>"
EN: "You lot, go tell the lookouts to keep their eyes peeled!<end>"
KO: "너희들, 정신 바짝 차리고 찾으라고 감시하는 녀석들에게 말하고 와!<end>"

## 1920192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へい、おかしら！<line-break>直ちに！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Aye, Captain! Right away!<end>"
KO: "예, 두목! 즉시!<end>"

## 1920193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたのおかげで、父さんの…<line-break>カンダカンの遺宝を見つけられそう。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. Thanks to you, it looks like we'll find Father's... Kandakan's treasure.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 네 덕분에 아버지의… 칸다칸의 유물을 찾을 수 있을 것 같아.<end>"

## 1920194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、謙遜しなくていいのよ。<line-break>私だけじゃ、あの暗号を解くのは<line-break>無理だったから。<line-break>私には、父さんの思い出がつまった<line-break>首飾りを、うっかり地面に落とす<line-break>なんてこと、できなかったもの。<end>"
EN: "Heh, no need to be modest. I could never have deciphered that code alone. I couldn't very well have dropped the necklace with all my memories of Father on the ground by accident myself, could I?<end>"
KO: "후후, 겸손할 필요 없어. 나 혼자서는 그 암호를 푸는 게 무리였으니까. 아버지와의 추억이 담긴 목걸이를 일부러 바닥에 떨어뜨리는 일은 나로선 할 수 없었거든.<end>"

## 1920195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談よ。気にしないで。<line-break>あの首飾り、しまったままで　　　　<line-break>忘れてたくらいだから。<line-break>父さんは肌身はなさず持ってろって<line-break>言ってたけど、私はあんな派手な<line-break>首飾りなんてする柄じゃないからね。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. Don't worry about it. I'd left that necklace tucked away and practically forgotten about it. Father told me to keep it with me at all times, but I'm not the sort to wear anything that gaudy.<end>"
KO: "농담이야. 신경 쓰지 마. 그 목걸이는 넣어 둔 채 잊고 있었을 정도니까. 아버지는 늘 몸에 지니라고 했지만 난 그런 화려한 목걸이를 할 타입도 아니고.<end>"

## 1920196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が見つけて<line-break>なかったら、中に隠されてた<line-break>暗号文ごと箱の中で眠っていたわ。<end>"
EN: "If <value:$28> hadn't found it, it would still be lying in that box, along with the coded message hidden inside.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 찾아내지 않았으면 안에 숨겨진 암호문째 상자 속에서 잠들어 있었겠지.<end>"

## 1920197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当は私、<line-break>遺宝なんてどうでもいいのよ。<line-break>…ちょっとは興味あるけどね。<line-break>でも、私は何より、あなたが<line-break>私のところに来てくれたことが<line-break>一番うれしいの。<end>"
EN: "To tell the truth, I don't really care about the treasure. Well... I am a little curious. But more than anything, I'm glad you came to me.<end>"
KO: "사실 나는 유보 따윈 아무래도 좋아. …조금은 관심 있지만. 그래도 무엇보다 네가 나를 찾아와 준 게 제일 기뻐.<end>"

## 1920198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたには言ってあるわよね。<line-break>私はずっと求めていた。<line-break>疲れたとき、そっと寄りかかれる人。<line-break>くじけそうになったとき<line-break>私のことを励ましてくれる人。<line-break>父さんみたいな人…。<end>"
EN: "I've told you before, haven't I? I've always wanted someone I could lean on when I was tired. Someone who would encourage me when I felt I couldn't go on. Someone like Father...<end>"
KO: "당신에게 말했었지. 난 계속 원해 왔어. 지쳤을 때 조용히 기대어 쉴 수 있는 사람. 꺾일 것 같을 때 나를 격려해 주는 사람. 아버지 같은 사람….<end>"

## 1920199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "年下のあなたに、<line-break>こんなことを言うなんて、<line-break>弱い女だと思うかもしれないけど、<line-break>これが、偽りのない私。<line-break>あなたにだけ見せられる本当の私。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You might think me a weak woman for saying this to someone younger than I am, but this is who I truly am―the real me I can show only to you.<end>"
KO: "나보다 어린 당신에게 이런 말을 하면 약한 여자라고 생각할지도 모르지만, 이게 거짓 없는 나야. 당신에게만 보여줄 수 있는 진짜 나.<end>"

## 1920200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にここまで言わせたんだから、<line-break><value:$28>、これからもずっと<line-break>私と一緒に海に出てくれるわよね？<end>"
EN: "After making me say all that, <value:$28>, you'll keep sailing with me from now on, won't you?<end>"
KO: "내가 여기까지 말했으니, <value:$28>, 앞으로도 계속 나와 함께 바다로 나가 줄 거지?<end>"

## 1920201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、ありがとう。<line-break>頼りにしてるから<line-break>覚悟してね。<end>"
EN: "Heh, thank you. I'm counting on you, so you'd better be ready.<end>"
KO: "후후, 고마워. 의지할 테니 각오해.<end>"

## 1920202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかしら！　見えましたぜ！　　<line-break>暗号にあったとおりです。<line-break>　<line-break>太陽の光が海面に妙に強く<line-break>反射しているところがあります。<end>"
EN: "Captain! There it is! Just like the code said. The sunlight's reflecting unusually brightly off one spot on the water.<end>"
KO: "두목! 보입니다! 암호에 적힌 그대로예요. 태양빛이 해수면에 이상할 정도로 강하게 반사되는 곳이 있습니다.<end>"

## 1920203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやったね。<line-break>暗号によると、海面が光ってるのは<line-break>そんなに長い時間じゃないよ。<line-break>すぐに財宝を引き上げる<line-break>準備にかかりな！<end>"
EN: "Good work. According to the code, the water won't shine for long. Start preparing to haul up the treasure, now!<end>"
KO: "잘했어. 암호에 따르면 해면이 빛나는 시간은 그리 길지 않아. 바로 보물을 건질 준비를 시작해!<end>"

## 1920204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へい、おかしら！<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Aye, Captain!<end>"
KO: "예, 두목!<end>"

## 1920205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>父さんの遺宝にご対面といこうか！<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>. Let's go see Father's treasure for ourselves!<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>. 아버지의 유보를 직접 만나 보러 가자!<end>"

## 1920206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、待ちなさいよ<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Wait just a moment, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 기다려 <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1920207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このアタクシに何も言わずに<line-break>出ていくなんて、あなたに<line-break>許されていると思っていて？<end>"
EN: "Do you really think you can leave without saying a word to me?<end>"
KO: "이 아타쿠시에게 아무 말도 없이 떠나다니, 당신에게 그런 게 허락됐다고 생각하는 거야?<end>"

## 1920208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束は果たした、ですって？<line-break>世界は驚きに満ちていることが　　　<line-break>わかってもらえてよかった？<line-break>この程度ではまだまだ足りなくてよ。<line-break>アタクシのように博識のエルフは<line-break>これぐらいのことでは驚けないわ。<end>"
EN: "You say you've kept your promise? Glad you've shown me that the world's full of surprises, are you? This is nowhere near enough. An Elf as knowledgeable as I am can't be surprised by something like this.<end>"
KO: "약속을 지켰다고? 세계가 놀라움으로 가득하다는 걸 알게 돼서 다행이라고? 이 정도론 아직 멀었어. 이 몸처럼 박식한 엘프는 이런 정도로는 놀라지 않는다고.<end>"

## 1920209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "納得するまで、ついて行くわよ！<line-break>たかが数十年の冒険につきあうくらい<line-break>エルフのアタクシにはなんでもないわ！<end>"
EN: "I'm coming with you until I'm satisfied! A mere few decades of adventure are nothing to an Elf like me!<end>"
KO: "납득할 때까지 따라갈 거야! 고작 수십 년 모험에 함께하는 정도는 엘프인 이 몸에게 아무것도 아니라고!<end>"

## 1920210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、今度のことで、<line-break>アタクシも少しは<line-break>下等生物のことを見直した…<end>"
EN: "Well, after all this, perhaps I've gained a little respect for lesser creatures...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이번 일로 이 몸도 조금은 하등생물을 다시 보게 됐어…<end>"

## 1920211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんてワケがないでしょ！<line-break>やっぱり下等生物は下等生物だったわ。<line-break>救いようがないったら、ありゃしない。<line-break>だから、もうしばらくアタクシがそばに<line-break>いてあげてよ。アタクシのありあまる<line-break>慈愛の精神に感謝なさい！<end>"
EN: "As if! Lesser creatures are still lesser creatures. You're utterly beyond saving. So I shall graciously remain by your side a little longer. Be grateful for my boundless compassion!<end>"
KO: "…그럴 리가 없잖아! 역시 하등생물은 하등생물이었어. 구제할 길이 없다니까. 그러니 당분간은 내가 곁에 있어 주겠어. 넘쳐나는 내 자애에 감사하도록 해!<end>"

## 1920212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、行くわよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Now then, let's be off, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1920213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml

KO: "뭐? 무슨 말 했어, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1920214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、こら、<value:$28>！<line-break>アタクシの断りなしに先に行くのは<line-break>許さなくてよーーーーーーーーー！<end>"
EN: "Hey! <value:$28>! You are not going on ahead without my permissioooooon!<end>"
KO: "아, 잠깐, <value:$28>! 내 허락도 없이 먼저 가는 건 용서 못 해ーーーーーーーーー!<end>"

## 1920215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしてね、それから…。<end>"
EN: "And then, after that...<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이야, 그다음은….<end>"

## 1920216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、聞いてるの？　旅の最中<line-break>何があったのか話してくれって<line-break>頼んだのは、そっちでしょお。<end>"
EN: "Come on, are you even listening? You're the one who asked me to tell you what happened on the journey!<end>"
KO: "벌써 듣고 있는 거야? 여행 중에 무슨 일이 있었는지 말해 달라고 한 건 그쪽이잖아아.<end>"

## 1920217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>これから、どうするつもりですか？<line-break>　<line-break>どこかでのんびりと暮らすのなら<line-break>転送機で送ってあげますが。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. What do you intend to do now? If you'd like to settle down somewhere and take life easy, I can teleport you there.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 이제 어떻게 할 생각입니까? 어디선가 느긋하게 살 생각이라면 전송기로 보내 드릴 수도 있습니다.<end>"

## 1920218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言えば、あなたは旅の商人の<line-break>子供でしたね。腰を落ち着けるのは<line-break>性に合わないかもしれません。<line-break>でも、かと言って<line-break>お父さんのように旅の商人に<line-break>なることも難しいですかね。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, you're a traveling merchant's child, aren't you? Settling down may not be in your nature. Then again, becoming a traveling merchant like your father may not be so easy either.<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 당신은 여행 상인의 자식이었군요. 한곳에 정착하는 게 성미에 맞지 않을지도 모르겠어요. 그렇다고 아버지처럼 여행 상인이 되는 것도 어렵겠죠.<end>"

## 1920219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今のあなたは、お父さんと旅を<line-break>続けていたころと比べると別人のように<line-break>変わってしまったでしょうし。<line-break>ある意味でそれが<line-break>無限のソウルを持つことの代償です。<end>"
EN: "After all, you're practically a different person now from who you were when you traveled with your father. In a sense, that's the price of having the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "지금의 당신은 아버지와 여행하던 때와 비교하면 완전히 다른 사람처럼 변했을 테니까요. 어떤 의미에서는 그것이 무한의 소울을 가진 대가입니다.<end>"

## 1920220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>全然変わってないよ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> hasn't changed at all!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 전혀 변하지 않았어!<end>"

## 1920221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break><value:$28>だもん。　　<line-break>　<line-break>私の大好きな<line-break><value:$28>のままだよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is still <value:$28>. Still the <value:$28> I love so much.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 <value:$28>이잖아. 내가 정말 좋아하는 <value:$28> 그대로야.<end>"

## 1920222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。ルルアンタ、<line-break>あなたの言うとおりです。<end>"
EN: "Yes. You're right, Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 루루안타, 당신 말이 맞아요.<end>"

## 1920223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以前と変わらず接してくれる<line-break>ルルアンタが、どれほど貴重な存在か…<line-break>あなたにはわかっていますよね。<end>"
EN: "You do understand how precious it is to have Lulluantha treat you just as she always has, don't you?<end>"
KO: "예전과 다름없이 대해 주는 루루안타가 얼마나 소중한 존재인지… 당신도 알고 있지요.<end>"

## 1920224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。年を取ると、話し方が<line-break>ついお説教っぽくなりがちですね。<line-break>許してください。<line-break>で、さきほどの話ですが、<line-break>これから、どうするつもりですか？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Age does have a way of making one sound rather preachy. Forgive me. Now, back to what we were discussing: what do you intend to do?<end>"
KO: "후훗. 나이를 먹으면 말투가 자꾸 훈계조가 되네요. 용서하세요. 그럼 아까 이야기인데, 이제부터 어떻게 하실 생각인가요?<end>"

## 1920225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また旅に出るのですか？<line-break>それは、いいですね。<end>"
EN: "You're setting out on another journey? That sounds splendid.<end>"
KO: "다시 여행을 떠나는 건가요? 그거 좋네요.<end>"

## 1920227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、ルルアンタのこと、<line-break>連れてってくれるの？<end>"
EN: "What? You'll take Lulluantha with you?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 루루안타를 데려가 줄 거야?<end>"

## 1920228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>旅ができるなんて、うれしい！<end>"
EN: "I'm so happy I get to travel with <value:$28> again!<end>"
KO: "다시 <value:$28>과 함께 여행할 수 있다니 기뻐!<end>"

## 1920229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタは、これからも<line-break><value:$28>に、<line-break>ありったけの笑顔をあげるから。<line-break>だから、ずっと一緒にいようね。<end>"
EN: "From now on, Lulluantha will give <value:$28> every smile she has. So let's stay together forever, all right?<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 앞으로도 <value:$28>에게 있는 힘껏 웃어 줄 거야. 그러니까 계속 함께 있자.<end>"

## 1920230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふっ、<line-break>時々は、ここにも顔を見せに<line-break>寄ってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Do stop by and show your faces here from time to time.<end>"
KO: "후후, 가끔은 여기에도 얼굴을 비추러 들러 주세요.<end>"

## 1920231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その時は、ぶさいくなネコちゃんにも<line-break>ちゃんとお土産、持ってくるね。<end>"
EN: "And when we do, I'll be sure to bring that ugly little kitty a present.<end>"
KO: "그때는 못생긴 고양이한테도 제대로 선물 가져올게.<end>"

## 1920232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、ふたりの新たな旅立ちを祝して。<line-break>手始めに、まずどこに<line-break>転送しましょうか？<end>"
EN: "Then, in celebration of the two of you setting out on a new journey, where shall I teleport you first?<end>"
KO: "그럼 두 사람의 새로운 출발을 축하하며. 우선 어디로 전송해 드릴까요?<end>"

## 1920233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さーん！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨~!<end>"

## 1920234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった。<line-break><value:$28>さんと<line-break>すぐに会うことができて。<line-break>あの、実は大変なことに<line-break>なってしまって。<line-break>話を聞いてもらえますか？<end>"
EN: "Thank goodness I found you so quickly, <value:$28>. Um, something terrible has happened. Would you hear me out?<end>"
KO: "다행이에요. <value:$28>님과 바로 만날 수 있어서. 저기, 사실 큰일이 생겼어요. 이야기 좀 들어 주실래요?<end>"

## 1920235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日起きてみたら<line-break>こんな手紙が置いてあって…。<line-break>読んでみてください。<end>"
EN: "When I woke up today, I found this letter waiting for me... Please read it.<end>"
KO: "오늘 일어나 보니 이런 편지가 놓여 있어서…. 읽어 봐 주세요.<end>"

## 1920236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　ノエル、あたしとレイヴンは　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　このあたりで、あなたと<line-break>　　　　別行動を取ることにしたわ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Noel, Raven and I have decided that this is where we'll part ways with you.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 나와 레이븐은 이쯤에서 너와 따로 움직이기로 했어.<end>"

## 1920237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　いつまでも、あなたと一緒に　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　いては、お互いのために<line-break>　　　　　　よくないと思うの。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I don't think it would be good for any of us to stay together indefinitely.<end>"
KO: "언제까지나 당신과 함께 있는 건 서로에게 좋지 않다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1920238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　ナーシェスもいなくなって、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　パーティーを解散するのに<line-break>　　　今がちょうどいい頃合いでしょ？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Now that Narscess is gone too, this seems like the right time to disband the party, don't you think?<end>"
KO: "나셰스도 없어졌고, 파티를 해산하기엔 지금이 딱 좋은 때잖아?<end>"

## 1920239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　そうそう、偶然だけど、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　同じ宿に<value:$28>が<line-break>　　　　　　泊まっているみたいよ。<line-break>　　　　一度、話してみたらどうかしら。<end>"
EN: "Oh, by the way, it seems <value:$28> happens to be staying at the same inn. Why don't you have a word?<end>"
KO: "아, 참. 우연이지만 같은 여관에 <value:$28>가 묵고 있는 것 같아. 한번 이야기해 보는 게 어때?<end>"

## 1920240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "빈칸<end>"

## 1920241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　もしかしたら、一緒に冒険の旅に<line-break>　　連れていってくれるかもしれないわね。<line-break>　　　　　　それじゃ、またね。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　カフィンより　<end>"
EN: "Maybe you'll take me along on an adventure. Well, see you again. From Coffin<end>"
KO: "어쩌면 함께 모험 여행에 데려가 줄지도 모르겠네. 그럼 또 봐. 카핀으로부터<end>"

## 1920242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、わたし、どうして<line-break>こんな事になってしまったのか　　<line-break>全然わからなくって…。<line-break>最初にいなくなっちゃったのは<line-break>ナーシェスなんです。わたしたちの<line-break>役目は終わったとか言って…。<end>"
EN: "I... I don't understand how any of this happened. Narscess was the first to leave. He said something about our work being done...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는 왜 이런 일이 됐는지 전혀 모르겠어요…. 처음 사라진 건 나셰스예요. 우리 역할은 끝났다면서….<end>"

## 1920243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、今朝起きてみたら<line-break>今度はカフィンもレイヴンも<line-break>いなかったんです。<end>"
EN: "Then I woke up this morning, and Coffin and Raven were gone too.<end>"
KO: "그래서 오늘 아침 일어나 보니 이번엔 카핀도 레이븐도 없었어요.<end>"

## 1920244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてなんだろう…。<line-break>やっぱり、わたしがドジだから<line-break>みんな嫌いになっちゃったのかな…。<line-break>わたし、これから<line-break>どうしたらいいんだろう…。<end>"
EN: "Why would they leave...? Maybe they all hate me because I'm so clumsy. What am I going to do now...?<end>"
KO: "왜 그런 걸까…. 역시 내가 덤벙대서 다들 날 싫어하게 된 걸까…. 나, 이제 어떻게 해야 하지….<end>"

## 1920245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　わたしを<line-break>連れていってくれるんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? You'll take me with you?<end>"
KO: "네? 저를 데려가 주시는 건가요?<end>"

## 1920246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…で、でも、いいんですか？<line-break>わたし、<value:$28>さんに<line-break>迷惑をかけちゃうかも…。<end>"
EN: "B-but are you sure? I might only be a burden to you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…그, 그래도 괜찮아요? 제가 <value:$28>님께 폐를 끼칠지도….<end>"

## 1920247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、いえ、その、嫌じゃないですよ！<line-break>すっごく、嬉しいんです。<line-break>嬉しいんですけど…<line-break>本当に、わたしが一緒で<line-break>いいんですか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, no, it's not that I don't want to! I'm so happy. I really am, but... are you sure you want me to come?<end>"
KO: "아, 아니, 싫은 건 아니에요! 정말 기뻐요. 기쁘긴 한데… 정말 제가 함께해도 괜찮나요?<end>"

## 1920248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それでは、<line-break>お言葉に甘えさせて<line-break>もらっちゃおうかな…。　　　　　<line-break>わたしがもっとしっかりするように<line-break>なれば、カフィンもレイヴンも<line-break>また会ってくれるかもしれないし。<end>"
EN: "W-well then, maybe I will take you up on your offer... And if I can learn to be more dependable, maybe Coffin and Raven will want to see me again.<end>"
KO: "그, 그럼 말씀에 기대 볼까…. 내가 좀 더 제대로 하게 되면 카핀도 레이븐도 다시 만나 줄지도 모르니까.<end>"

## 1920249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>わたし、一生懸命がんばりますから<line-break>これから、よろしくお願いします！<end>"
EN: "I'll do my very best, <value:$28>. I hope we'll get along!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 저 열심히 할 테니 앞으로 잘 부탁드려요!<end>"

## 1920250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだか、夢みたいです。<line-break>憧れの<value:$28>さんと一緒に<line-break>冒険の旅ができるなんて…。<end>"
EN: "It almost feels like a dream. I can't believe I get to go adventuring with someone I've always admired, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…왠지 꿈만 같아요. 동경하던 <value:$28>님과 함께 모험할 수 있다니….<end>"

## 1920251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、行きましょうか、<line-break><value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we be on our way, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 가 볼까요, <value:$28>님!<end>"

## 1920252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってしまったな。<end>"
EN: "She's gone.<end>"
KO: "가버렸군.<end>"

## 1920253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ、未練でもあるの？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ノエルと<value:$28>を<line-break>くっつけるっていう、あたしの<line-break>計画に賛成だったんじゃないの？<end>"
EN: "What, having second thoughts? I thought you agreed with my plan to get Noel and <value:$28> together.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 미련이라도 있어? 노엘과 <value:$28>을 이어 주겠다는 내 계획에 찬성했던 거 아니야?<end>"

## 1920254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反対ではない。<line-break>それが、ノエルのためだと　<line-break>思うからな。<line-break>お前の方こそ、<line-break>いつまでもノエルと一緒に<line-break>いたかったのではないのか？<end>"
EN: "I wasn't opposed. I believe it's best for Noel. But weren't you the one who wanted to stay with her forever?<end>"
KO: "반대하는 건 아니다. 그게 노엘을 위한 일이라 생각하니까. 오히려 너야말로 언제까지나 노엘과 함께 있고 싶었던 것 아닌가?<end>"

## 1920255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あの子のそばにいると<line-break>目が離せないのは確かだけどね。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あたしだってまだ若いんだから、<line-break>いつまでも子守ばかりしてられないわ。<line-break>ここらが潮時なのよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, it's true you can't take your eyes off that girl for a moment. But I'm still young too. I can't spend forever looking after her. It was time.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그 아이 곁에 있으면 눈을 뗄 수 없는 건 사실이지만. 그래도 나도 아직 젊은데 언제까지나 아이만 돌볼 순 없잖아. 이쯤이 그만둘 때야.<end>"

## 1920256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう？　ひとり者どうし、<line-break>しばらく、ふたりで旅を続けてみる？<end>"
EN: "So, what do you say? We're both unattached. Want to travel together for a while?<end>"
KO: "어때? 둘 다 혼자인데, 당분간 둘이서 여행을 계속해 볼래?<end>"

## 1920257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…遠慮する。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺にも、俺の人生を<line-break>自由に生きる権利がある。<end>"
EN: "No, thank you. I have the right to live my own life as I please.<end>"
KO: "…사양하지. 나도 내 인생을 자유롭게 살 권리가 있어.<end>"

## 1920258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ！　失礼ね！<line-break>あとで、後悔しても知らないわよ！<end>"
EN: "What! How rude! Don't come crying to me when you regret it!<end>"
KO: "뭐야! 실례네! 나중에 후회해도 난 몰라!<end>"

## 1920259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec192-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちなさい！<line-break>ちょっとは振り返ったりしなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! You could at least look back at me!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 조금은 뒤돌아보기도 해!<end>"

## 1930001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。あの子と共に、<line-break>この海の向こうを目指しますか。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you and that one are bound for the far side of this sea.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그 아이와 함께 이 바다 너머를 향하시겠습니까.<end>"

## 1930002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、あの子が、<line-break>自分の旅に他人を誘うなんて、<line-break>本当に珍しい。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It's very rare for that one to invite anyone along on a journey.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그 아이가 자기 여행에 남을 초대하다니 정말 드문 일이네.<end>"

## 1930003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、ひょっとしたら、　　　<line-break>あの子が<value:$28>の旅に<line-break>　<line-break>ついていくということなのかも<line-break>しれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Then again, perhaps it's the other way around, and that one is joining <value:$28> on the journey.<end>"
KO: "아니, 어쩌면 그 아이가 <value:$28>의 여행에 따라간다는 뜻일지도 모르겠군요.<end>"

## 1930004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行こうか、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Shall we go, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1930005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ゼネさんが<line-break>ファーロスの御曹司だったとはねぇ。<line-break>スラムの住人一同びっくりだったよ。<end>"
EN: "Still, who'd have thought Zen was the heir to Pharos? Everyone in the Slums was stunned.<end>"
KO: "그런데 제네 씨가 파로스의 도련님이었다니. 슬럼 주민 모두 깜짝 놀랐어.<end>"

## 1930006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへ、悪かった。<line-break>だますつもりはなかったんだが、<line-break>ま、許してくれや。<end>"
EN: "Heh, sorry about that. I didn't mean to fool you. Forgive me, all right?<end>"
KO: "헤헤, 미안. 속일 생각은 없었는데, 뭐 용서해 줘.<end>"

## 1930007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃ、もちろんですよ。<line-break>ゼネテスさんのおかげで、<line-break>この国は助かったんだから！<end>"
EN: "Of course! This country was saved thanks to Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "그야 물론이죠. 제네테스 씨 덕분에 이 나라가 살아났으니까요!<end>"

## 1930008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の力じゃないさ。<line-break>必死に国を守ろうとした<line-break>みんなのおかげだ。<end>"
EN: "It wasn't my doing. The credit goes to everyone who did all they could to protect this country.<end>"
KO: "내 힘이 아니야. 필사적으로 나라를 지키려 한 모두의 덕분이지.<end>"

## 1930009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったくだよ。感謝しなきゃね。<line-break>貴族だって、ゼネさんみたいなのがいるんだ。<line-break>この国は変わるかもしれないよ。<end>"
EN: "You're right. We owe them our thanks. If there are nobles like Zen, maybe this country really can change.<end>"
KO: "정말 그래. 감사해야겠네. 귀족 중에도 제네 씨 같은 사람이 있어. 이 나라도 변할지 몰라.<end>"

## 1930010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにねー、ゼネテスには、<line-break>さいこーの相棒がいるんだもんね。<end>"
EN: "And Xenetes has the best partner ever!<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이지—, 제네테스에겐 최고의 동료가 있잖아.<end>"

## 1930011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<line-break>ハンナはよくわかってるな。<end>"
EN: "Exactly. Hannah understands.<end>"
KO: "그래. 한나는 잘 알고 있구나.<end>"

## 1930012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "후후후.<end>"

## 1930013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、ゼネさん、<line-break>あんたこんなところにいていいのかい？<end>"
EN: "By the way, Zen, should you really be hanging around here?<end>"
KO: "그런데 제네 씨, 이런 데 있어도 괜찮아?<end>"

## 1930014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　ああ、まあ、なんだ、<line-break>宮廷は退屈なんでね。<line-break>ちっと相棒と遊びにでも行こうかと。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, well... Life at court is dull, so I thought I'd go have a little fun with my partner.<end>"
KO: "응? 아, 뭐랄까, 궁정은 지루해서 말이야. 잠깐 파트너랑 놀러라도 갈까 해서.<end>"

## 1930015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ギルドでいい仕事を　　　　　　　<line-break>見つけたんだわ。<line-break>どうだ、ちょっくら<line-break>やってみないか？<line-break>古代王国の秘宝探しなんだけどよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I found a good job at the Guild. How about giving it a shot? It's a hunt for an Ancient Kingdom treasure.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 길드에서 괜찮은 일을 찾았어. 어때, 한번 해 보지 않을래? 고대 왕국의 비보 찾기야.<end>"

## 1930016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、決まりだ！<line-break>行こうぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Good, it's settled! Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 결정이다! 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1930017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスも<value:$28>も<line-break>結局、冒険好きなのね。<line-break>子供ねー。<end>"
EN: "So Xenetes and <value:$28> both love a good adventure after all. You're such children.<end>"
KO: "제네테스도 <value:$28>도 결국 모험을 좋아하는구나. 어린애네~.<end>"

## 1930018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺を捜しにきたのか、　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "…Did you come looking for me, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…나를 찾으러 온 건가, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1930019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつまでも姉離れできない<line-break>お前の弟と一緒にするな。<end>"
EN: "Don't compare me to that younger brother of yours who still can't let go of his sister.<end>"
KO: "언제까지나 누나에게서 독립 못 하는 네 동생과 나를 같게 보지 마.<end>"

## 1930020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は姉よりも<line-break>もっともっと大きなものを　<line-break>探し求める。<line-break>それが何かはわからない。<line-break>だが、お前が一緒なら<line-break>必ず、そこにたどりつける。<end>"
EN: "I'll seek something far, far greater than my sister. I don't know what that might be. But with you beside me, I know I'll find my way to it.<end>"
KO: "나는 누나보다도 훨씬 더 큰 무언가를 찾을 거야. 그게 뭔지는 몰라. 하지만 네가 함께라면 반드시 거기에 도달할 수 있어.<end>"

## 1930021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう、お前さえ、<line-break>一緒にいれば…。<end>"
EN: "…Yes. As long as you're with me…<end>"
KO: "…그래, 너만 함께 있어 준다면….<end>"

## 1930022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それとも、お前こそ、<line-break>ロイがいなければ<line-break>まだ何もできないのか？<line-break>ロイに会いたいなら、<line-break>俺が会わせてやるぞ。<end>"
EN: "…Or are you the one who still can't do anything without Roy? If you want to see him, I'll take you to him.<end>"
KO: "…아니면 너야말로 로이가 없으면 아직 아무것도 못 하는 건가? 로이를 만나고 싶다면 내가 만나게 해 주지.<end>"

## 1930023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Then let's go.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면, 간다.<end>"

## 1930024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘却の仮面はここにおいていけ。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>いわば、ささやかな祝いの品だ。<line-break>…それに、このくらいの面倒は<line-break>かけてもかまうまい。<end>"
EN: "Leave the Mask of Oblivion here. Consider it a modest gift to mark the occasion. Besides, I think I can allow myself to put you to this much trouble.<end>"
KO: "망각의 가면은 여기에 두고 가라. 말하자면 소소한 축하 선물이다. …이 정도 폐는 끼쳐도 괜찮겠지.<end>"

## 1930025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "…Farewell.<end>"
KO: "…잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1930026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前も<line-break>さようならを言っておけ。<end>"
EN: "…You should say goodbye too.<end>"
KO: "…너도 작별 인사를 해 둬.<end>"

## 1930027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってしまいましたね。<end>"
EN: "He's gone, then.<end>"
KO: "가버렸네요.<end>"

## 1930028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "月光を持つセラなら、<line-break>私たちがここにいることは<line-break>知っていただろうにな。<end>"
EN: "As the bearer of moonlight, Sera must have known we were here.<end>"
KO: "월광을 지닌 세라라면 우리가 여기 있는 걸 알고 있었을 텐데.<end>"

## 1930029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ…、<line-break>あなたの祝福は<line-break>確かに受け取ったわ。<end>"
EN: "Sera… I have received your blessing.<end>"
KO: "세라… 당신의 축복은 확실히 받았어.<end>"

## 1930030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちはふたりで<line-break>このミイスを復興させ　　　<line-break>神器を守ろう。<line-break>お前たちは自由に旅をし、<line-break>お前たちの探し求めるものを<line-break>手に入れるがいい。<end>"
EN: "The two of us will rebuild Miis and guard the sacred artifact. You are free to travel as you please and obtain whatever it is you seek.<end>"
KO: "우리 둘이서 이 미이스를 부흥시키고 신기를 지키자. 너희는 자유롭게 여행하며 너희가 찾아 헤매던 것을 손에 넣으면 돼.<end>"

## 1930031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I'm certain you can find it. Sera, <value:$28>…<end>"

## 1930032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですか、お父さま？<end>"
EN: "What is it, Father?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이세요, 아버님?<end>"

## 1930033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、話しておきたいことがあってな。<end>"
EN: "There's something I want to tell you.<end>"
KO: "아니, 미리 해두고 싶은 말이 있어서.<end>"

## 1930034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険に行かせろという<line-break>お話でしたらダメですよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>アキュリュース自衛隊長なんですし、<line-break>それに、もうお年なんですから。<end>"
EN: "If you're going to ask me to let you go adventuring, the answer is no. You're the commander of the Aqyurius Self-Defense Force, and you're not getting any younger.<end>"
KO: "모험을 보내 달라는 이야기라면 안 됩니다. 아큐류스 자위대장이시고, 게다가 이제 연세도 있으시잖아요.<end>"

## 1930035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっておる。<line-break>まあ、わしの話を聞いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I know. Just hear me out.<end>"
KO: "알고 있다. 뭐, 내 이야기를 들어 보게.<end>"

## 1930036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お前も年頃じゃ。<line-break>信頼できる若者を<line-break>そばに置いておきたくてな。<end>お前も水の巫女となり、<line-break>これからいろいろと<line-break>気苦労もあるじゃろう。<line-break>じゃから、お前が、<line-break>一生つきあえる友人が、<line-break>そばにいればと思ってな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0You're of age now. I wanted you to have a trustworthy young person at your side.<end>Now that you've become the Priestess of Water, you'll have many burdens to bear. I thought it would be good for you to have a lifelong friend at your side.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0너도 이제 혼기가 찼으니 말이다. 믿을 만한 젊은이를 곁에 두고 싶어서 말이지.<end>너도 물의 무녀가 되었으니 앞으로 여러 가지 마음고생이 있겠지. 그래서 네가 평생 함께할 수 있는 친구가 곁에 있으면 해서 말이다.<end>"

## 1930037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>娘を頼んだぞ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, take care of my daughter.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 딸을 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1930038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0お、お父さま…！<line-break>と、突然、<line-break>何を言い出すんですか！<end>お父さま……。<line-break>突然、どうしたのですか？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0F-Father! Wh-what are you saying all of a sudden?!<end>Father... Why are you saying this all of a sudden?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0아, 아버님…! 가, 갑자기, 무슨 말씀을 하시는 겁니까!<end>아버님……. 갑자기 왜 그러십니까?<end>"

## 1930039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この俺が、姉を捜すため、<line-break>何も言わずにお前のそばを<line-break>去るとでも？　フッ、バカな。<end>"
EN: "You think I'd leave your side without a word just to search for my sister? Hmph. Don't be absurd.<end>"
KO: "내가 누나를 찾으려고 아무 말도 없이 네 곁을 떠날 것 같나? 흥, 바보 같은 소리.<end>"

## 1930040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>娘を頼んだぞ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, take care of my daughter.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 딸을 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1930041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父さま……。<line-break>突然、どうしたのですか？<end>"
EN: "Father... Why are you saying this all of a sudden?<end>"
KO: "아버님……. 갑자기 무슨 일이세요?<end>"

## 1930042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしも、もう長くなかろう。<line-break>ずいぶんと生きてきた。<line-break>いつ死が訪れてもおかしくない。　<line-break>我が人生には満足しておる。<line-break>だが、イークレムンよ、お前には、<line-break>何か残しておいておきたくての。<end>"
EN: "I doubt I have much time left. I've lived a long life, and death could come for me at any moment. I am content with the life I've led. But Iiklmn, I want to leave you something before I go.<end>"
KO: "나도 이제 오래 남진 않았겠지. 꽤 오래 살았어. 언제 죽음이 와도 이상하지 않아. 내 인생에는 만족한다. 하지만 이크레문, 너에게는 뭔가 남겨 주고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1930043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父さま…、そんな…。<line-break>私は、何もいりませぬ。<line-break>お父さまさえいてくだされば。<end>"
EN: "Father... don't say that. I don't need anything. All I want is for you to be here.<end>"
KO: "아버님…, 그런…. 저는 아무것도 필요 없습니다. 아버님만 계셔 주신다면.<end>"

## 1930044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が娘よ。悲しむでない。<line-break>死とは、誰にでも訪れるのものじゃ。<line-break>これは定めなのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "My daughter, do not grieve. Death comes for us all. Such is our fate.<end>"
KO: "내 딸아. 슬퍼하지 마라. 죽음은 누구에게나 찾아오는 것이다. 이것이 정해진 운명인 게야.<end>"

## 1930045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お父さま…、そんな…。<end>"
EN: "F-Father... no...<end>"
KO: "아, 아버님… 그럴 수가….<end>"

## 1930046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "というわけで、わしは旅に出る。<line-break>死ぬ前に、お世話になったみんなに<line-break>あいさつしておかねばの。<line-break>では、<value:$28>。<line-break>娘はよろしく頼んだぞ。<end>"
EN: "And so, I am setting out on a journey. Before I die, I must bid farewell to everyone who has helped me over the years. Well then, <value:$28>. Take good care of my daughter.<end>"
KO: "그래서 나는 여행을 떠나려 한다. 죽기 전에 신세 진 모두에게 인사해야지. 그럼, <value:$28>. 딸아이를 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1930047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、え？　え？<end>"
EN: "Ah... what? What?<end>"
KO: "아…, 어? 어?<end>"

## 1930048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー！　あんなこと言って、　<line-break>冒険にでるつもりです！<line-break>　<line-break>きっとあいさつする相手には、<line-break>モンスターとか宝物とかも<line-break>入ってるんだわ！<end>"
EN: "Aha! He said all that just so he could go adventuring! I bet the ones he plans to bid farewell to include monsters and treasure!<end>"
KO: "아! 저런 말을 하고 모험을 떠날 생각이에요! 분명 인사할 상대에는 몬스터나 보물도 들어 있는 거야!<end>"

## 1930049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　なんて父親なのかしら！<end>"
EN: "Honestly! What kind of father does that?<end>"
KO: "정말! 무슨 아버지가 이래!<end>"

## 1930050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>心配なのでついていって<line-break>あげてください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'm worried about him. Please go with him.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 걱정되니 따라가 주세요.<end>"

## 1930051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帰ってきたね…。<line-break>父さんの畑に…。<end>"

## 1930052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Sis... You're going to carry on with your adventures, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "누나…. 누나는 모험을 계속할 거지.<end>"

## 1930053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やっぱり、そうだよな。<line-break>姉ちゃんには<line-break>このノーブルはせますぎるもんな。<end>"
EN: "Yeah... I thought so. Noble's too small for you, Sis.<end>"
KO: "…역시 그렇지. 누나한텐 이 노블은 너무 좁으니까.<end>"

## 1930054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいかげん、姉ちゃんも　　　　　　<line-break>俺離れしないとな。<line-break>　<line-break>俺みたいないい男がすぐそばにいるから<line-break>姉ちゃん、えり好みしちゃって<line-break>まーだ、恋人もいねえんだよ。<end>"
EN: "It's about time you stopped being so attached to me, Sis. Having a catch like me around all the time has made you too picky. That's why you still haven't found anyone.<end>"
KO: "슬슬 누나도 나한테서 독립해야지. 나 같은 좋은 남자가 바로 곁에 있으니까 누나가 눈이 높아져서 아직 애인도 없는 거야.<end>"

## 1930055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いってーなー。<line-break>すぐ手が出るんだから！<line-break>もう！<end>"
EN: "Ow! You're always so quick to hit me! Come on!<end>"
KO: "아프잖아. 바로 손부터 나가네! 정말!<end>"

## 1930056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いってこいよ。<line-break>俺は、ここに残る。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ノーブルと父さんの畑と<line-break>そして、姉ちゃんが帰ってくる場所を<line-break>守ってる。<end>"
EN: "Go on, then. I'll stay here. I'll watch over Noble, Dad's field, and the place you'll come home to.<end>"
KO: "…다녀와. 난 여기 남을게. 노블과 아버지의 밭, 그리고 누나가 돌아올 곳을 지키고 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1930057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ…。<end>"
EN: "Hey...<end>"
KO: "있잖아….<end>"

## 1930058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>どうしたの、ヴァン？<end>"
EN: "Hm? What's wrong, Van?<end>"
KO: "응? 왜 그래, 반?<end>"

## 1930059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや…、<line-break>けっこう、いいもんだよな。　　<line-break>テラネの町って…。<line-break>ちょっと…かなり地味だけどよ、<line-break>なんていうか、こう、みんな<line-break>生きてるな、って感じがしてさ。<end>"
EN: "Nah... I was just thinking Terane's a pretty nice town, really. It's a little... well, actually, it's pretty plain, but I don't know. You get the sense that people are really living their lives here.<end>"
KO: "아니…, 꽤 괜찮은 곳이네. 테라네라는 마을…. 조금… 꽤 수수하지만, 뭐랄까, 다들 살아 있다는 느낌이 들어.<end>"

## 1930060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね……。<end>"
EN: "Yeah...<end>"
KO: "그렇네…….<end>"

## 1930061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "But still!<end>"
KO: "하지만!<end>"

## 1930062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺やっぱ、もっともっと　　　<line-break>お前らと旅がしたいぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>もっといろんなもの見て<line-break>いろんなもの食って<line-break>いろんな奴と会ってみたいぜ！<end>"
EN: "I really want to keep traveling with you two. A whole lot more. I want to see all kinds of things, eat all kinds of food, and meet all kinds of people!<end>"
KO: "나 역시 너희와 더, 더 오래 여행하고 싶어. 더 많은 걸 보고, 더 많은 걸 먹고, 더 많은 녀석을 만나 보고 싶다고!<end>"

## 1930063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕も、そう思うよ。<end>"
EN: "So do I.<end>"
KO: "나도 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 1930064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん…。この大陸はなんか<line-break>だいたい歩き回った気がするから<line-break>次は別の大陸だな！！<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Feels like we've been just about everywhere on this continent, so another continent's next!<end>"
KO: "음…. 이 대륙은 대충 다 돌아본 것 같으니 다음은 다른 대륙이다!!<end>"

## 1930065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン、別の大陸って？<end>"
EN: "Van, what do you mean, another continent?<end>"
KO: "반, 다른 대륙이라니?<end>"

## 1930066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　<line-break>だから、どっか別の大陸さ。<line-break>あるだろ？　そういうの。<end>"
EN: "Hm? You know, some other continent. There's got to be one, right?<end>"
KO: "응? 그러니까 다른 대륙 어딘가야. 있잖아? 그런 데.<end>"

## 1930067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン…。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>相変わらずだね…。<line-break>　<line-break>あ、でも、そういえば昔<line-break>お爺ちゃんが、西の方にすごく大きな<line-break>大陸があるって言ってたような…。<end>"
EN: "Van... You never change. Oh, but now that I think about it, my grandfather once said something about a huge continent to the west...<end>"

## 1930068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1930069

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、じゃあ行くか！！<end>"

## 1930070

## 1930071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml

## 1930072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "자, 간다! 뜨거운 혼을 불태우러!!<end>"

## 1930073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン、熱い魂を燃やすって？<end>"

## 1930074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1930075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "師匠、よく復帰してくれました。<line-break>でも、売り物も作ってくださいよ。<end>"
EN: "Master, it's good to have you back. But please make something we can sell, too.<end>"
KO: "스승님, 복귀해 주셔서 다행입니다. 하지만 팔 물건도 만들어 주세요.<end>"

## 1930076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "武器はな、強く正しき魂に使われて、<line-break>初めてその真価を発揮する。<line-break>　<line-break>わしは、先の長い旅で、<line-break>その使い手を見つけたのじゃ。<line-break>わしはそいつのためにしかつくらん。<end>"
EN: "A weapon only shows its true worth in the hands of a strong and righteous soul. On my long journey, I found such a wielder. I forge for no one else.<end>"
KO: "무기는 강하고 올바른 영혼이 써야 비로소 진가를 발휘한다. 긴 여행 끝에 그 사용자를 찾았다. 난 그 녀석을 위해서만 만들 거다.<end>"

## 1930077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "師匠、お客なんですが、　　　　　　　　<line-break>どうしますか？<line-break>断ってきましょうか？<line-break><value:$28>という人なんですが…。<end>"
EN: "Master, we have a customer. What should I do? Shall I turn the visitor away? The name is <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "스승님, 손님이 왔는데 어떻게 할까요? 거절할까요? <value:$28>이라는 사람인데….<end>"

## 1930079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "師匠？<end>"
EN: "Master?<end>"
KO: "스승님?<end>"

## 1930080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<line-break>最高の武器を打ってみせよう。<line-break>ふふふ、腕が鳴るわい。<end>"
EN: "All right. I'll forge the finest weapon there is. Heh heh. I'm itching to put my skills to work.<end>"
KO: "알겠다. 최고의 무기를 만들어 보이지. 후후후, 솜씨를 발휘할 때군.<end>"

## 1930081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお！　師匠がやる気に！<end>"
EN: "Oh! Master's fired up!<end>"
KO: "오오! 스승님이 의욕을!<end>"

## 1930082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、ぼさっとするな！<line-break>大仕事だぞ！<line-break>気を引き締めるんじゃぞ！<end>"
EN: "Come on, don't just stand there! This is a big job! Look sharp!<end>"
KO: "자, 멍하니 있지 마라! 큰일이다! 정신 바짝 차려라!<end>"

## 1930083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すばらしい！<line-break>これがレルラ＝ロントンが作った<line-break><value:$28>の詩か。<end>"
EN: "Wonderful! So this is the poem Relra-Lonton wrote about <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "훌륭해! 이게 렐라=론톤이 만든 <value:$28>의 시인가.<end>"

## 1930084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンコール！<line-break>アンコール！<end>"
EN: "Encore! Encore!<end>"
KO: "앙코르! 앙코르!<end>"

## 1930085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんなー。<line-break>ぼくの歌を聴いてくれてありがとう！<line-break>じゃあねー。<end>"
EN: "Thank you all for listening to my song! Bye now!<end>"
KO: "여러분—. 내 노래를 들어줘서 고마워! 그럼 안녕—.<end>"

## 1930086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お疲れさまでした。<end>"
EN: "Well done.<end>"
KO: "수고하셨습니다.<end>"

## 1930087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きみ、もう、<line-break>この歌、おぼえてくれたよね。<end>"
EN: "You've learned the song by now, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "이제 이 노래, 외워 줬지?<end>"

## 1930088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "明日からはきみが<line-break>この歌をみんなに聴かせるんだ。<line-break>ぼくは、また旅に出るよ。<end>"
EN: "Starting tomorrow, you'll be the one singing it for everyone. I'm going back on the road.<end>"
KO: "내일부터는 네가 이 노래를 모두에게 들려줘. 나는 다시 여행을 떠날게.<end>"

## 1930089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなすばらしい歌を作られたのに…。<line-break>また、旅なんかに出るんですか？<line-break>この町に落ち着かれては…。<end>"
EN: "After writing such a wonderful song... must you really go traveling again? Couldn't you settle down in this town...?<end>"
KO: "이렇게 훌륭한 노래를 만드셨는데…. 또 여행을 떠나시는 건가요? 이 마을에 정착하시면….<end>"

## 1930090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えーっ！　きみはぼくに<line-break>この歌だけで満足しろって言うのー？<line-break>それはムリだよぉー。<line-break>ぼくは、ずーっと<line-break>キレイのかけらを探してさまよう<line-break>さすらいの吟遊詩人なんだ。<end>"
EN: "What? You expect me to be satisfied with just this one song? No way! I'm a wandering minstrel, forever roaming in search of fragments of beauty.<end>"
KO: "에~! 너는 나한테 이 노래 하나로 만족하라는 거야~? 그건 무리야~. 나는 계속 아름다움의 조각을 찾아 헤매는 방랑 음유시인이거든.<end>"

## 1930091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくは<line-break><value:$28>との旅で<line-break>たっくさんのキレイに出会った。<end>"
EN: "Traveling with <value:$28>, I found so much beauty.<end>"
KO: "나는 <value:$28>와 여행하면서 정말 많은 아름다움을 만났어.<end>"

## 1930092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくはね、エルファスも<line-break>ゾフォルもシャリも、その生き方は<line-break>みーんな、キレイだったと思う。<end>"
EN: "Elfas, Zofor, and Shalli―I think there was beauty in the way each of them lived.<end>"
KO: "나는 엘파스도 조포르도 샤리도, 그 살아온 방식은 전부 아름다웠다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1930093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれ、みんな歌にするつもりだよ。<line-break>みんなの憎しみが、<line-break>時にいやされたころにね。<line-break>それまで、<line-break>キレイのかけらを求めて<line-break>旅をするつもり。<end>"
EN: "Someday, once time has softened people's hatred, I'll turn each of them into a song. Until then, I'll keep traveling in search of fragments of beauty.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 모두 노래로 만들 생각이야. 모두의 증오가 시간에 치유될 무렵에 말이야. 그때까지 아름다움의 조각을 찾아 여행할 거야.<end>"

## 1930094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくに一番キレイを見せてくれる<line-break><value:$28>と<line-break>一緒にね。<end>"
EN: "Together with <value:$28>, who shows me more beauty than anyone.<end>"
KO: "내게 가장 아름다운 모습을 보여주는 <value:$28>과 함께 말이야.<end>"

## 1930095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか……。<end>"
EN: "You've come...<end>"
KO: "왔는가…….<end>"

## 1930096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決着をつけよう。<end>"
EN: "Let us settle this.<end>"
KO: "결판을 내자.<end>"

## 1930097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか、<line-break>この大陸を出ていくのですか。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you're leaving the continent.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 이 대륙을 떠나시는 겁니까.<end>"

## 1930098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、それがいいかもしれませんね。<line-break>今のあなたの存在は、<line-break>あまりに巨大ですから。<line-break>ロストールとディンガルが<line-break>手を取り合って、バイアシオンは<line-break>ひとつになろうとしています。<end>"
EN: "Well, perhaps that is for the best. You have become far too great a presence. Rostorl and Dyneskal are joining hands, and Vyashion is on the verge of becoming one.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그게 좋을지도 모르겠군요. 지금 당신의 존재는 너무 거대하니까요. 로스톨과 딘갈이 손을 잡고 바이아시온은 하나가 되려 하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1930099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの存在は、<line-break>その流れをゆるがしかねない。<line-break>あなたの意志に関係なく、ね。<end>"
EN: "Your presence could upset that course, whether you intend it or not.<end>"
KO: "당신의 존재는 그 흐름을 뒤흔들 수도 있어. 당신의 의지와 상관없이 말이야.<end>"

## 1930100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は簡単に<line-break>他人を祭りあげたりするからな。<line-break>　<line-break>これからの変革によって、<line-break>今まで持っていた力を<line-break>脅かされた連中が、<end>"
EN: "Humans are quick to put others on a pedestal. The changes ahead will threaten the power some have long held.<end>"
KO: "인간은 쉽게 남을 떠받들곤 하니까. 앞으로의 변혁으로 지금까지 가진 힘을 위협받은 녀석들이,<end>"

## 1930101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の<line-break>名前を利用して反対勢力を作る<line-break>…あり得る話だ。<end>"
EN: "Those people might use <value:$28>'s name to build an opposition movement.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 이름을 이용해 반대 세력을 만든다… 충분히 있을 법한 이야기다.<end>"

## 1930102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこまでいかないにしても、　　　　<line-break><value:$28>を冒険に出る前の<line-break><value:$28>として接してくれる<line-break>人は、もういないかもしれませんね。<line-break>ある意味、それが無限のソウルを<line-break>持ったことの代償かもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Even if things never go that far, there may be no one left who can treat <value:$28> as <value:$28>, the person you were before setting out on your journey. In a sense, that may be the price of gaining the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "그 정도까지는 아니더라도, 이제 <value:$28>을 모험을 떠나기 전의 <value:$28>으로 대해 주는 사람은 없을지도 모르겠네요. 어떤 의미에서는 그것이 무한의 소울을 지닌 대가일지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1930103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、私たちの他にも　<line-break>冒険で知り合った人たちが<line-break>いるでしょうに。<line-break>その人たちには何も告げずに<line-break>ひとりで行くなんて、<line-break>兄さんといい、あなたといい…。<end>"
EN: "Still, you must have met others on your travels besides us. Leaving alone without a word to any of them... First my brother, and now you.<end>"
KO: "그런데 우리 말고도 모험하며 알게 된 사람들이 있잖아요. 그 사람들에게 아무 말도 하지 않고 혼자 가다니, 오빠도 그렇고 당신도 그렇고….<end>"

## 1930104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア、人はいろいろです。<line-break>それが、<value:$28>や<line-break>あの子なりの気の使い方なのですよ。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia, everyone is different. This is simply how <value:$28> and that child choose to show consideration.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아, 사람은 저마다 다릅니다. 그게 <value:$28>이나 그 아이 나름의 배려 방식인 거예요.<end>"

## 1930105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってるわ、そんなこと。　　　<line-break>でも、残される方にも、<line-break>それぞれの思いがある。<line-break><value:$28>なら、<line-break>わかってくれると思ったんだけど。<end>"
EN: "I know that. But those left behind have feelings of their own. I thought <value:$28>, at least, would understand.<end>"
KO: "그런 건 알아. 하지만 남겨지는 쪽에도 저마다의 마음이 있어. <value:$28>이라면 알아줄 거라 생각했는데.<end>"

## 1930106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その辺にしておきなさい。<line-break><value:$28>だって<line-break>考えた上での選択でしょう。<end>"
EN: "That will do. I'm sure <value:$28> thought carefully before making this choice.<end>"
KO: "그쯤 해 두세요. <value:$28>도 생각한 끝에 내린 선택이겠죠.<end>"

## 1930107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、そろそろ<line-break>あなたの望むところへ<line-break>お連れしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Then it is time I took you where you wish to go.<end>"
KO: "그럼 슬슬 당신이 원하는 곳으로 데려다드리죠.<end>"

## 1930108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、どうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Go on.<end>"
KO: "자, 여기요.<end>"

## 1930109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけて、<value:$28>。<line-break>また、会える日まで。<end>"
EN: "Take care, <value:$28>. Until we meet again.<end>"
KO: "조심해, <value:$28>. 다시 만날 날까지.<end>"

## 1930111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、もうひとりの連れの方が<line-break>来たみたいですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it looks like your other companion is here.<end>"
KO: "어라, 다른 일행도 온 모양이군요.<end>"

## 1930112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりね、<value:$28>。<line-break>どう？　しばらく会ってなかったけど<line-break>腕は上がった？<end>"
EN: "It's been a while, <value:$28>. So, have you gotten any better since I last saw you?<end>"
KO: "오랜만이네, <value:$28>. 어때? 한동안 못 봤는데 실력은 늘었어?<end>"

## 1930113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、期待してるわ。<line-break>これからはまた同じパーティーの<line-break>一員だもの。よろしくね。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm counting on you. We're in the same party again, after all. It'll be good working with you.<end>"
KO: "후후, 기대할게. 이제 다시 같은 파티의 일원이니까. 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1930114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、あなたがネメア様の足を<line-break>引っ張るなんてことは<line-break>ないでしょうけどね。<line-break>正直言って、私がふたりの邪魔に<line-break>ならないようにする方が<line-break>大変かもしれないし。<end>"
EN: "Not that I expect you to hold Lord Nemea back. Honestly, I may have enough trouble just keeping out of the way of the two of you.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 당신이 네메아 님의 발목을 잡을 일은 없겠지만요. 솔직히 말하면 제가 두 사람을 방해하지 않게 하는 쪽이 더 힘들지도 모르고.<end>"

## 1930115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が守ってくれるの？<line-break>…それは仲間として？<line-break><line-break>それとも…<end>"
EN: "You'll protect me, <value:$28>? As a comrade? Or...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 지켜 줄 거야? …그건 동료로서? 아니면…<end>"

## 1930116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんてね。冗談よ。<line-break>おくれをとらないように、<line-break>精一杯ついていくわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm only teasing. I'll do my best to keep up.<end>"
KO: "…라고 해도 농담이야. 뒤처지지 않도록 죽을힘을 다해 따라갈게.<end>"

## 1930117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が守ってくれるの？<line-break><line-break><line-break>…フフ、期待してるわ。<line-break>私もこの弓で、精一杯<line-break>バックアップさせてもらうから。<end>"
EN: "You'll protect me, <value:$28>? Heh, I'm counting on you. I'll do everything I can to support you with this bow.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 지켜 줄 거야? …후후, 기대할게. 나도 이 활로 힘껏 지원할 테니까.<end>"

## 1930118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待たせたな、ふたりとも。<end>"
EN: "Sorry to keep you both waiting.<end>"
KO: "기다렸지, 둘 다.<end>"

## 1930119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行こうか、オイフェ、<line-break>そして、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Shall we go, Aoife―and <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "가자, 오이페. 그리고 <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1930120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec193-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３人とも、気をつけて。<line-break>よい旅を。<end>"
EN: "Take care, all three of you. Safe travels.<end>"
KO: "셋 다 조심해. 좋은 여행 되길.<end>"
